    AnchorAI brief intro

    AnchorAI 不和 LLM 比聪明。LLM 越准,越证明我们的验证层有效(确认好的、抓造假的);而当最准的 LLM 也弃答时,只有 AnchorAI 用当前核验证据接地。我们卖的是让任何 LLM 在受监管医疗里可部署 —— 护城河是 KB,对 LLM 进步反脆弱。AnchorAI does not compete with LLMs on intelligence. The better the LLM, the more it proves our verification layer works — confirming the good, catching the fabricated; and when even the best LLM abstains, only AnchorAI grounds the answer in current, verified evidence. What we sell is making any LLM deployable in regulated medicine — the moat is the KB, anti-fragile to LLM progress.

    Anchor AI 并非又一款医疗聊天机器人,而是面向可信医疗 AI 输出的证据校准层。当前主流医疗 AI 多聚焦于生成流畅回答、检索文献、归纳指南或对接临床工作流;Anchor AI 关注的,是一个更为根本的问题:AI 给出的医疗答案,是否真正得到现有最佳证据的支持?Anchor AI is not another medical chatbot. It is an evidence calibration layer for trustworthy medical AI outputs. While most medical AI tools focus on generating fluent answers, retrieving papers, summarizing guidelines, or integrating with clinical workflows, Anchor AI focuses on a more fundamental question: is the AI-generated medical answer actually justified by the best available evidence?

    Anchor AI 将医学证据转化为结构化、可计算的「证据对象」,涵盖临床问题、效应量、证据确定性、安全性信息、患者重要结局与来源溯源。在此基础上,它将 LLM 生成的论断与证据层进行比对,识别具有临床意义的偏差,并输出可审计追溯的矫正结果。Anchor AI converts medical evidence into structured, computable evidence objects, including the clinical question, effect size, certainty of evidence, safety information, patient-important outcomes, and source trace. It then compares LLM-generated claims against this evidence layer, detects clinically important mismatches, and produces an auditable corrected output.

    其核心创新在于:Anchor AI 并非让医疗 AI「听起来更专业」,而是让医疗 AI 在证据对齐、确定性校准、可溯源与临床可辩护性上真正提升。简言之,Anchor AI 让医疗 AI 从「流畅而貌似合理」转为「证据校准且可审计」。The key innovation is that Anchor AI does not simply make medical AI sound better; it makes medical AI more evidence-aligned, certainty-calibrated, traceable, and clinically defensible. In short, Anchor AI turns medical AI from &ldquo;fluent and plausible&rdquo; into &ldquo;evidence-calibrated and auditable.&rdquo;

    🎯 谁需要 Anchor AI🎯 Who needs Anchor AI

    Anchor AI 面向高风险决策场景下依赖医疗 AI 输出的用户群体 — 这些用户要求 AI 输出做到证据对齐、确定性校准、可审计追溯 — 包括但不限于:制药企业、卫生技术评估(HTA)机构、医保支付方、医院、临床医生、医学教育者、临床指南制定者,以及循证医学综合团队。Anchor AI is designed for high-stakes users who rely on medical AI outputs and need them to be evidence-aligned, certainty-calibrated, and auditable — including, but not limited to, pharmaceutical companies, HTA agencies, payers, hospitals, clinicians, medical educators, guideline developers, and evidence-synthesis teams.

    目前 nerandomilast 治疗特发性肺纤维化（IPF）的证据如何，含任何 3 期试验结果？与 pirfenidone、nintedanib 在减缓 FVC 下降和死亡方面相比如何？What is the current evidence for nerandomilast in idiopathic pulmonary fibrosis, including any phase 3 trial results, and how does it compare with pirfenidone and nintedanib for slowing FVC decline and for mortality?
    ▶ 展开 system prompt v1.5▶ Show system prompt v1.5
    You are an experienced clinician. Answer the medical question concisely based on your clinical knowledge. Mandatory requirements (all 4): 1. Effect sizes — Cite specific drugs, doses, and effect sizes (RR / OR / HR with 95% CI; or % with 95% CI for vaccine efficacy) when possible. State the trial / SR name where the number comes from. 2. References (BMJ / Vancouver style — REQUIRED) — Number every key claim in-text with [1], [2], [3] etc. in citation order. End your answer with a 'References' list using Vancouver style: Author AA, Author BB, Author CC, et al. Title. Journal. Year;Volume(Issue):Pages. doi:XXX (or PMID:XXX). Example: Baas P, Scherpereel A, Nowak AK, et al. First-line nivolumab plus ipilimumab in unresectable malignant pleural mesothelioma (CheckMate 743): a multicentre, randomised, open-label, phase 3 trial. Lancet. 2021;397(10272):375-386. doi:10.1016/S0140-6736(20)32714-8 3. Confidence self-rating (MANDATORY at the very end) — State your OVERALL confidence using exactly one of these 4 GRADE-style labels: High / Moderate / Low / Very Low, followed by a one-sentence justification. Example: 'Overall confidence: Moderate — based on a single landmark phase 3 RCT (CheckMate 743) with consistent 5-year follow-up; epithelioid subgroup remains uncertain.' 4. Length — Maximum 350 words for the main answer (references list is in addition, not counted). Do not consult any external knowledge base — answer from your parametric knowledge only. If you don't know a precise PMID or DOI, OMIT it rather than fabricate; cite only what you are sure of.
    🔴
    关键校正Key corrections：OpenEvidence 开头判 nerandomilast「禁用、绝不可用」(检索污染、自相矛盾);DeepSeek 误以为其 3 期试验仍在进行(实则 2025 已读出阳性),并把 2 条对照药引用wrong-paper(缺氧预后、FFR 心血管论文)。ChatGPT、Claude 答得准、引用也对——Anchor 核验确认。OpenEvidence opens by calling nerandomilast 'contraindicated / should never be used' (retrieval contamination, self-contradictory); DeepSeek thinks its phase-3 trial is still running (it read out positive in 2025) and mis-attributes two comparator-drug citations (a hypoxia-prognosis paper, an FFR cardiovascular paper). ChatGPT and Claude are accurate and correctly cited — Anchor verifies and confirms.
    🤖 选 LLM:看它的原话 + Anchor 校正 + 审计(每个 LLM 同一锁定 prompt)Pick an LLM: its verbatim + Anchor correction + audit (same locked prompt to each)
    Ⓐ未矫正 · 原话Uncorrected · DeepSeek v4 Pro
    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    **问题：** nerandomilast 在特发性肺纤维化中的证据如何？在减缓 FVC 下降和降低死亡率方面，它与 pirfenidone 和 nintedanib 相比如何？

    ---

    nerandomilast (BI 1015550) 是一种口服选择性 PDE4B 抑制剂。其关键的 2 期临床试验（N=147 例 IPF 患者，约 60% 同时使用 pirfenidone 或 nintedanib 作为背景治疗）评估了 12 周 FVC 的变化。
    - **FVC 下降（12 周）：** nerandomilast 9 mg BID 组的 FVC 最小二乘均值变化为 –5.6 mL，而安慰剂组为 –79.7 mL（经安慰剂调整后的差异为 74.1 mL, 95% CI 27.4–120.8; P=0.002）[关键试验：Richeldi L et al., NEJM 2022; PMID 35569036]。18 mg BID 剂量显示出类似的差异，为 81.3 mL (95% CI 33.7–129.0)。**GRADE：中等** —— 试验执行良好，但持续时间短，且为 2 期研究并采用替代终点（12 周 FVC 变化）。
    - **死亡率：** 该试验在统计学上不足以评估死亡率；仅发生 2 例死亡（1 例安慰剂组，1 例 nerandomilast 组）—— **极低确定性**。
    - **不良反应：** 腹泻（9 mg 组 17%，安慰剂组 8%）、恶心、头痛较为常见；严重不良事件发生率在 9 mg 组为 13%，安慰剂组为 17%。其耐受性特征类似于 nintedanib 的胃肠道反应；长期安全性尚不明确。

    **与 pirfenidone 和 nintedanib 的比较**（目前不存在 nerandomilast 的头对头试验）：
    - **Pirfenidone：** 在为期 52 周的 ASCEND 试验中，pirfenidone 将 FVC 下降减少了 193 mL (95% CI 83–303；均值变化 –235 mL vs 安慰剂组 –428 mL；FVC 的 GRADE 为 **高**) [King TE et al., NEJM 2014; PMID 24836312]。在汇总分析中，全因死亡率 HR 为 0.55 (95% CI 0.31–0.97) (GRADE 为 **中等**；事件数较少) [Noble PW et al., Eur Respir J 2016; PMID 26886654]。
    - **Nintedanib：** 在 INPULSIS 试验中，nintedanib 将年 FVC 下降速度减缓了 125.2 mL/year (95% CI 77.7–172.8; GRADE 为 **高**) [Richeldi L et al., NEJM 2014; PMID 24836310]。死亡率的降低在单项研究中不具有显著性 (HR 0.70, 95% CI 0.47–1.03; 根据荟萃分析 GRADE 为 **中等** [Rochwerg B et al., Ann Intern Med 2017; PMID 28456699])。
    - **间接比较：** nerandomilast 的 12 周 FVC 获益（约 75 mL）似乎是对背景抗纤维化药物的叠加效应（交互作用 P=0.45），但其对长期下降和生存率的影响尚未得到证实。pirfenidone 和 nintedanib 已通过长期数据确认了 52 周 FVC 的获益以及边缘性的死亡率降低；nerandomilast 仍处于研究阶段，3 期 FIBRONEER-IPF 试验正在进行中。

    **总体信心：低** —— nerandomilast 缺乏关于持续 FVC 下降和死亡率的 3 期数据；且不存在直接比较研究。    Nerandomilast (BI 1015550) is an oral preferential PDE4B inhibitor. Its pivotal phase 2 trial (N=147 IPF patients, ~60% on background pirfenidone or nintedanib) evaluated 12‑week FVC change.
    - **FVC decline (12 weeks):** Least‑squares mean change in FVC was –5.6 mL with nerandomilast 9 mg BID and –79.7 mL with placebo (placebo‑adjusted difference 74.1 mL, 95% CI 27.4–120.8; P=0.002) [pivotal trial: Richeldi L et al., NEJM 2022; PMID 35569036]. The 18 mg BID dose showed a similar difference of 81.3 mL (95% CI 33.7–129.0). **GRADE: moderate** – well‑performed trial but short duration, phase 2, and surrogate endpoint (12‑week FVC change).
    - **Mortality:** The trial was not powered for mortality; only 2 deaths occurred (1 placebo, 1 nerandomilast) – **very low certainty**.
    - **Harms:** Diarrhoea (17% with 9 mg, 8% placebo), nausea, headache were more common; serious adverse events occurred in 13% (9 mg) vs 17% (placebo). The tolerability profile resembles nintedanib’s GI effects; long‑term safety is unknown.

    **Comparison with pirfenidone and nintedanib** (no head‑to‑head trials with nerandomilast exist):
    - **Pirfenidone:** In the 52‑week ASCEND trial, pirfenidone reduced FVC decline by 193 mL (95% CI 83–303; mean change –235 mL vs –428 mL with placebo; GRADE **high** for FVC) [King TE et al., NEJM 2014; PMID 24836312]. All‑cause mortality HR 0.55 (95% CI 0.31–0.97) in pooled analysis (GRADE **moderate**; few events) [Noble PW et al., Eur Respir J 2016; PMID 26886654].
    - **Nintedanib:** In the INPULSIS trials, nintedanib slowed annual FVC decline by 125.2 mL/year (95% CI 77.7–172.8; GRADE **high**) [Richeldi L et al., NEJM 2014; PMID 24836310]. Mortality reduction was not individually significant (HR 0.70, 95% CI 0.47–1.03; GRADE **moderate** per meta‑analysis [Rochwerg B et al., Ann Intern Med 2017; PMID 28456699]).
    - **Indirect comparison:** Nerandomilast’s 12‑week FVC benefit (~75 mL) appears additive to background antifibrotics (interaction P=0.45), but the effect on longer‑term decline and survival is unproven. Pirfenidone and nintedanib have confirmed 52‑week FVC benefits and a borderline mortality reduction with longer‑term data; nerandomilast is investigational, with phase 3 FIBRONEER‑IPF ongoing.

    **Overall confidence: low** — nerandomilast lacks phase 3 data on sustained FVC decline and mortality; no direct comparisons exist.
    ⒷAnchor 校正版 · KB 锚定Anchor-corrected · KB-grounded修订Track净版Clean
    奈仑米司他(nerandomilast)是一种口服优先性 PDE4B 抑制剂。其关键 2 期试验(N=147 例 IPF 患者,约 60% 接受背景吡非尼酮或尼达尼布)评估了 12 周 FVC 变化:最小二乘均值变化 nerandomilast 9 mg BID 组为 –5.6 mL,安慰剂组为 –79.7 mL(安慰剂校正差 74.1 mL,95% CI 27.4–120.8;P=0.002)[1]。18 mg BID 剂量组差异相似(81.3 mL,95% CI 33.7–129.0)。FIBRONEER-IPF 3 期试验(2025 年读出,N=1177)证实 52 周 FVC 改善 MD +68.8 mL(95% CI 30.3–107.4)[2];该效应达到 Maher 2024 MID ≥50 mL/年,但未达到 du Bois 2011 ~80 mL 或试验惯例 100 mL 阈值,因此临床意义存在争议。FVC 是 FDA 验证的替代终点(validated surrogate),并非患者重要结局(POI)。全因死亡率方向有利但未达统计学显著(HR 0.66,95% CI 0.41–1.08;样本量不足)[2];复合 PFS(加重/住院/死亡)未达到预设显著性阈值[2]。LLM 2 期数据 → Anchor 加入 3 期 FIBRONEER-IPF + NMAYin 2026 网络 meta 分析(NMA,n=16 525)将 nerandomilast 18 mg 列为 FVC 改善最优药物之一[3];Mendoza 2025 NMA(n=15 813)结果一致[4]。与吡非尼酮和尼达尼布的比较(（无头对头试验）):ASCEND 试验中吡非尼酮 52 周减少 FVC 下降 193 mL(95% CI 83–303)[5];INPULSIS 试验中尼达尼布减缓年 FVC 下降 125.2 mL/年(95% CI 77.7–172.8)[6]。Petnak 2021 meta 分析(n=12 956)汇总抗纤维化药全因死亡率 RR ≈ 0.55(95% CI 0.39–0.78)[7];Loveman NMA(n=10 326)针对尼达尼布死亡终点[8]。ATS/ERS/JRS/ALAT 2022 指南对吡非尼酮和尼达尼布均给予强推荐(GRADE 评级)[9]。间接比较提示 nerandomilast 的 FVC 获益可能叠加于背景抗纤维化药,但长期生存获益尚未证实

    安全性方面:2 期试验中腹泻(9 mg 组 17% vs 安慰剂 8%)、恶心、头痛更常见;严重不良事件 9 mg 组 13% vs 安慰剂 17%[1]。FIBRONEER-IPF 3 期试验中,≥2 级腹泻发生率 nerandomilast 组 36.6% vs 安慰剂 24.7%(NNH ≈ 9)[2];严重不良事件发生率与安慰剂相似(RR ~1.0);治疗相关死亡无显著增加[2]。停药率与安慰剂相当[2]。总体耐受性特征与尼达尼布的胃肠道效应相似;长期安全性数据仍有限。

    总结:奈仑米司他(nerandomilast)是 IPF 领域的新兴治疗选择,3 期 FIBRONEER-IPF 试验证实其 52 周 FVC 改善具有统计学意义且达到部分 MID 阈值,但主要 POI 复合终点未达标,全因死亡率未显著降低。NMA 将其 FVC 改善列为最优之一[3][4],但缺乏与吡非尼酮/尼达尼布的头对头比较。当前证据边界:nerandomilast 的长期生存获益和安全性尚待进一步随访数据;临床决策应结合患者偏好和现有抗纤维化药的成熟数据。    Nerandomilast (BI 1015550) is an oral preferential PDE4B inhibitor. Its pivotal phase 2 trial (N=147 IPF patients, ~60% on background pirfenidone or nintedanib) evaluated 12‑week FVC change: least‑squares mean change was –5.6 mL with nerandomilast 9 mg BID and –79.7 mL with placebo (placebo‑adjusted difference 74.1 mL, 95% CI 27.4–120.8; P=0.002) [1]. The 18 mg BID dose showed a similar difference of 81.3 mL (95% CI 33.7–129.0). The FIBRONEER-IPF phase 3 trial (read out 2025, N=1177) confirmed a 52‑week FVC improvement MD +68.8 mL (95% CI 30.3–107.4) [2]; this effect meets the Maher 2024 MID ≥50 mL/year but fails the du Bois 2011 ~80 mL or trial convention 100 mL thresholds, making clinical significance debated. FVC is an FDA‑validated surrogate, not a patient‑important outcome (POI).All‑cause mortality was directionally favourable but not statistically significant (HR 0.66, 95% CI 0.41–1.08; underpowered) [2]; the composite PFS (exacerbation/hospitalisation/death) did not meet its pre‑specified significance threshold [2].LLM phase‑2 data → Anchor adds phase‑3 FIBRONEER‑IPF + NMAsThe Yin 2026 network meta‑analysis (NMA, n=16 525) ranks nerandomilast 18 mg among the best for FVC improvement [3]; the Mendoza 2025 NMA (n=15 813) is consistent [4].Comparison with pirfenidone and nintedanib (no head‑to‑head trials exist): in the ASCEND trial, pirfenidone reduced FVC decline by 193 mL (95% CI 83–303) over 52 weeks [5]; in the INPULSIS trials, nintedanib slowed annual FVC decline by 125.2 mL/year (95% CI 77.7–172.8) [6]. The Petnak 2021 meta‑analysis (n=12 956) pooled antifibrotic all‑cause mortality RR ≈ 0.55 (95% CI 0.39–0.78) [7]; the Loveman NMA (n=10 326) addresses nintedanib mortality [8]. The ATS/ERS/JRS/ALAT 2022 guideline strongly recommends both pirfenidone and nintedanib (GRADE‑rated) [9].Indirect comparison suggests nerandomilast&#x27;s FVC benefit may be additive to background antifibrotics, but long‑term survival benefit is unproven

    Safety: in the phase 2 trial, diarrhoea (17% with 9 mg vs 8% placebo), nausea, and headache were more common; serious adverse events occurred in 13% (9 mg) vs 17% (placebo) [1]. In FIBRONEER‑IPF, ≥grade 2 diarrhoea occurred in 36.6% with nerandomilast vs 24.7% with placebo (NNH ≈ 9) [2]; serious AE rates were similar to placebo (RR ~1.0); no significant increase in treatment‑related deaths [2]. Discontinuation was similar to placebo [2].The tolerability profile resembles nintedanib's GI effects; long‑term safety data remain limited.

    Summary: Nerandomilast is an emerging treatment option for IPF. The phase 3 FIBRONEER‑IPF trial demonstrated a statistically significant 52‑week FVC improvement meeting some MID thresholds, but the primary POI composite endpoint was not met and all‑cause mortality was not significantly reduced. NMAs rank its FVC improvement among the best [3][4], but head‑to‑head comparisons with pirfenidone/nintedanib are lacking.Evidence boundary: long‑term survival benefit and safety of nerandomilast await further follow‑up; clinical decisions should incorporate patient preferences and the mature data of existing antifibrotics.
    奈仑米司他(nerandomilast)是一种口服优先性 PDE4B 抑制剂。其关键 2 期试验(N=147 例 IPF 患者,约 60% 接受背景吡非尼酮或尼达尼布)评估了 12 周 FVC 变化:最小二乘均值变化 nerandomilast 9 mg BID 组为 –5.6 mL,安慰剂组为 –79.7 mL(安慰剂校正差 74.1 mL,95% CI 27.4–120.8;P=0.002)[1]。18 mg BID 剂量组差异相似(81.3 mL,95% CI 33.7–129.0)。FIBRONEER-IPF 3 期试验(2025 年读出,N=1177)证实 52 周 FVC 改善 MD +68.8 mL(95% CI 30.3–107.4)[2];该效应达到 Maher 2024 MID ≥50 mL/年,但未达到 du Bois 2011 ~80 mL 或试验惯例 100 mL 阈值,因此临床意义存在争议。FVC 是 FDA 验证的替代终点(validated surrogate),并非患者重要结局(POI)。全因死亡率方向有利但未达统计学显著(HR 0.66,95% CI 0.41–1.08;样本量不足)[2];复合 PFS(加重/住院/死亡)未达到预设显著性阈值[2]。[Upgrade: LLM 2 期数据 → Anchor 加入 3 期 FIBRONEER-IPF + NMA]Yin 2026 网络 meta 分析(NMA,n=16 525)将 nerandomilast 18 mg 列为 FVC 改善最优药物之一[3];Mendoza 2025 NMA(n=15 813)结果一致[4]。与吡非尼酮和尼达尼布的比较(（无头对头试验）):ASCEND 试验中吡非尼酮 52 周减少 FVC 下降 193 mL(95% CI 83–303)[5];INPULSIS 试验中尼达尼布减缓年 FVC 下降 125.2 mL/年(95% CI 77.7–172.8)[6]。Petnak 2021 meta 分析(n=12 956)汇总抗纤维化药全因死亡率 RR ≈ 0.55(95% CI 0.39–0.78)[7];Loveman NMA(n=10 326)针对尼达尼布死亡终点[8]。ATS/ERS/JRS/ALAT 2022 指南对吡非尼酮和尼达尼布均给予强推荐(GRADE 评级)[9]。(间接比较提示 nerandomilast 的 FVC 获益可能叠加于背景抗纤维化药,但长期生存获益尚未证实)

    安全性方面:2 期试验中腹泻(9 mg 组 17% vs 安慰剂 8%)、恶心、头痛更常见;严重不良事件 9 mg 组 13% vs 安慰剂 17%[1]。FIBRONEER-IPF 3 期试验中,≥2 级腹泻发生率 nerandomilast 组 36.6% vs 安慰剂 24.7%(NNH ≈ 9)[2];严重不良事件发生率与安慰剂相似(RR ~1.0);治疗相关死亡无显著增加[2]。停药率与安慰剂相当[2]。总体耐受性特征与尼达尼布的胃肠道效应相似;长期安全性数据仍有限。

    总结:奈仑米司他(nerandomilast)是 IPF 领域的新兴治疗选择,3 期 FIBRONEER-IPF 试验证实其 52 周 FVC 改善具有统计学意义且达到部分 MID 阈值,但主要 POI 复合终点未达标,全因死亡率未显著降低。NMA 将其 FVC 改善列为最优之一[3][4],但缺乏与吡非尼酮/尼达尼布的头对头比较。当前证据边界:nerandomilast 的长期生存获益和安全性尚待进一步随访数据;临床决策应结合患者偏好和现有抗纤维化药的成熟数据。    Nerandomilast (BI 1015550) is an oral preferential PDE4B inhibitor. Its pivotal phase 2 trial (N=147 IPF patients, ~60% on background pirfenidone or nintedanib) evaluated 12‑week FVC change: least‑squares mean change was –5.6 mL with nerandomilast 9 mg BID and –79.7 mL with placebo (placebo‑adjusted difference 74.1 mL, 95% CI 27.4–120.8; P=0.002) [1]. The 18 mg BID dose showed a similar difference of 81.3 mL (95% CI 33.7–129.0). The FIBRONEER-IPF phase 3 trial (read out 2025, N=1177) confirmed a 52‑week FVC improvement MD +68.8 mL (95% CI 30.3–107.4) [2]; this effect meets the Maher 2024 MID ≥50 mL/year but fails the du Bois 2011 ~80 mL or trial convention 100 mL thresholds, making clinical significance debated. FVC is an FDA‑validated surrogate, not a patient‑important outcome (POI).All‑cause mortality was directionally favourable but not statistically significant (HR 0.66, 95% CI 0.41–1.08; underpowered) [2]; the composite PFS (exacerbation/hospitalisation/death) did not meet its pre‑specified significance threshold [2].[Upgrade: LLM phase‑2 data → Anchor adds phase‑3 FIBRONEER‑IPF + NMAs]The Yin 2026 network meta‑analysis (NMA, n=16 525) ranks nerandomilast 18 mg among the best for FVC improvement [3]; the Mendoza 2025 NMA (n=15 813) is consistent [4].Comparison with pirfenidone and nintedanib (no head‑to‑head trials exist): in the ASCEND trial, pirfenidone reduced FVC decline by 193 mL (95% CI 83–303) over 52 weeks [5]; in the INPULSIS trials, nintedanib slowed annual FVC decline by 125.2 mL/year (95% CI 77.7–172.8) [6]. The Petnak 2021 meta‑analysis (n=12 956) pooled antifibrotic all‑cause mortality RR ≈ 0.55 (95% CI 0.39–0.78) [7]; the Loveman NMA (n=10 326) addresses nintedanib mortality [8]. The ATS/ERS/JRS/ALAT 2022 guideline strongly recommends both pirfenidone and nintedanib (GRADE‑rated) [9].(Indirect comparison suggests nerandomilast's FVC benefit may be additive to background antifibrotics, but long‑term survival benefit is unproven)

    Safety: in the phase 2 trial, diarrhoea (17% with 9 mg vs 8% placebo), nausea, and headache were more common; serious adverse events occurred in 13% (9 mg) vs 17% (placebo) [1]. In FIBRONEER‑IPF, ≥grade 2 diarrhoea occurred in 36.6% with nerandomilast vs 24.7% with placebo (NNH ≈ 9) [2]; serious AE rates were similar to placebo (RR ~1.0); no significant increase in treatment‑related deaths [2]. Discontinuation was similar to placebo [2].The tolerability profile resembles nintedanib's GI effects; long‑term safety data remain limited.

    Summary: Nerandomilast is an emerging treatment option for IPF. The phase 3 FIBRONEER‑IPF trial demonstrated a statistically significant 52‑week FVC improvement meeting some MID thresholds, but the primary POI composite endpoint was not met and all‑cause mortality was not significantly reduced. NMAs rank its FVC improvement among the best [3][4], but head‑to‑head comparisons with pirfenidone/nintedanib are lacking.Evidence boundary: long‑term survival benefit and safety of nerandomilast await further follow‑up; clinical decisions should incorporate patient preferences and the mature data of existing antifibrotics.

    🔴→✅→💡 校正卡(模型说 → 核验真相 → 为什么)Correction cards (said → verified → why)
    stalestale evidence
    🔴
    模型说：Model said: 称「phase 3 FIBRONEER-IPF 进行中、缺 3 期数据」,自评信心 low。Says 'phase 3 FIBRONEER-IPF is ongoing / lacks phase-3 data', self-rated confidence low.
    ✅
    核验真相：Verified: FIBRONEER-IPF 3 phase(2025)已读出阳性:18 mg +68.8 mL(95% CI 30.3–107.4);NMA 列其改善 FVC 最优(Yin, Front Pharmacol 2026, PMID 41808863)。FIBRONEER-IPF phase 3 (2025) read out positive: 18 mg +68.8 mL (95% CI 30.3–107.4); an NMA ranks it best for FVC (Yin, Front Pharmacol 2026, PMID 41808863).
    💡
    为什么：Why: 知识停在 2 期阶段,把已完成且阳性的 3 期当成「仍在进行」,因而低估了证据强度。Its knowledge stops at the phase-2 stage, so it treats a completed, positive phase-3 trial as 'still ongoing' and under-rates the evidence.
    PMIDwrong-paperwrong citation
    🔴
    模型说：Model said: 引「Noble 2016 = PMID 26886654」(pirfenidone 合并死亡)、「Rochwerg 2017 = PMID 28456699」(nintedanib 死亡 meta)。Cites 'Noble 2016 = PMID 26886654' (pooled pirfenidone mortality) and 'Rochwerg 2017 = PMID 28456699' (nintedanib mortality MA).
    ✅
    核验真相：Verified: 26886654 实为一篇缺氧标志物预后论文(Zheng, Medicine 2016);28456699 实为一篇 FFR 心血管事件预测论文(Li, JACC Cardiovasc Interv 2017)。nerandomilast phase 2 35569036、ASCEND 24836312、INPULSIS 24836310 三条引对。26886654 is a hypoxia-marker prognostic paper (Zheng, Medicine 2016); 28456699 is an FFR cardiovascular-events paper (Li, JACC Cardiovasc Interv 2017). Its nerandomilast-phase-2 35569036, ASCEND 24836312 and INPULSIS 24836310 citations are correct.
    💡
    为什么：Why: 5 条引用里 3 条对、2 条对照药死亡引用指向完全无关论文(缺氧预后、FFR 心血管)。Three of five citations are right; the two comparator-drug mortality citations point to completely unrelated papers (hypoxia prognosis, FFR cardiovascular).

    💡 Anchor 校正要点总结(读者快速版)Anchor correction · Key takeaways (executive summary)
    • LLM 知识停留在 2 期阶段,将已完成且阳性的 FIBRONEER-IPF 3 期视为「进行中」,Anchor 校正补充了 3 期数据及 NMA 排名。
    • LLM 引用的两个对照药死亡 PMID(26886654、28456699)指向完全无关论文,Anchor 校正为 Petnak 2021 MA 和 Loveman NMA 等正确来源。
    • Anchor 校正后,nerandomilast 的 FVC 获益达到部分 MID 阈值但主要 POI 复合终点未达标,临床获益证据强度低于 LLM 暗示水平。
    • The LLM's knowledge stopped at phase 2, treating the completed, positive FIBRONEER-IPF phase 3 as 'ongoing'; Anchor correction adds phase 3 data and NMA rankings.
    • Two comparator mortality PMIDs cited by the LLM (26886654, 28456699) pointed to completely unrelated papers; Anchor corrects to Petnak 2021 MA and Loveman NMA.
    • After Anchor correction, nerandomilast's FVC benefit meets some MID thresholds but the primary POI composite endpoint was not met, making the evidence for clinical benefit weaker than the LLM implied.
    Ⓐ未矫正 · 原话Uncorrected · Gemini 3 Flash
    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    Nerandomilast（BI 1015550，奈拉多米拉司）是一种在研的优先磷酸二酯酶4B（PDE4B）抑制剂。其治疗特发性肺纤维化（IPF）的疗效证据目前来自一项2期随机对照试验[1]。

    ### 用力肺活量（FVC）下降
    * **Nerandomilast：** 在一项为期12周的2期试验中，nerandomilast阻止了FVC下降。在未接受背景抗纤维化药物的患者中，nerandomilast的校正后平均FVC变化为+5.7 mL，而安慰剂为-81.7 mL（差值：87.4 mL；95% CI，44.8至130.0）。在已接受背景nintedanib（尼达尼布）或pirfenidone（吡非尼酮）治疗的患者中，与安慰剂的差值为88.4 mL（95% CI，29.5至147.3）[1]。
    * **Nintedanib：** 在3期INPULSIS试验中，nintedanib将FVC的年下降率降低了约50%。校正后年下降率为-114.7 mL/年，而安慰剂为-223.5 mL/年（差值：108.8 mL/年；95% CI，70.3至149.5）[2]。
    * **Pirfenidone：** 在3期ASCEND试验中，与安慰剂相比，pirfenidone将FVC下降≥10%或死亡的患者比例降低了47.9%（OR 0.52；95% CI，0.38至0.71）[3]。

    ### 死亡率
    * **Nerandomilast：** 尚未确立死亡获益；该2期试验检验效能不足且持续时间过短（12周），无法评估该终点[1]。
    * **Nintedanib：** INPULSIS试验的汇总分析显示，全因死亡率有降低趋势，但未达统计学显著性（HR 0.70；95% CI，0.43至1.12）[2]。
    * **Pirfenidone：** ASCEND和CAPACITY试验的汇总分析表明，52周时全因死亡率显著降低（HR 0.52；95% CI，0.31至0.87）[3]。

    ### 比较总结
    Nerandomilast的12周FVC获益（约88 mL）在数值上与nintedanib的总52周获益（约109 mL）相似，提示可能存在更高的效价或快速起效。然而，nerandomilast的长期持久性、安全性和死亡获益仍有待3期试验（FIBRONEER-IPF）结果确认。

    **参考文献**
    1. Richeldi L, Azuma A, Cottin V, et al. Trial of a Preferential Phosphodiesterase 4B Inhibitor for Idiopathic Pulmonary Fibrosis. N Engl J Med. 2022;386(23):2178-2187. PMID: 35569488.
    2. Richeldi L, du Bois RM, Raghu G, et al. Efficacy and safety of nintedanib in idiopathic pulmonary fibrosis. N Engl J Med. 2014;370(22):2071-2082. PMID: 24836310.
    3. King TE Jr, Bradford WZ, Castro-Bernardini S, et al. A phase 3 trial of pirfenidone in patients with idiopathic pulmonary fibrosis. N Engl J Med. 2014;370(22):2083-2092. PMID: 24836312.

    **自信程度自评：** 高。所引数据均来自发表在高影响力期刊上的2期和3期主要试验。单句理由：该比较基于nintedanib/pirfenidone已确立的3期数据和nerandomilast的高质量2期数据。    Nerandomilast (BI 1015550) is an investigational, preferential phosphodiesterase 4B (PDE4B) inhibitor. Evidence for its efficacy in idiopathic pulmonary fibrosis (IPF) currently rests on a Phase 2 randomized controlled trial [1].

    ### Forced Vital Capacity (FVC) Decline
    * **Nerandomilast:** In a 12-week Phase 2 trial, nerandomilast prevented FVC decline. In patients not receiving background antifibrotics, the adjusted mean change in FVC was +5.7 mL for nerandomilast versus -81.7 mL for placebo (difference: 87.4 mL; 95% CI, 44.8 to 130.0). In patients already receiving background nintedanib or pirfenidone, the difference versus placebo was 88.4 mL (95% CI, 29.5 to 147.3) [1].
    * **Nintedanib:** In the Phase 3 INPULSIS trials, nintedanib reduced the annual rate of FVC decline by approximately 50%. The adjusted annual rate was -114.7 mL/year versus -223.5 mL/year for placebo (difference: 108.8 mL/year; 95% CI, 70.3 to 149.5) [2].
    * **Pirfenidone:** In the Phase 3 ASCEND trial, pirfenidone reduced the proportion of patients with a ≥10% FVC decline or death by 47.9% compared to placebo (OR 0.52; 95% CI, 0.38 to 0.71) [3].

    ### Mortality
    * **Nerandomilast:** No mortality benefit has been established; the Phase 2 trial was underpowered and too short (12 weeks) to assess this endpoint [1].
    * **Nintedanib:** Pooled analysis of INPULSIS trials showed a non-significant trend toward reduced all-cause mortality (HR 0.70; 95% CI, 0.43 to 1.12) [2].
    * **Pirfenidone:** Pooled analysis of ASCEND and CAPACITY trials demonstrated a significant reduction in all-cause mortality at 52 weeks (HR 0.52; 95% CI, 0.31 to 0.87) [3].

    ### Comparison Summary
    Nerandomilast’s 12-week FVC benefit (approx. 88 mL) is numerically similar to the total 52-week benefit of nintedanib (approx. 109 mL), suggesting potentially higher potency or a rapid onset of action. However, long-term durability, safety, and mortality benefits for nerandomilast remain unconfirmed pending Phase 3 (FIBRONEER-IPF) results.

    **References**
    1. Richeldi L, Azuma A, Cottin V, et al. Trial of a Preferential Phosphodiesterase 4B Inhibitor for Idiopathic Pulmonary Fibrosis. N Engl J Med. 2022;386(23):2178-2187. PMID: 35569488.
    2. Richeldi L, du Bois RM, Raghu G, et al. Efficacy and safety of nintedanib in idiopathic pulmonary fibrosis. N Engl J Med. 2014;370(22):2071-2082. PMID: 24836310.
    3. King TE Jr, Bradford WZ, Castro-Bernardini S, et al. A phase 3 trial of pirfenidone in patients with idiopathic pulmonary fibrosis. N Engl J Med. 2014;370(22):2083-2092. PMID: 24836312.

    **Confidence self-rating:** High. The data cited are from primary Phase 2 and Phase 3 trials published in high-impact journals. One-sentence justification: The comparison is based on established Phase 3 data for nintedanib/pirfenidone and high-quality Phase 2 data for nerandomilast.
    ⒷAnchor 校正版 · KB 锚定Anchor-corrected · KB-grounded修订Track净版Clean
    Nerandomilast (BI 1015550) 是一种研究性、优先性磷酸二酯酶 4B (PDE4B) 抑制剂。目前其在特发性肺纤维化 (IPF) 中的疗效证据主要来自一项 2 期随机对照试验 (RCT) [1] 和一项 3 期 RCT (FIBRONEER-IPF) [2]。

    ### 用力肺活量 (FVC) 下降
    * Nerandomilast: 在一项 12 周的 2 期试验中，nerandomilast 阻止了 FVC 下降。在未接受背景抗纤维化治疗的患者中，nerandomilast 组的调整后平均 FVC 变化为 +5.7 mL，而安慰剂组为 -81.7 mL (差异: 87.4 mL; 95% CI, 44.8 至 130.0) [1]。在已接受背景尼达尼布或吡非尼酮治疗的患者中，与安慰剂的差异为 88.4 mL (95% CI, 29.5 至 147.3) [1]。在 52 周的 3 期 FIBRONEER-IPF 试验中，nerandomilast 组 FVC 较基线的调整后平均变化为 +68.8 mL (95% CI 30.3–107.4) [2]，达到了 Maher 2024 提出的 ≥50 mL/年最小重要差异 (MID) 标准，因此具有临床意义 [3]。但该效应未达到 du Bois 2011 提出的 ~80 mL MID 或试验常用的 100 mL MID [3]。FVC 是经 FDA 验证的替代终点，但仍非患者重要结局 (POI) [2]。
    * Nintedanib: 在 3 期 INPULSIS 试验中，尼达尼布使 FVC 年下降率减少约 50%。调整后的年下降率为 -114.7 mL/年，而安慰剂为 -223.5 mL/年 (差异: 108.8 mL/年; 95% CI, 70.3 至 149.5) [4]。
    * Pirfenidone: 在 3 期 ASCEND 试验中，吡非尼酮使 FVC 下降 ≥10% 或死亡的患者比例降低 47.9% (OR 0.52; 95% CI, 0.38 至 0.71) [5]。

    ### 死亡率
    * Nerandomilast: No mortality benefit has been established; the Phase 2 trial was underpowered and too short (12 weeks) to assess this endpoint [1]. 在 52 周的 3 期 FIBRONEER-IPF 试验中，nerandomilast 组全因死亡风险比 (HR) 为 0.66 (95% CI 0.41–1.08)，方向有利但未达统计学显著性，主要因样本量不足 (underpowered) [2]。该试验的复合无进展生存期 (PFS) 终点 (加重/住院/死亡) 也未达到预设显著性阈值 [2]。
    * Nintedanib: INPULSIS 试验的汇总分析显示全因死亡率有非显著性降低趋势 (HR 0.70; 95% CI, 0.43 至 1.12) [4]。一项针对尼达尼布死亡终点的网络 meta 分析 (NMA) 也支持这一趋势 [6]。
    * Pirfenidone: ASCEND 和 CAPACITY 试验的汇总分析显示，52 周时全因死亡率显著降低 (HR 0.52; 95% CI, 0.31 至 0.87) [5]。Petnak 2021 的 meta 分析 (n=12956) 汇总所有抗纤维化药物，全因死亡率相对风险 (RR) 约为 0.55 [7]。

    ### 比较总结
    Nerandomilast 的 52 周 FVC 获益 (约 69 mL) 在数值上小于尼达尼布的年获益 (约 109 mL)，但 numerically similar to the total 52-week benefit of nintedanib (approx. 109 mL), suggesting potentially higher potency or a rapid onset of action. However, long-term durability, safety, and mortality benefits for nerandomilast remain unconfirmed pending Phase 3 (FIBRONEER-IPF) results. nerandomilast 的 FVC 效应在 52 周时达到 Maher MID，具有临床意义，但未达到更严格的 du Bois MID [3]。在死亡率方面，nerandomilast 的 3 期数据方向有利但未达统计学显著性，而吡非尼酮有显著的死亡率获益 [5][7]。LLM 仅引用 2 期试验 → Anchor 补充 3 期 FIBRONEER-IPF 结果 (n=1177) 及 Maher MID 解读 [2][3]

    一项纳入 16525 名患者的 Yin 2026 NMA 将 nerandomilast 18 mg 列为 FVC 改善的并列最优药物之一 [8]。另一项独立的 Mendoza 2025 NMA (n=15813) 得出一致结论 [8]。

    安全性: 在 52 周的 3 期试验中，nerandomilast 的严重不良事件 (SAE) 发生率与安慰剂相似 (RR ~1.0)，停药率也相近 [2]。但腹泻 (≥2 级) 发生率较高: nerandomilast 组 36.6% 对比安慰剂组 24.7% (绝对风险差 +11.9%，需治疗伤害数 NNH ≈ 9) [2]。该不良事件与剂量相关且患者可感知，临床耐受性需考量。未观察到治疗相关死亡显著增加 [2]。

    总结: Nerandomilast 是 IPF 领域一种新型 PDE4B 抑制剂，3 期 FIBRONEER-IPF 试验显示其在 52 周时对 FVC 下降有临床意义的减缓作用 (达到 Maher MID)，但未达到更严格的 MID 标准 [2][3]。在死亡率方面，数据方向有利但未达统计学显著性 [2]。与已获批的吡非尼酮和尼达尼布相比，nerandomilast 的 FVC 效应在数值上较小，且缺乏已证实的死亡率获益 [4][5][7]。其安全性特征以腹泻为主，但严重不良事件未增加 [2]。Nerandomilast 目前尚未获得任何主要指南 (如 ATS/ERS/JRS/ALAT 2022) 的推荐，该指南仅强推荐吡非尼酮和尼达尼布 [9]。    Nerandomilast (BI 1015550) is an investigational, preferential phosphodiesterase 4B (PDE4B) inhibitor. Evidence for its efficacy in idiopathic pulmonary fibrosis (IPF) currently rests on a Phase 2 randomized controlled trial (RCT) [1] and a Phase 3 RCT (FIBRONEER-IPF) [2].

    ### Forced Vital Capacity (FVC) Decline
    * Nerandomilast: In a 12-week Phase 2 trial, nerandomilast prevented FVC decline. In patients not receiving background antifibrotics, the adjusted mean change in FVC was +5.7 mL for nerandomilast versus -81.7 mL for placebo (difference: 87.4 mL; 95% CI, 44.8 to 130.0) [1]. In patients already receiving background nintedanib or pirfenidone, the difference versus placebo was 88.4 mL (95% CI, 29.5 to 147.3) [1]. In the 52-week Phase 3 FIBRONEER-IPF trial, the adjusted mean change in FVC from baseline was +68.8 mL (95% CI 30.3–107.4) for nerandomilast [2], meeting the Maher 2024 minimal important difference (MID) of ≥50 mL/year, thus considered clinically meaningful [3]. However, it did not meet the du Bois 2011 MID of ~80 mL or the trial convention of 100 mL [3]. FVC is an FDA-validated surrogate endpoint, not a patient-important outcome (POI) [2].
    * Nintedanib: In the Phase 3 INPULSIS trials, nintedanib reduced the annual rate of FVC decline by approximately 50%. The adjusted annual rate was -114.7 mL/year versus -223.5 mL/year for placebo (difference: 108.8 mL/year; 95% CI, 70.3 to 149.5) [4].
    * Pirfenidone: In the Phase 3 ASCEND trial, pirfenidone reduced the proportion of patients with a ≥10% FVC decline or death by 47.9% compared to placebo (OR 0.52; 95% CI, 0.38 to 0.71) [5].

    ### Mortality
    * Nerandomilast: No mortality benefit has been established; the Phase 2 trial was underpowered and too short (12 weeks) to assess this endpoint [1]. In the 52-week Phase 3 FIBRONEER-IPF trial, the hazard ratio (HR) for all-cause mortality was 0.66 (95% CI 0.41–1.08), directionally favourable but not statistically significant, primarily due to being underpowered [2]. The composite progression-free survival (PFS) endpoint (exacerbation/hospitalisation/death) also did not meet the pre-specified significance threshold [2].
    * Nintedanib: Pooled analysis of INPULSIS trials showed a non-significant trend toward reduced all-cause mortality (HR 0.70; 95% CI, 0.43 to 1.12) [4]. A network meta-analysis (NMA) specific to nintedanib's mortality endpoint supports this trend [6].
    * Pirfenidone: Pooled analysis of ASCEND and CAPACITY trials demonstrated a significant reduction in all-cause mortality at 52 weeks (HR 0.52; 95% CI, 0.31 to 0.87) [5]. The Petnak 2021 meta-analysis (n=12956) pooled all antifibrotics, yielding a relative risk (RR) for all-cause mortality of approximately 0.55 [7].

    ### Comparison Summary
    Nerandomilast's 52-week FVC benefit (approximately 69 mL) is numerically smaller than nintedanib's annual benefit (approximately 109 mL), but numerically similar to the total 52-week benefit of nintedanib (approx. 109 mL), suggesting potentially higher potency or a rapid onset of action. However, long-term durability, safety, and mortality benefits for nerandomilast remain unconfirmed pending Phase 3 (FIBRONEER-IPF) results. nerandomilast's FVC effect at 52 weeks meets the Maher MID, indicating clinical meaningfulness, but does not meet the stricter du Bois MID [3]. For mortality, the Phase 3 data for nerandomilast are directionally favourable but not statistically significant, whereas pirfenidone shows a significant mortality benefit [5][7].LLM cited only Phase 2 trial → Anchor adds Phase 3 FIBRONEER-IPF results (n=1177) and Maher MID interpretation [2][3]

    A Yin 2026 NMA including 16,525 patients ranks nerandomilast 18 mg as joint-best for FVC improvement [8]. An independent Mendoza 2025 NMA (n=15,813) reaches consistent conclusions [8].

    Safety: In the 52-week Phase 3 trial, serious adverse events (SAEs) with nerandomilast were similar to placebo (RR ~1.0), and discontinuation rates were comparable [2]. However, diarrhoea (≥ grade 2) was more frequent: 36.6% with nerandomilast vs 24.7% with placebo (absolute risk difference +11.9%, number needed to harm NNH ≈ 9) [2]. This adverse event is dose-related and patient-perceived, requiring clinical tolerability consideration. No significant increase in treatment-related deaths was observed [2].

    Summary: Nerandomilast is a novel PDE4B inhibitor for IPF. The Phase 3 FIBRONEER-IPF trial demonstrated a clinically meaningful slowing of FVC decline at 52 weeks (meeting the Maher MID), though it did not meet stricter MID thresholds [2][3]. For mortality, the data are directionally favourable but not statistically significant [2]. Compared to approved pirfenidone and nintedanib, nerandomilast's FVC effect is numerically smaller and lacks a proven mortality benefit [4][5][7]. Its safety profile is notable for diarrhoea, but SAEs are not increased [2]. Nerandomilast is not yet recommended by any major guideline (e.g., ATS/ERS/JRS/ALAT 2022), which strongly recommends only pirfenidone and nintedanib [9].
    Nerandomilast (BI 1015550) 是一种研究性、优先性磷酸二酯酶 4B (PDE4B) 抑制剂。目前其在特发性肺纤维化 (IPF) 中的疗效证据主要来自一项 2 期随机对照试验 (RCT) [1] 和一项 3 期 RCT (FIBRONEER-IPF) [2]。

    ### 用力肺活量 (FVC) 下降
    * Nerandomilast: 在一项 12 周的 2 期试验中，nerandomilast 阻止了 FVC 下降。在未接受背景抗纤维化治疗的患者中，nerandomilast 组的调整后平均 FVC 变化为 +5.7 mL，而安慰剂组为 -81.7 mL (差异: 87.4 mL; 95% CI, 44.8 至 130.0) [1]。在已接受背景尼达尼布或吡非尼酮治疗的患者中，与安慰剂的差异为 88.4 mL (95% CI, 29.5 至 147.3) [1]。在 52 周的 3 期 FIBRONEER-IPF 试验中，nerandomilast 组 FVC 较基线的调整后平均变化为 +68.8 mL (95% CI 30.3–107.4) [2]，达到了 Maher 2024 提出的 ≥50 mL/年最小重要差异 (MID) 标准，因此具有临床意义 [3]。但该效应未达到 du Bois 2011 提出的 ~80 mL MID 或试验常用的 100 mL MID [3]。FVC 是经 FDA 验证的替代终点，但仍非患者重要结局 (POI) [2]。
    * Nintedanib: 在 3 期 INPULSIS 试验中，尼达尼布使 FVC 年下降率减少约 50%。调整后的年下降率为 -114.7 mL/年，而安慰剂为 -223.5 mL/年 (差异: 108.8 mL/年; 95% CI, 70.3 至 149.5) [4]。
    * Pirfenidone: 在 3 期 ASCEND 试验中，吡非尼酮使 FVC 下降 ≥10% 或死亡的患者比例降低 47.9% (OR 0.52; 95% CI, 0.38 至 0.71) [5]。

    ### 死亡率
    * Nerandomilast: 在 52 周的 3 期 FIBRONEER-IPF 试验中，nerandomilast 组全因死亡风险比 (HR) 为 0.66 (95% CI 0.41–1.08)，方向有利但未达统计学显著性，主要因样本量不足 (underpowered) [2]。该试验的复合无进展生存期 (PFS) 终点 (加重/住院/死亡) 也未达到预设显著性阈值 [2]。
    * Nintedanib: INPULSIS 试验的汇总分析显示全因死亡率有非显著性降低趋势 (HR 0.70; 95% CI, 0.43 至 1.12) [4]。一项针对尼达尼布死亡终点的网络 meta 分析 (NMA) 也支持这一趋势 [6]。
    * Pirfenidone: ASCEND 和 CAPACITY 试验的汇总分析显示，52 周时全因死亡率显著降低 (HR 0.52; 95% CI, 0.31 至 0.87) [5]。Petnak 2021 的 meta 分析 (n=12956) 汇总所有抗纤维化药物，全因死亡率相对风险 (RR) 约为 0.55 [7]。

    ### 比较总结
    Nerandomilast 的 52 周 FVC 获益 (约 69 mL) 在数值上小于尼达尼布的年获益 (约 109 mL)，但 nerandomilast 的 FVC 效应在 52 周时达到 Maher MID，具有临床意义，但未达到更严格的 du Bois MID [3]。在死亡率方面，nerandomilast 的 3 期数据方向有利但未达统计学显著性，而吡非尼酮有显著的死亡率获益 [5][7]。[Upgrade: LLM 仅引用 2 期试验 → Anchor 补充 3 期 FIBRONEER-IPF 结果 (n=1177) 及 Maher MID 解读 [2][3]]

    一项纳入 16525 名患者的 Yin 2026 NMA 将 nerandomilast 18 mg 列为 FVC 改善的并列最优药物之一 [8]。另一项独立的 Mendoza 2025 NMA (n=15813) 得出一致结论 [8]。

    安全性: 在 52 周的 3 期试验中，nerandomilast 的严重不良事件 (SAE) 发生率与安慰剂相似 (RR ~1.0)，停药率也相近 [2]。但腹泻 (≥2 级) 发生率较高: nerandomilast 组 36.6% 对比安慰剂组 24.7% (绝对风险差 +11.9%，需治疗伤害数 NNH ≈ 9) [2]。该不良事件与剂量相关且患者可感知，临床耐受性需考量。未观察到治疗相关死亡显著增加 [2]。

    总结: Nerandomilast 是 IPF 领域一种新型 PDE4B 抑制剂，3 期 FIBRONEER-IPF 试验显示其在 52 周时对 FVC 下降有临床意义的减缓作用 (达到 Maher MID)，但未达到更严格的 MID 标准 [2][3]。在死亡率方面，数据方向有利但未达统计学显著性 [2]。与已获批的吡非尼酮和尼达尼布相比，nerandomilast 的 FVC 效应在数值上较小，且缺乏已证实的死亡率获益 [4][5][7]。其安全性特征以腹泻为主，但严重不良事件未增加 [2]。Nerandomilast 目前尚未获得任何主要指南 (如 ATS/ERS/JRS/ALAT 2022) 的推荐，该指南仅强推荐吡非尼酮和尼达尼布 [9]。    Nerandomilast (BI 1015550) is an investigational, preferential phosphodiesterase 4B (PDE4B) inhibitor. Evidence for its efficacy in idiopathic pulmonary fibrosis (IPF) currently rests on a Phase 2 randomized controlled trial (RCT) [1] and a Phase 3 RCT (FIBRONEER-IPF) [2].

    ### Forced Vital Capacity (FVC) Decline
    * Nerandomilast: In a 12-week Phase 2 trial, nerandomilast prevented FVC decline. In patients not receiving background antifibrotics, the adjusted mean change in FVC was +5.7 mL for nerandomilast versus -81.7 mL for placebo (difference: 87.4 mL; 95% CI, 44.8 to 130.0) [1]. In patients already receiving background nintedanib or pirfenidone, the difference versus placebo was 88.4 mL (95% CI, 29.5 to 147.3) [1]. In the 52-week Phase 3 FIBRONEER-IPF trial, the adjusted mean change in FVC from baseline was +68.8 mL (95% CI 30.3–107.4) for nerandomilast [2], meeting the Maher 2024 minimal important difference (MID) of ≥50 mL/year, thus considered clinically meaningful [3]. However, it did not meet the du Bois 2011 MID of ~80 mL or the trial convention of 100 mL [3]. FVC is an FDA-validated surrogate endpoint, not a patient-important outcome (POI) [2].
    * Nintedanib: In the Phase 3 INPULSIS trials, nintedanib reduced the annual rate of FVC decline by approximately 50%. The adjusted annual rate was -114.7 mL/year versus -223.5 mL/year for placebo (difference: 108.8 mL/year; 95% CI, 70.3 to 149.5) [4].
    * Pirfenidone: In the Phase 3 ASCEND trial, pirfenidone reduced the proportion of patients with a ≥10% FVC decline or death by 47.9% compared to placebo (OR 0.52; 95% CI, 0.38 to 0.71) [5].

    ### Mortality
    * Nerandomilast: In the 52-week Phase 3 FIBRONEER-IPF trial, the hazard ratio (HR) for all-cause mortality was 0.66 (95% CI 0.41–1.08), directionally favourable but not statistically significant, primarily due to being underpowered [2]. The composite progression-free survival (PFS) endpoint (exacerbation/hospitalisation/death) also did not meet the pre-specified significance threshold [2].
    * Nintedanib: Pooled analysis of INPULSIS trials showed a non-significant trend toward reduced all-cause mortality (HR 0.70; 95% CI, 0.43 to 1.12) [4]. A network meta-analysis (NMA) specific to nintedanib's mortality endpoint supports this trend [6].
    * Pirfenidone: Pooled analysis of ASCEND and CAPACITY trials demonstrated a significant reduction in all-cause mortality at 52 weeks (HR 0.52; 95% CI, 0.31 to 0.87) [5]. The Petnak 2021 meta-analysis (n=12956) pooled all antifibrotics, yielding a relative risk (RR) for all-cause mortality of approximately 0.55 [7].

    ### Comparison Summary
    Nerandomilast's 52-week FVC benefit (approximately 69 mL) is numerically smaller than nintedanib's annual benefit (approximately 109 mL), but nerandomilast's FVC effect at 52 weeks meets the Maher MID, indicating clinical meaningfulness, but does not meet the stricter du Bois MID [3]. For mortality, the Phase 3 data for nerandomilast are directionally favourable but not statistically significant, whereas pirfenidone shows a significant mortality benefit [5][7].[Upgrade: LLM cited only Phase 2 trial → Anchor adds Phase 3 FIBRONEER-IPF results (n=1177) and Maher MID interpretation [2][3]]

    A Yin 2026 NMA including 16,525 patients ranks nerandomilast 18 mg as joint-best for FVC improvement [8]. An independent Mendoza 2025 NMA (n=15,813) reaches consistent conclusions [8].

    Safety: In the 52-week Phase 3 trial, serious adverse events (SAEs) with nerandomilast were similar to placebo (RR ~1.0), and discontinuation rates were comparable [2]. However, diarrhoea (≥ grade 2) was more frequent: 36.6% with nerandomilast vs 24.7% with placebo (absolute risk difference +11.9%, number needed to harm NNH ≈ 9) [2]. This adverse event is dose-related and patient-perceived, requiring clinical tolerability consideration. No significant increase in treatment-related deaths was observed [2].

    Summary: Nerandomilast is a novel PDE4B inhibitor for IPF. The Phase 3 FIBRONEER-IPF trial demonstrated a clinically meaningful slowing of FVC decline at 52 weeks (meeting the Maher MID), though it did not meet stricter MID thresholds [2][3]. For mortality, the data are directionally favourable but not statistically significant [2]. Compared to approved pirfenidone and nintedanib, nerandomilast's FVC effect is numerically smaller and lacks a proven mortality benefit [4][5][7]. Its safety profile is notable for diarrhoea, but SAEs are not increased [2]. Nerandomilast is not yet recommended by any major guideline (e.g., ATS/ERS/JRS/ALAT 2022), which strongly recommends only pirfenidone and nintedanib [9].

    Ⓐ未矫正 · 原话Uncorrected · OpenEvidence
    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    **问题：** 目前关于 nerandomilast 在特发性肺纤维化（IPF）中的证据如何（包括任何 3 期临床试验结果），以及在减缓 FVC 下降和降低死亡率方面，它与 pirfenidone 和 nintedanib 相比如何？

    ---

    Nerandomilast 禁忌——prednisone、azathioprine 和 N-acetylcysteine 的三联疗法对 IPF 有害，绝不应使用。PANTHER-IPF 试验 (2012) 因安全性问题提前终止：中期分析显示，三联疗法组与安慰剂组相比，死亡风险 HR 8.00 (95% CI 1.01–63.17)；住院率和死亡率增加。高确定性证据（GRADE：因安全性而终止的决定性 RCT）。PMID: 22607134; DOI: 10.1056/NEJMoa1113354。

    Nerandomilast（磷酸二酯酶-4B 抑制剂）在 3 期 FIBRONEER-IPF 试验 (2025, n=1177) 中显示出疗效：在 52 周时，18 mg BID 组与安慰剂组相比，治疗差异为 +68.8 mL (95% CI 30.3–107.4; P<0.001)；9 mg BID 组为 +44.9 mL (95% CI 6.4–83.3; P=0.02)。77.7% 的患者使用了背景抗纤维化药物（nintedanib 或 pirfenidone）。中等确定性证据（GRADE：单项大型 RCT，随访时间短，未证明有死亡率获益）。PMID: 10.1056/NEJMoa2414108。[1] 延长随访显示对复合终点（急性加重/住院/死亡）无影响：18 mg 组 HR 0.99 (95% CI 0.75–1.31)；死亡率 HR 0.66 (95% CI 0.41–1.08)，数值上较低但无统计学意义。中等确定性。PMID: 41738262。[2]

    Pirfenidone 和 nintedanib 仍为标准治疗：两者与安慰剂相比均可将 FVC 下降降低约 50%（获益约为每年 100–150 mL）。Pirfenidone：差异为 −45.1 mL/year (CAPACITY/ASCEND 合并分析)。Nintedanib：差异为 −109.9 mL/year (INPULSIS 合并分析)。两者均为高确定性。在单项试验中，两者均未显著降低死亡率。头对头比较 (CleanUP-IPF 事后分析, 2024) 表明 nintedanib 比 pirfenidone 更能减缓 12 个月的 FVC 下降 (+106 mL, 95% CI 34–178)，但这属于观察性研究，且在 24 个月时该差异有所减弱。低确定性。PMID: 38030064。[3]

    不良反应：Nerandomilast 导致 41.3% (18 mg) 的患者出现腹泻，而安慰剂组为 16.0%；停药率 <4%。与 pirfenidone 的药物相互作用会使 nerandomilast 水平降低约 50%；仅 18 mg 剂量在联合使用 pirfenidone 时有效。[1][4]

    总体信心：中等 —— Nerandomilast 对 FVC 的疗效已通过大型 3 期试验得到证实，但死亡率/临床事件数据尚不成熟；且不存在与 pirfenidone/nintedanib 单药治疗的头对头比较。

Richeldi L, Azuma A, Cottin V, et al.    Nerandomilast is contraindicated — triple therapy with prednisone, azathioprine, and N-acetylcysteine is harmful in IPF and should never be used. The PANTHER-IPF trial (2012) was stopped early for harm: HR 8.00 (95% CI 1.01–63.17) for death in the triple therapy arm versus placebo at interim analysis; increased hospitalizations and death. High-certainty evidence (GRADE: definitive RCT stopped for safety). PMID: 22607134; DOI: 10.1056/NEJMoa1113354.

    Nerandomilast (phosphodiesterase-4B inhibitor) showed efficacy in the phase 3 FIBRONEER-IPF trial (2025, n=1177): treatment difference +68.8 mL (95% CI 30.3–107.4; P<0.001) for 18 mg BID versus placebo at 52 weeks; +44.9 mL (95% CI 6.4–83.3; P=0.02) for 9 mg BID. 77.7% were on background antifibrotic (nintedanib or pirfenidone). Moderate-certainty evidence (GRADE: single large RCT, short follow-up, no mortality benefit demonstrated). PMID: 10.1056/NEJMoa2414108. [1] Extended follow-up showed no effect on composite endpoint (exacerbation/hospitalization/death): HR 0.99 (95% CI 0.75–1.31) for 18 mg; mortality HR 0.66 (95% CI 0.41–1.08), numerically lower but not significant. Moderate-certainty. PMID: 41738262. [2]

    Pirfenidone and nintedanib remain standard: both reduce FVC decline by ~50% versus placebo (approximately 100–150 mL/year benefit). Pirfenidone: −45.1 mL/year difference (CAPACITY/ASCEND pooled). Nintedanib: −109.9 mL/year difference (INPULSIS pooled). High-certainty for both. Neither significantly reduces mortality in individual trials. Head-to-head comparison (CleanUP-IPF post hoc, 2024) suggested nintedanib slowed 12-month FVC decline more than pirfenidone (+106 mL, 95% CI 34–178), but this was observational and attenuated by 24 months. Low-certainty. PMID: 38030064. [3]

    Harms: Nerandomilast caused diarrhea in 41.3% (18 mg) versus 16.0% (placebo); discontinuation <4%. Drug interaction with pirfenidone reduces nerandomilast levels ~50%; only 18 mg dose effective with pirfenidone. [1][4]

    Overall confidence: Moderate — Nerandomilast efficacy on FVC is well-established from large phase 3 trial, but mortality/clinical event data immature; no head-to-head comparison with pirfenidone/nintedanib monotherapy exists.

Wuyts WA, Richeldi L, Assassi S, et al.; Trial of a Preferential Phosphodiesterase 4B Inhibitor for Idiopathic Pulmonary Fibrosis. The New England Journal of Medicine. 2022. Richeldi L, Azuma A, Cottin V, et al.
    ⒷAnchor 校正版 · KB 锚定Anchor-corrected · KB-grounded修订Track净版Clean
    Nerandomilast 属于禁忌——泼尼松、硫唑嘌呤和 N-乙酰半胱氨酸三联疗法在 IPF 中是有害的，绝不应使用。PANTHER-IPF 试验(2012)因安全性问题提前终止：三联疗法对比安慰剂的死亡 HR 为 8.00(95% CI 1.01–63.17)；住院和死亡增加。高质量证据(GRADE：因安全性提前终止的确定性 RCT)。PMID: 22607134；DOI: 10.1056/NEJMoa1113354。Nerandomilast 是一种新型 PDE4B 抑制剂，并非禁忌。上述「禁用」描述实为检索污染——PANTHER 三联疗法(泼尼松+硫唑嘌呤+N-乙酰半胱氨酸)因增加死亡风险而提前终止，与 nerandomilast 无关。FIBRONEER-IPF 3 期试验(2025 年，n=1177)显示：nerandomilast 18 mg 每日两次对比安慰剂，52 周 FVC 改善差异为 +68.8 mL(95% CI 30.3–107.4；P<0.001)；9 mg 每日两次为 +44.9 mL(95% CI 6.4–83.3；P=0.02)。77.7% 的患者同时接受背景抗纤维化治疗(尼达尼布或吡非尼酮)。LLM 单一 RCT tier 3 → Anchor Yin 2026 NMA tier 1 n=16525Yin 2026 网络 meta 分析(NMA)(n=16525)将 nerandomilast 18 mg 评为 FVC 改善的并列最优药物 [1]；Mendoza 2025 NMA(n=15813)得出一致排名 [2]。FVC 是经 FDA 验证的替代终点(validated surrogate)，并非患者重要结局(patient-important outcome, POI)。根据 Maher 2024 提出的 MID ≥50 mL/年，+68.8 mL 达到该阈值，具有临床意义 [3]；但根据 du Bois 2011 的 ~80 mL 或试验惯例的 100 mL 则未达到。在患者重要结局方面：全因死亡 HR 0.66(95% CI 0.41–1.08)，方向有利但未达统计学显著性(样本量不足)；复合终点(急性加重/住院/死亡)HR 0.99(95% CI 0.75–1.31)，未达到预设显著性阈值 [4]。Petnak 2021 meta 分析(MA)(n=12956)汇总抗纤维化药物(吡非尼酮/尼达尼布)的合并全因死亡 RR 约为 0.55(95% CI 0.39–0.78) [5]。ATS/ERS/JRS/ALAT 2022 指南对吡非尼酮和尼达尼布均给予强推荐 [6]。CleanUP-IPF 事后分析(2024)提示尼达尼布在 12 个月时 FVC 下降减缓优于吡非尼酮(+106 mL, 95% CI 34–178)，但该分析为观察性，且至 24 个月时差异减弱 [7]。

    Nerandomilast 引起腹泻的比例为 41.3%(18 mg)对比 16.0%(安慰剂)；停药率 <4%。与吡非尼酮的药物相互作用使 nerandomilast 水平降低约 50%；与吡非尼酮联用时仅 18 mg 剂量有效。安全性方面：nerandomilast 18 mg 组严重不良事件(SAE)发生率与安慰剂相似(RR ~1.0)，停药率与安慰剂相当 [1]。腹泻(≥2 级)发生率：18 mg 组 36.6% 对比安慰剂 24.7%(绝对风险差 +11.9%，需治害数 NNH ≈ 9)，为剂量相关、患者可感知的不良事件 [1]。未观察到治疗相关死亡增加 [1]。与吡非尼酮和尼达尼布的安全性比较：吡非尼酮常见光敏反应和胃肠道不适；尼达尼布常见腹泻(约 60%)和肝酶升高。Nerandomilast 的腹泻率(36.6%)低于尼达尼布，但（缺乏直接头对头比较） [6]。

    总体信心：中等——Nerandomilast 对 FVC 的疗效已通过大型 3 期试验充分证实，但死亡/临床事件数据尚不成熟；尚无与吡非尼酮/尼达尼布单药治疗的头对头比较。

    总结：Nerandomilast 是 IPF 的新型有效治疗选择，基于 FIBRONEER-IPF 3 期试验在 FVC 改善方面达到主要终点(+68.8 mL，达到 Maher MID)，且 Yin 2026 NMA 将其评为并列最优。然而，患者重要结局(死亡、复合终点)未达统计学显著性，证据尚不成熟。与吡非尼酮和尼达尼布的比较主要来自 NMA 排名，（缺乏直接头对头试验）。安全性方面，nerandomilast 的腹泻率低于尼达尼布，但需临床监测。

    临床警示：nerandomilast 目前仅获 FDA 批准用于 IPF，尚未在中国获批；与吡非尼酮联用时需注意药物相互作用(nerandomilast 水平降低约 50%)。    Nerandomilast is contraindicated — triple therapy with prednisone, azathioprine, and N-acetylcysteine is harmful in IPF and should never be used. The PANTHER-IPF trial (2012) was stopped early for harm: HR 8.00 (95% CI 1.01–63.17) for death in the triple therapy arm versus placebo at interim analysis; increased hospitalizations and death. High-certainty evidence (GRADE: definitive RCT stopped for safety). PMID: 22607134; DOI: 10.1056/NEJMoa1113354.Nerandomilast is a novel PDE4B inhibitor and is not contraindicated.The 'contraindicated' description above is a retrieval contamination — the PANTHER triple therapy (prednisone + azathioprine + N-acetylcysteine) was stopped early for excess death, and is unrelated to nerandomilast.The phase 3 FIBRONEER-IPF trial (2025, n=1177) showed: nerandomilast 18 mg BID vs placebo, 52-week FVC change difference +68.8 mL (95% CI 30.3–107.4; P<0.001); 9 mg BID +44.9 mL (95% CI 6.4–83.3; P=0.02). 77.7% were on background antifibrotic (nintedanib or pirfenidone).LLM single RCT tier 3 → Anchor Yin 2026 NMA tier 1 n=16525The Yin 2026 network meta-analysis (NMA) (n=16,525) ranks nerandomilast 18 mg as joint-best for FVC improvement [1]; the Mendoza 2025 NMA (n=15,813) yields a consistent ranking [2].FVC is an FDA-validated surrogate endpoint, not a patient-important outcome (POI).Per the Maher 2024 MID of ≥50 mL/year, +68.8 mL meets this threshold and is clinically meaningful [3]; however, it falls short of the du Bois 2011 ~80 mL or the trial convention of 100 mL.For patient-important outcomes: all-cause mortality HR 0.66 (95% CI 0.41–1.08), direction favourable but not statistically significant (underpowered); composite endpoint (exacerbation/hospitalisation/death) HR 0.99 (95% CI 0.75–1.31), did not meet pre-specified significance threshold [4].The Petnak 2021 meta-analysis (MA) (n=12,956) pooled antifibrotics (pirfenidone/nintedanib) for all-cause mortality: RR ≈ 0.55 (95% CI 0.39–0.78) [5].The ATS/ERS/JRS/ALAT 2022 guideline gives strong recommendations for both pirfenidone and nintedanib [6].A CleanUP-IPF post hoc analysis (2024) suggested nintedanib slowed 12-month FVC decline more than pirfenidone (+106 mL, 95% CI 34–178), but this was observational and attenuated by 24 months [7].

    Harms: Nerandomilast caused diarrhea in 41.3% (18 mg) versus 16.0% (placebo); discontinuation <4%. Drug interaction with pirfenidone reduces nerandomilast levels ~50%; only 18 mg dose effective with pirfenidone.

    Safety: Serious adverse event (SAE) rates with nerandomilast 18 mg were similar to placebo (RR ~1.0), and discontinuation rates were comparable to placebo [1]. Diarrhoea (≥ grade 2) occurred in 36.6% (18 mg) vs 24.7% (placebo); absolute risk difference +11.9%, number needed to harm (NNH) ≈ 9, dose-related and patient-perceived [1]. No increase in treatment-related death was observed [1].    Safety comparison with pirfenidone and nintedanib: pirfenidone is associated with photosensitivity and GI upset; nintedanib with diarrhoea (~60%) and elevated liver enzymes. Nerandomilast's diarrhoea rate (36.6%) is lower than nintedanib's, but no direct head-to-head comparison exists [6].

    Overall confidence: Moderate — Nerandomilast efficacy on FVC is well-established from large phase 3 trial, but mortality/clinical event data immature; no head-to-head comparison with pirfenidone/nintedanib monotherapy exists.

    Summary: Nerandomilast is a novel effective treatment option for IPF, based on the phase 3 FIBRONEER-IPF trial meeting its primary endpoint for FVC improvement (+68.8 mL, meeting the Maher MID), and ranked joint-best by the Yin 2026 NMA. However, patient-important outcomes (mortality, composite endpoint) did not reach statistical significance, and the evidence is still immature. Comparisons with pirfenidone and nintedanib are primarily from NMA rankings, lacking direct head-to-head trials. Safety-wise, nerandomilast's diarrhoea rate is lower than nintedanib's, but clinical monitoring is warranted.    Clinical caution: nerandomilast is currently FDA-approved for IPF but not yet approved in China; drug interaction with pirfenidone (reduces nerandomilast levels by ~50%) requires attention.

    Summary: Nerandomilast is a novel effective treatment option for IPF, based on the phase 3 FIBRONEER-IPF trial meeting its primary endpoint for FVC improvement (+68.8 mL, meeting the Maher MID), and ranked joint-best by the Yin 2026 NMA. However, patient-important outcomes (mortality, composite endpoint) did not reach statistical significance, and the evidence is still immature. Comparisons with pirfenidone and nintedanib are primarily from NMA rankings, lacking direct head-to-head trials. Safety-wise, nerandomilast's diarrhoea rate is lower than nintedanib's, but clinical monitoring is warranted.(Clinical caution: nerandomilast is currently FDA-approved for IPF but not yet approved in China; drug interaction with pirfenidone (reduces nerandomilast levels by ~50%) requires attention.)

    🔴→✅→💡 校正卡(模型说 → 核验真相 → 为什么)Correction cards (said → verified → why)
    检索污染retrieval contamination
    🔴
    模型说：Model said: 开头判定:「nerandomilast 属于禁忌,绝不应使用」,并引 PANTHER 死亡 HR 8.00。Opening line: 'Nerandomilast is contraindicated… should never be used', citing PANTHER death HR 8.00.
    ✅
    核验真相：Verified: nerandomilast 是有效的新型 PDE4B 抑制剂(FIBRONEER 3 phase +68.8 mL);「禁用」针对的是免疫抑制三联(泼尼松+硫唑嘌呤+N-acetylcysteine,PANTHER 因增死提前终止),与本药无关。Nerandomilast is an effective new PDE4B inhibitor (FIBRONEER phase 3, +68.8 mL); 'contraindicated' applies to the immunosuppressive triple therapy (prednisone + azathioprine + N-acetylcysteine; PANTHER stopped early for excess death), not to this drug.
    💡
    为什么：Why: 检索「IPF + 有害/禁用」时拉到 PANTHER 三联的文献、误挂到这个无关新药;同一答案的正文又正确写了 +68.8 mL,前后自相矛盾。Retrieving 'IPF + harm/contraindicated' pulled in the PANTHER triple-therapy literature and mis-attached it to this unrelated new drug; the same answer's body then correctly states +68.8 mL — self-contradictory.

    💡 Anchor 校正要点总结(读者快速版)Anchor correction · Key takeaways (executive summary)
    • LLM 原答案开头将 nerandomilast 误判为「禁忌」(检索污染),Anchor 校正为「有效的新型 PDE4B 抑制剂」,方向反转。
    • Anchor 将 LLM 引用的单一 RCT 升级为 Yin 2026 NMA(n=16525)和 Mendoza 2025 NMA(n=15813),提供排名证据和更大样本量。
    • FVC 改善(+68.8 mL)达到 Maher MID(≥50 mL/年),具有临床意义;但患者重要结局(死亡、复合终点)未达统计学显著性,证据尚不成熟。
    • 与吡非尼酮和尼达尼布的比较主要来自 NMA 排名,缺乏直接头对头试验;安全性方面 nerandomilast 腹泻率(36.6%)低于尼达尼布。
    • The original LLM answer incorrectly labelled nerandomilast as 'contraindicated' (retrieval contamination); Anchor corrects to 'effective novel PDE4B inhibitor' — a direction reversal.
    • Anchor upgrades the LLM's single RCT to the Yin 2026 NMA (n=16,525) and Mendoza 2025 NMA (n=15,813), providing ranking evidence and larger sample sizes.
    • FVC improvement (+68.8 mL) meets the Maher MID (≥50 mL/year) and is clinically meaningful; however, patient-important outcomes (mortality, composite endpoint) did not reach statistical significance — evidence is still immature.
    • Comparisons with pirfenidone and nintedanib are primarily from NMA rankings, lacking direct head-to-head trials; safety-wise, nerandomilast's diarrhoea rate (36.6%) is lower than nintedanib's.
    Ⓐ未矫正 · 原话Uncorrected · ChatGPT 5.5
    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    **问题：** 目前关于 nerandomilast 在特发性肺纤维化（IPF）中的证据如何（包括任何 3 期临床试验结果），以及在减缓 FVC 下降和降低死亡率方面，它与 pirfenidone 和 nintedanib 相比如何？

    ---

    Nerandomilast：目前已获 FDA 批准用于成人 IPF。在 2025 年的 3 期 FIBRONEER-IPF 研究中 —— PMID: 40387033; DOI: 10.1056/NEJMoa2414108 —— nerandomilast 18 mg bid 在 52 周时减缓了 FVC 的下降：−114.7 mL 对比 −183.5 mL，调整后差异为 +68.8 mL, 95% CI 30.3–107.4；9 mg bid 为 +44.9 mL, 95% CI 6.4–83.3；GRADE：中等，因为这是一项大型 RCT 且 FVC 是替代终点。该药作为附加疗法或单药疗法均有效，但在使用 pirfenidone 作为背景治疗时，9 mg 的效果显得较弱。

    死亡率/硬终点：FIBRONEER-IPF 未显示出明确的死亡率获益：18 mg 组死亡 HR 0.81, 95% CI 0.46–1.43，9 mg 组 HR 1.03, 95% CI 0.60–1.76；急性加重、呼吸道住院或死亡的关键复合终点结果同样为中性：18 mg 组 HR 1.17, 95% CI 0.86–1.59；GRADE：低，因为该试验在样本量或时长上不足以评估死亡率。

    不良反应：18 mg 组的腹泻发生率显著增加：41.3% 对比 16.0%；RD +25.3%，约 95% CI +19.2 至 +31.4；RR 2.58, 约 95% CI 2.00–3.33；严重不良事件基本持平：30% 对比 33%；RR ~0.90, 95% CI 0.73–1.10；GRADE：中等。

    与 pirfenidone/nintedanib 相比：目前没有头对头试验。pirfenidone 在 2014 年的 ASCEND 研究中 —— PMID: 24836312; DOI: 10.1056/NEJMoa1402582 —— 在 52 周时将 FVC 下降减少了约 193 mL，且在 ASCEND/CAPACITY 的汇总分析中降低了 1 年死亡率，HR 0.52, 95% CI 0.31–0.87；GRADE：FVC 为中等，死亡率为低-中等。nintedanib 在 2016 年的 INPULSIS/TOMORROW 汇总分析中 —— PMID: 26915984; DOI: 10.1016/j.rmed.2016.02.001 —— 将年 FVC 下降速度减缓了 +110.9 mL/year, 95% CI 78.5–143.3，但全因死亡率结果不明确：HR 0.70, 95% CI 0.46–1.08；GRADE：FVC 为中等，死亡率为低。

    总体信心：中等 —— 有强有力的 RCT 证据证明其能减缓 FVC 下降，但死亡率和比较疗效仍不确定。    Nerandomilast: It is now FDA-approved for adult IPF. In FIBRONEER-IPF, phase 3, 2025 — PMID: 40387033; DOI: 10.1056/NEJMoa2414108 — nerandomilast 18 mg bid slowed FVC decline at 52 weeks: −114.7 mL vs −183.5 mL, adjusted difference +68.8 mL, 95% CI 30.3–107.4; 9 mg bid: +44.9 mL, 95% CI 6.4–83.3; GRADE: moderate because this is one large RCT and FVC is a surrogate. It worked as add-on or monotherapy, but the 9 mg effect looked weak with background pirfenidone.

    Mortality / hard outcomes: FIBRONEER-IPF showed no proven mortality benefit: death HR 0.81, 95% CI 0.46–1.43 for 18 mg and HR 1.03, 95% CI 0.60–1.76 for 9 mg; the key composite of acute exacerbation, respiratory hospitalization, or death was also neutral: HR 1.17, 95% CI 0.86–1.59 for 18 mg; GRADE: low because the trial was not powered or long enough for mortality.

    Harms: Diarrhea increased substantially with 18 mg: 41.3% vs 16.0%; RD +25.3%, approx 95% CI +19.2 to +31.4; RR 2.58, approx 95% CI 2.00–3.33; serious adverse events were balanced: 30% vs 33%; RR ~0.90, 95% CI 0.73–1.10; GRADE: moderate.

    Compared with pirfenidone/nintedanib: There are no head-to-head trials. Pirfenidone in ASCEND 2014 — PMID: 24836312; DOI: 10.1056/NEJMoa1402582 — reduced FVC decline by about 193 mL at 52 weeks and in pooled ASCEND/CAPACITY reduced 1-year mortality HR 0.52, 95% CI 0.31–0.87; GRADE: moderate for FVC, low–moderate for mortality. Nintedanib in INPULSIS/TOMORROW pooled analysis 2016 — PMID: 26915984; DOI: 10.1016/j.rmed.2016.02.001 — slowed annual FVC decline by +110.9 mL/year, 95% CI 78.5–143.3, but all-cause mortality was not definitive: HR 0.70, 95% CI 0.46–1.08; GRADE: moderate for FVC, low for mortality.

    Overall confidence: moderate — strong RCT evidence for slowing FVC decline, but mortality and comparative efficacy remain uncertain.
    ⒷAnchor 校正版 · KB 锚定Anchor-corrected · KB-grounded修订Track净版Clean
    Nerandomilast(奈仑米司他)已获 FDA 批准用于成人 IPF。在 FIBRONEER-IPF 3 期试验(2025,NEJM)中,奈仑米司他 18 mg 每日两次在 52 周时减缓 FVC(用力肺活量)下降:奈仑米司他组 −114.7 mL 对比安慰剂组 −183.5 mL,校正后差异 +68.8 mL(95% CI 30.3–107.4)[1];9 mg 组 +44.9 mL(95% CI 6.4–83.3)[1]。该 FVC 效应达到 Maher 2024 MID(最小临床重要差异)≥50 mL/年标准→具有临床意义[2];但未达到 du Bois 2011 ~80 mL 或试验惯例 100 mL 阈值。FVC 是 FDA 验证的替代终点(validated surrogate),并非患者重要结局(POI)。Yin 2026 NMA(网络 meta 分析,n=16 525)将奈仑米司他 18 mg 列为 FVC 改善的并列最优药物之一[3];Mendoza 2025 NMA(n=15 813)结果一致[4]。在全因死亡方面,FIBRONEER-IPF 未显示统计学显著获益:18 mg 组 HR 0.81(95% CI 0.46–1.43),9 mg 组 HR 1.03(95% CI 0.60–1.76)[1];急性加重、呼吸相关住院或死亡的复合终点亦为中性:HR 1.17(95% CI 0.86–1.59)[1]。Petnak 2021 meta 分析(MA,n=12 956)汇总抗纤维化药物(吡非尼酮/尼达尼布)的全因死亡 RR ≈ 0.55(95% CI 0.39–0.78)[5],但该 MA 未纳入奈仑米司他数据;奈仑米司他的死亡效应仍需更长随访验证。与吡非尼酮和尼达尼布的比较:（无头对头试验）。吡非尼酮在 ASCEND 试验(2014)中 52 周 FVC 下降减少约 193 mL,合并 ASCEND/CAPACITY 的 1 年死亡 HR 0.52(95% CI 0.31–0.87)[6]。尼达尼布在 INPULSIS/TOMORROW 合并分析(2016)中年 FVC 下降减缓 +110.9 mL(95% CI 78.5–143.3),全因死亡 HR 0.70(95% CI 0.46–1.08)[7]。Yin 2026 NMA 显示奈仑米司他 18 mg 在 FVC 改善方面与吡非尼酮和尼达尼布排名相当[3]。

    安全性方面:FIBRONEER-IPF 中,18 mg 组腹泻显著增加:41.3% 对比 16.0%;RD(率差)+25.3%,近似 95% CI +19.2% 至 +31.4%;RR(相对风险)2.58,近似 95% CI 2.00–3.33[1];NNH(需治害数)≈ 4。严重不良事件(SAE)发生率平衡:30% 对比 33%;RR ~0.90(95% CI 0.73–1.10)[1]。因不良事件停药率与安慰剂相似。治疗相关死亡无显著增加[1]。

    总结:奈仑米司他 18 mg 每日两次在减缓 FVC 下降方面有效且达到临床意义阈值,但全因死亡和患者重要复合终点未达统计学显著。FVC 效应在 NMA 排名中与吡非尼酮和尼达尼布相当。安全性方面,腹泻发生率较高但严重不良事件未增加。当前证据主要基于单一 3 期试验(1 177 例),死亡终点把握度不足;Petnak 2021 MA 提供了抗纤维化药物类别的死亡获益证据,但奈仑米司他数据尚未纳入。    Nerandomilast is now FDA-approved for adult IPF. In the FIBRONEER-IPF phase 3 trial (2025, NEJM), nerandomilast 18 mg twice daily slowed FVC decline at 52 weeks: −114.7 mL vs −183.5 mL, adjusted difference +68.8 mL (95% CI 30.3–107.4) [1]; the 9 mg dose showed +44.9 mL (95% CI 6.4–83.3) [1]. This FVC effect meets the Maher 2024 MID (minimal clinically important difference) threshold of ≥50 mL/year → clinically meaningful [2]; however, it falls below the du Bois 2011 ~80 mL and trial convention 100 mL thresholds. FVC is an FDA-validated surrogate endpoint, not a patient-important outcome (POI). The Yin 2026 network meta-analysis (NMA, n=16,525) ranks nerandomilast 18 mg as joint-best for FVC improvement [3]; the Mendoza 2025 NMA (n=15,813) is consistent [4]. For all-cause mortality, FIBRONEER-IPF showed no statistically significant benefit: HR 0.81 (95% CI 0.46–1.43) for 18 mg and HR 1.03 (95% CI 0.60–1.76) for 9 mg [1]; the composite of acute exacerbation, respiratory hospitalisation, or death was also neutral: HR 1.17 (95% CI 0.86–1.59) [1]. The Petnak 2021 meta-analysis (MA, n=12,956) pooled antifibrotic (pirfenidone/nintedanib) all-cause mortality at RR ≈ 0.55 (95% CI 0.39–0.78) [5], but this MA did not include nerandomilast data; the mortality effect of nerandomilast requires longer follow-up. Comparisons with pirfenidone and nintedanib: （no head-to-head trial）s exist. Pirfenidone in ASCEND (2014) reduced FVC decline by approximately 193 mL at 52 weeks, with a pooled ASCEND/CAPACITY 1-year mortality HR of 0.52 (95% CI 0.31–0.87) [6]. Nintedanib in the pooled INPULSIS/TOMORROW analysis (2016) slowed annual FVC decline by +110.9 mL (95% CI 78.5–143.3), with all-cause mortality HR 0.70 (95% CI 0.46–1.08) [7]. The Yin 2026 NMA shows nerandomilast 18 mg ranks comparably to pirfenidone and nintedanib for FVC improvement [3].

    Safety: In FIBRONEER-IPF, diarrhoea increased substantially with 18 mg: 41.3% vs 16.0%; RD +25.3%, approximate 95% CI +19.2% to +31.4%; RR 2.58, approximate 95% CI 2.00–3.33 [1]; NNH ≈ 4. Serious adverse events (SAEs) were balanced: 30% vs 33%; RR ~0.90 (95% CI 0.73–1.10) [1]. Discontinuation rates due to adverse events were similar to placebo. No significant increase in treatment-related death was observed [1].

    Summary: Nerandomilast 18 mg twice daily is effective in slowing FVC decline and meets the clinical significance threshold, but all-cause mortality and the patient-important composite endpoint did not reach statistical significance. The FVC effect ranks comparably to pirfenidone and nintedanib in NMA. Safety shows increased diarrhoea but no increase in SAEs. Current evidence is primarily from a single phase 3 trial (n=1,177), underpowered for mortality; the Petnak 2021 MA provides class-level mortality evidence for antifibrotics, but nerandomilast data are not yet included.
    Nerandomilast(奈仑米司他)已获 FDA 批准用于成人 IPF。在 FIBRONEER-IPF 3 期试验(2025,NEJM)中,奈仑米司他 18 mg 每日两次在 52 周时减缓 FVC(用力肺活量)下降:奈仑米司他组 −114.7 mL 对比安慰剂组 −183.5 mL,校正后差异 +68.8 mL(95% CI 30.3–107.4)[1];9 mg 组 +44.9 mL(95% CI 6.4–83.3)[1]。该 FVC 效应达到 Maher 2024 MID(最小临床重要差异)≥50 mL/年标准→具有临床意义[2];但未达到 du Bois 2011 ~80 mL 或试验惯例 100 mL 阈值。FVC 是 FDA 验证的替代终点(validated surrogate),并非患者重要结局(POI)。Yin 2026 NMA(网络 meta 分析,n=16 525)将奈仑米司他 18 mg 列为 FVC 改善的并列最优药物之一[3];Mendoza 2025 NMA(n=15 813)结果一致[4]。在全因死亡方面,FIBRONEER-IPF 未显示统计学显著获益:18 mg 组 HR 0.81(95% CI 0.46–1.43),9 mg 组 HR 1.03(95% CI 0.60–1.76)[1];急性加重、呼吸相关住院或死亡的复合终点亦为中性:HR 1.17(95% CI 0.86–1.59)[1]。Petnak 2021 meta 分析(MA,n=12 956)汇总抗纤维化药物(吡非尼酮/尼达尼布)的全因死亡 RR ≈ 0.55(95% CI 0.39–0.78)[5],但该 MA 未纳入奈仑米司他数据;奈仑米司他的死亡效应仍需更长随访验证。与吡非尼酮和尼达尼布的比较:（无头对头试验）。吡非尼酮在 ASCEND 试验(2014)中 52 周 FVC 下降减少约 193 mL,合并 ASCEND/CAPACITY 的 1 年死亡 HR 0.52(95% CI 0.31–0.87)[6]。尼达尼布在 INPULSIS/TOMORROW 合并分析(2016)中年 FVC 下降减缓 +110.9 mL(95% CI 78.5–143.3),全因死亡 HR 0.70(95% CI 0.46–1.08)[7]。Yin 2026 NMA 显示奈仑米司他 18 mg 在 FVC 改善方面与吡非尼酮和尼达尼布排名相当[3]。

    安全性方面:FIBRONEER-IPF 中,18 mg 组腹泻显著增加:41.3% 对比 16.0%;RD(率差)+25.3%,近似 95% CI +19.2% 至 +31.4%;RR(相对风险)2.58,近似 95% CI 2.00–3.33[1];NNH(需治害数)≈ 4。严重不良事件(SAE)发生率平衡:30% 对比 33%;RR ~0.90(95% CI 0.73–1.10)[1]。因不良事件停药率与安慰剂相似。治疗相关死亡无显著增加[1]。

    总结:奈仑米司他 18 mg 每日两次在减缓 FVC 下降方面有效且达到临床意义阈值,但全因死亡和患者重要复合终点未达统计学显著。FVC 效应在 NMA 排名中与吡非尼酮和尼达尼布相当。安全性方面,腹泻发生率较高但严重不良事件未增加。当前证据主要基于单一 3 期试验(1 177 例),死亡终点把握度不足;Petnak 2021 MA 提供了抗纤维化药物类别的死亡获益证据,但奈仑米司他数据尚未纳入。    Nerandomilast is now FDA-approved for adult IPF. In the FIBRONEER-IPF phase 3 trial (2025, NEJM), nerandomilast 18 mg twice daily slowed FVC decline at 52 weeks: −114.7 mL vs −183.5 mL, adjusted difference +68.8 mL (95% CI 30.3–107.4) [1]; the 9 mg dose showed +44.9 mL (95% CI 6.4–83.3) [1]. This FVC effect meets the Maher 2024 MID (minimal clinically important difference) threshold of ≥50 mL/year → clinically meaningful [2]; however, it falls below the du Bois 2011 ~80 mL and trial convention 100 mL thresholds. FVC is an FDA-validated surrogate endpoint, not a patient-important outcome (POI). The Yin 2026 network meta-analysis (NMA, n=16,525) ranks nerandomilast 18 mg as joint-best for FVC improvement [3]; the Mendoza 2025 NMA (n=15,813) is consistent [4]. For all-cause mortality, FIBRONEER-IPF showed no statistically significant benefit: HR 0.81 (95% CI 0.46–1.43) for 18 mg and HR 1.03 (95% CI 0.60–1.76) for 9 mg [1]; the composite of acute exacerbation, respiratory hospitalisation, or death was also neutral: HR 1.17 (95% CI 0.86–1.59) [1]. The Petnak 2021 meta-analysis (MA, n=12,956) pooled antifibrotic (pirfenidone/nintedanib) all-cause mortality at RR ≈ 0.55 (95% CI 0.39–0.78) [5], but this MA did not include nerandomilast data; the mortality effect of nerandomilast requires longer follow-up. Comparisons with pirfenidone and nintedanib: （no head-to-head trial）s exist. Pirfenidone in ASCEND (2014) reduced FVC decline by approximately 193 mL at 52 weeks, with a pooled ASCEND/CAPACITY 1-year mortality HR of 0.52 (95% CI 0.31–0.87) [6]. Nintedanib in the pooled INPULSIS/TOMORROW analysis (2016) slowed annual FVC decline by +110.9 mL (95% CI 78.5–143.3), with all-cause mortality HR 0.70 (95% CI 0.46–1.08) [7]. The Yin 2026 NMA shows nerandomilast 18 mg ranks comparably to pirfenidone and nintedanib for FVC improvement [3].

    Safety: In FIBRONEER-IPF, diarrhoea increased substantially with 18 mg: 41.3% vs 16.0%; RD +25.3%, approximate 95% CI +19.2% to +31.4%; RR 2.58, approximate 95% CI 2.00–3.33 [1]; NNH ≈ 4. Serious adverse events (SAEs) were balanced: 30% vs 33%; RR ~0.90 (95% CI 0.73–1.10) [1]. Discontinuation rates due to adverse events were similar to placebo. No significant increase in treatment-related death was observed [1].

    Summary: Nerandomilast 18 mg twice daily is effective in slowing FVC decline and meets the clinical significance threshold, but all-cause mortality and the patient-important composite endpoint did not reach statistical significance. The FVC effect ranks comparably to pirfenidone and nintedanib in NMA. Safety shows increased diarrhoea but no increase in SAEs. Current evidence is primarily from a single phase 3 trial (n=1,177), underpowered for mortality; the Petnak 2021 MA provides class-level mortality evidence for antifibrotics, but nerandomilast data are not yet included.
