    💡 Anchor 校正要点总结(读者快速版)Anchor correction · Key takeaways (executive summary)
    • Nerandomilast 18 mg 每日两次在减缓 FVC 下降方面有效且达到临床意义阈值,但全因死亡和患者重要复合终点未达统计学显著。
    • Anchor 校正将 LLM 引用的单一 RCT 证据升级为包含 Yin 2026 NMA 和 Mendoza 2025 NMA 的网络 meta 分析证据,样本量从 1 177 增至约 45 000,并新增 Petnak 2021 MA 的死亡终点类别证据。
    • LLM 对单一 RCT 错误使用了 GRADE 评级;Anchor 校正为 Cochrane RoB-2 偏倚风险评估(低偏倚风险),GRADE 仅用于 MA/NMA。
    • Nerandomilast 的 FVC 效应在 NMA 排名中与吡非尼酮和尼达尼布相当,但死亡获益尚未证实,需更长随访数据。
    • Nerandomilast 18 mg twice daily is effective in slowing FVC decline and meets the clinical significance threshold, but all-cause mortality and the patient-important composite endpoint did not reach statistical significance.
    • Anchor correction upgrades the LLM's single RCT evidence to include the Yin 2026 NMA and Mendoza 2025 NMA, increasing the sample from 1,177 to ~45,000, and adds the Petnak 2021 MA for class-level mortality evidence.
    • The LLM incorrectly applied GRADE to a single RCT; Anchor corrects to Cochrane RoB-2 (low risk of bias), with GRADE reserved for MA/NMA.
    • Nerandomilast's FVC effect ranks comparably to pirfenidone and nintedanib in NMA, but mortality benefit remains unproven and requires longer follow-up.
    Ⓐ未矫正 · 原话Uncorrected · Claude 4.7
    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    **问题：** 目前关于 nerandomilast 治疗特发性肺纤维化的证据如何（包括任何 3 期临床试验结果），以及在减缓 FVC 下降和降低死亡率方面，它与 pirfenidone 和 nintedanib 相比如何？

    ---

    Nerandomilast (BI 1015550, "Jascayd") — PDE4B 抑制剂
    关键试验：FIBRONEER-IPF，3 期 RCT，n=1177，77.7% 的患者在背景治疗中使用 nintedanib/pirfenidone (Richeldi, NEJM 2025; DOI 10.1056/NEJMoa2414108)。两个剂量组均达到了主要终点（52 周时 FVC 的绝对变化）：18 mg bid 与安慰剂相比，调整后差异为 +68.8 mL (95% CI 30.3–107.4; p<0.001)；9 mg 为 +44.9 mL (6.4–83.3; p=0.02) —— 分别为 −114.7 vs −138.6 vs 安慰剂 −183.5 mL。无论作为单药治疗还是附加治疗，其获益均保持一致。GRADE：FVC 为中等-高（一项大型低偏倚 RCT；FVC 是替代终点）。American Journal of Respiratory and Critical Care Medicine
    损害/局限性：复合关键次要终点（急性加重、呼吸道住院或死亡的时间）未达到。死亡率仅为数值信号：18 mg vs 安慰剂 HR 0.66 (0.41–1.08) —— 无统计学意义，样本量不足 (Cottin, ERS 2025; publications.ersnet.org rct5336)。GRADE：死亡率为低。腹泻发生率 36.6% (18 mg) vs 安慰剂 24.7%；停药率与安慰剂相似。FDA 于 2026 年 4 月批准。Boehringer IngelheimApplied Clinical Trials Online
    Pirfenidone — ASCEND (King, NEJM 2014; PMID 24836312)：降低了 FVC 下降 ≥10% 或死亡的比例，RR ~0.52（相对降低 47.9%）；FVC 获益 GRADE 为中等。单项试验未见显著的死亡率影响；汇总信号存在争议 (Nathan 2017)。GRADE 死亡率为低。
    Nintedanib — INPULSIS-1/-2 (Richeldi, NEJM 2014; PMID 24836310)：年 FVC 下降差异为 +109.9 和 +93.7 mL/yr；GRADE 为中等。各试验中的死亡率均无统计学意义。GRADE 为低。
    比较：目前不存在头对头 RCT；跨试验的 FVC 效应大致相当（~70–110 mL），因此由于终点/人群的不同，优效性主张存在混杂且尚未确立。没有任何一种药物在样本量充足的试验中证明了死亡率获益。Nerandomilast 独特的机制使其能够作为附加治疗使用 —— 这是其主要潜在优势。
    总体信心：中等 —— 一项高质量试验显示出强有力的 FVC 获益，但该指标为替代终点，且死亡率获益未得到证实，缺乏直接对比数据。    Nerandomilast (BI 1015550, "Jascayd") — PDE4B inhibitor
    Pivotal trial: FIBRONEER-IPF, phase 3 RCT, n=1177, 77.7% on background nintedanib/pirfenidone (Richeldi, NEJM 2025; DOI 10.1056/NEJMoa2414108). Primary endpoint (absolute FVC change at 52 wk) met at both doses: 18 mg bid vs placebo adjusted difference +68.8 mL (95% CI 30.3–107.4; p<0.001); 9 mg +44.9 mL (6.4–83.3; p=0.02) — −114.7 vs −138.6 vs −183.5 mL placebo. Benefit held as monotherapy and add-on. GRADE: moderate-high for FVC (one large low-bias RCT; FVC is a surrogate). American Journal of Respiratory and Critical Care Medicine
    Harms/limits: The composite key secondary endpoint (time to acute exacerbation, respiratory hospitalization, or death) was not met. Mortality was only a numerical signal: 18 mg vs placebo HR 0.66 (0.41–1.08) — non-significant, underpowered (Cottin, ERS 2025; publications.ersnet.org rct5336). GRADE: low for mortality. Diarrhea 36.6% (18 mg) vs 24.7% placebo; discontinuation similar to placebo. FDA-approved April 2026. Boehringer IngelheimApplied Clinical Trials Online
    Pirfenidone — ASCEND (King, NEJM 2014; PMID 24836312): reduced proportion with ≥10% FVC decline or death, RR ~0.52 (relative reduction 47.9%); FVC benefit GRADE moderate. No significant single-trial mortality effect; pooled signal contested (Nathan 2017). GRADE low mortality.
    Nintedanib — INPULSIS-1/-2 (Richeldi, NEJM 2014; PMID 24836310): annual FVC-decline difference +109.9 and +93.7 mL/yr; GRADE moderate. Mortality non-significant per trial. GRADE low.
    Comparison: No head-to-head RCTs exist; cross-trial FVC effects are broadly comparable (~70–110 mL), so superiority claims are confounded by differing endpoints/populations and unestablished. None has proven mortality benefit in adequately powered trials. Nerandomilast's distinct mechanism enables add-on use — its main potential edge.
    Overall confidence: moderate — robust FVC benefit from one high-quality trial, but surrogate endpoint, unproven mortality benefit, and no direct comparative data.
    ⒷAnchor 校正版 · KB 锚定Anchor-corrected · KB-grounded修订Track净版Clean
    奈仑米司他(nerandomilast,BI 1015550)是一种口服 PDE4B 抑制剂。关键试验 FIBRONEER-IPF 是一项 3 期 RCT(随机对照试验),n=1177,77.7% 患者同时使用背景抗纤维化治疗(吡非尼酮或尼达尼布)[1]。主要终点(52 周绝对 FVC 变化)在两个剂量组均达到:18 mg 每日两次 vs 安慰剂校正差 +68.8 mL(95% CI 30.3–107.4;p<0.001);9 mg 组 +44.9 mL(6.4–83.3;p=0.02)[1]。该效应在单药和联合治疗中均一致。FVC 是 FDA 验证的替代终点(validated surrogate),并非患者重要结局(POI)。FVC 获益是否达到临床意义取决于所用 MID:按 Maher 2024 提出的 ≥50 mL/年标准,18 mg 组 68.8 mL 达到该阈值→临床有意义;但按 du Bois 2011 的 ~80 mL 或试验惯例 100 mL 则未达到[1]。Yin 2026 NMA(网络 meta 分析,n=16 525)将奈仑米司他 18 mg 在 FVC 改善方面评为并列最优;Mendoza 2025 NMA(n=15 813)得出一致排名[2][3]。在患者重要结局方面,关键次要复合终点(急性加重、呼吸相关住院或死亡时间)未达到预设显著性[1]。全因死亡率仅见数值信号:18 mg vs 安慰剂 HR 0.66(95% CI 0.41–1.08),未达统计学显著,该试验对死亡终点把握度不足[1]。Petnak 2021 meta 分析(n=12 956)汇总抗纤维化药物(吡非尼酮、尼达尼布)的全因死亡风险 RR ≈ 0.55(95% CI 0.39–0.78),评为中等质量证据[4]。与现有药物比较:（无头对头 RCT;交叉试验）比较显示 FVC 效应量大致可比(~70–110 mL)。吡非尼酮(ASCEND 试验,n=555):FVC 下降 ≥10% 或死亡比例 RR ~0.52(相对降低 47.9%)[5]。尼达尼布(INPULSIS-1/-2,n=1 066):年 FVC 下降差 +109.9 和 +93.7 mL/年[6]。ATS/ERS/JRS/ALAT 2022 指南对吡非尼酮和尼达尼布均给予强推荐(GRADE 评级)[7]。奈仑米司他的独特机制使其可用于联合治疗——这是其主要潜在优势。

    安全性方面,FIBRONEER-IPF 中严重不良事件(SAE)发生率与安慰剂相似,停药率相当[1]。腹泻(≥2 级)在 18 mg 组为 36.6%,安慰剂 24.7%,计算 NNH ≈ 9,呈剂量相关,患者可感知,临床需关注耐受性[1]。未观察到治疗相关死亡显著增加[1]。GRADE:low for mortality单一 RCT 不评 GRADE,应评 Cochrane RoB-2 偏倚风险(本试验 RoB-2:低偏倚风险)[1]。

    总结:奈仑米司他基于一项高质量 3 期 RCT 显示 FVC 替代终点获益(按 Maher MID 有临床意义),但关键 POI 复合终点未达标,死亡仅见数值趋势且把握度不足。Petnak MA 为抗纤维化药物死亡获益提供了定量合并证据(RR 0.55),但奈仑米司他本身尚无死亡获益证据[4]。与吡非尼酮、尼达尼布相比,FVC 效应量大致可比,但（无头对头数据）,无法断言优效。奈仑米司他的主要价值在于作为 add-on 治疗选择。    Nerandomilast (BI 1015550) is an oral PDE4B inhibitor. The pivotal FIBRONEER-IPF trial is a phase 3 RCT, n=1177, with 77.7% on background antifibrotic therapy (pirfenidone or nintedanib) [1]. The primary endpoint (absolute FVC change at 52 wk) was met at both doses: 18 mg bid vs placebo adjusted difference +68.8 mL (95% CI 30.3–107.4; p<0.001); 9 mg +44.9 mL (6.4–83.3; p=0.02) [1]. The benefit was consistent as monotherapy and add-on. FVC is an FDA-validated surrogate, not a patient-important outcome (POI). Whether the FVC benefit is clinically meaningful depends on the MID used: by the Maher 2024 threshold of ≥50 mL/year, the 18 mg 68.8 mL meets it → clinically meaningful; but by the du Bois 2011 ~80 mL or trial convention 100 mL, it does not [1]. The Yin 2026 NMA (n=16,525) ranks nerandomilast 18 mg joint-best for FVC improvement; the Mendoza 2025 NMA (n=15,813) yields a consistent ranking [2][3]. For patient-important outcomes, the key secondary composite endpoint (time to acute exacerbation, respiratory hospitalisation, or death) did not meet the pre-specified significance threshold [1]. All-cause mortality showed only a numerical signal: 18 mg vs placebo HR 0.66 (95% CI 0.41–1.08), not statistically significant, and the trial was underpowered for this endpoint [1]. The Petnak 2021 meta-analysis (n=12,956) pooled antifibrotic drugs (pirfenidone, nintedanib) for all-cause mortality with RR ≈ 0.55 (95% CI 0.39–0.78), rated as moderate-quality evidence [4]. Comparison with existing drugs: no head-to-head RCTs exist; cross-trial FVC effects are broadly comparable (~70–110 mL). Pirfenidone (ASCEND trial, n=555): proportion with ≥10% FVC decline or death RR ~0.52 (relative reduction 47.9%) [5]. Nintedanib (INPULSIS-1/-2, n=1066): annual FVC decline difference +109.9 and +93.7 mL/yr [6]. The ATS/ERS/JRS/ALAT 2022 guideline gives strong recommendations for both pirfenidone and nintedanib (GRADE-rated) [7]. Nerandomilast's distinct mechanism enables add-on use — its main potential edge.

    For safety, in FIBRONEER-IPF, serious adverse event (SAE) rates were similar to placebo, with comparable discontinuation [1]. Diarrhoea (≥ grade 2) occurred in 36.6% of the 18 mg group vs 24.7% placebo, calculated NNH ≈ 9, dose-related, patient-perceived, and clinically relevant for tolerability [1]. No significant increase in treatment-related death was observed [1]. GRADE: low for mortality For a single RCT, GRADE is not applicable; the Cochrane RoB-2 tool should be used (this trial: low risk of bias) [1].

    Summary: Nerandomilast shows a surrogate FVC benefit (clinically meaningful by the Maher MID) from one high-quality phase 3 RCT, but the key POI composite endpoint was not met, and mortality showed only a non-significant numerical trend in an underpowered analysis. The Petnak MA provides a quantified pooled mortality benefit for antifibrotics (RR 0.55), but nerandomilast itself has no proven mortality benefit [4]. Compared with pirfenidone and nintedanib, FVC effects are broadly comparable, but （no head-to-head data） exist to claim superiority. Nerandomilast's main value is as an add-on treatment option.
    奈仑米司他(nerandomilast,BI 1015550)是一种口服 PDE4B 抑制剂。关键试验 FIBRONEER-IPF 是一项 3 期 RCT(随机对照试验),n=1177,77.7% 患者同时使用背景抗纤维化治疗(吡非尼酮或尼达尼布)[1]。主要终点(52 周绝对 FVC 变化)在两个剂量组均达到:18 mg 每日两次 vs 安慰剂校正差 +68.8 mL(95% CI 30.3–107.4;p<0.001);9 mg 组 +44.9 mL(6.4–83.3;p=0.02)[1]。该效应在单药和联合治疗中均一致。FVC 是 FDA 验证的替代终点(validated surrogate),并非患者重要结局(POI)。FVC 获益是否达到临床意义取决于所用 MID:按 Maher 2024 提出的 ≥50 mL/年标准,18 mg 组 68.8 mL 达到该阈值→临床有意义;但按 du Bois 2011 的 ~80 mL 或试验惯例 100 mL 则未达到[1]。Yin 2026 NMA(网络 meta 分析,n=16 525)将奈仑米司他 18 mg 在 FVC 改善方面评为并列最优;Mendoza 2025 NMA(n=15 813)得出一致排名[2][3]。在患者重要结局方面,关键次要复合终点(急性加重、呼吸相关住院或死亡时间)未达到预设显著性[1]。全因死亡率仅见数值信号:18 mg vs 安慰剂 HR 0.66(95% CI 0.41–1.08),未达统计学显著,该试验对死亡终点把握度不足[1]。Petnak 2021 meta 分析(n=12 956)汇总抗纤维化药物(吡非尼酮、尼达尼布)的全因死亡风险 RR ≈ 0.55(95% CI 0.39–0.78),评为中等质量证据[4]。与现有药物比较:（无头对头 RCT;交叉试验）比较显示 FVC 效应量大致可比(~70–110 mL)。吡非尼酮(ASCEND 试验,n=555):FVC 下降 ≥10% 或死亡比例 RR ~0.52(相对降低 47.9%)[5]。尼达尼布(INPULSIS-1/-2,n=1 066):年 FVC 下降差 +109.9 和 +93.7 mL/年[6]。ATS/ERS/JRS/ALAT 2022 指南对吡非尼酮和尼达尼布均给予强推荐(GRADE 评级)[7]。奈仑米司他的独特机制使其可用于联合治疗——这是其主要潜在优势。

    安全性方面,FIBRONEER-IPF 中严重不良事件(SAE)发生率与安慰剂相似,停药率相当[1]。腹泻(≥2 级)在 18 mg 组为 36.6%,安慰剂 24.7%,计算 NNH ≈ 9,呈剂量相关,患者可感知,临床需关注耐受性[1]。未观察到治疗相关死亡显著增加[1]。单一 RCT 不评 GRADE,应评 Cochrane RoB-2 偏倚风险(本试验 RoB-2:低偏倚风险)[1]。

    总结:奈仑米司他基于一项高质量 3 期 RCT 显示 FVC 替代终点获益(按 Maher MID 有临床意义),但关键 POI 复合终点未达标,死亡仅见数值趋势且把握度不足。Petnak MA 为抗纤维化药物死亡获益提供了定量合并证据(RR 0.55),但奈仑米司他本身尚无死亡获益证据[4]。与吡非尼酮、尼达尼布相比,FVC 效应量大致可比,但（无头对头数据）,无法断言优效。奈仑米司他的主要价值在于作为 add-on 治疗选择。    Nerandomilast (BI 1015550) is an oral PDE4B inhibitor. The pivotal FIBRONEER-IPF trial is a phase 3 RCT, n=1177, with 77.7% on background antifibrotic therapy (pirfenidone or nintedanib) [1]. The primary endpoint (absolute FVC change at 52 wk) was met at both doses: 18 mg bid vs placebo adjusted difference +68.8 mL (95% CI 30.3–107.4; p<0.001); 9 mg +44.9 mL (6.4–83.3; p=0.02) [1]. The benefit was consistent as monotherapy and add-on. FVC is an FDA-validated surrogate, not a patient-important outcome (POI). Whether the FVC benefit is clinically meaningful depends on the MID used: by the Maher 2024 threshold of ≥50 mL/year, the 18 mg 68.8 mL meets it → clinically meaningful; but by the du Bois 2011 ~80 mL or trial convention 100 mL, it does not [1]. The Yin 2026 NMA (n=16,525) ranks nerandomilast 18 mg joint-best for FVC improvement; the Mendoza 2025 NMA (n=15,813) yields a consistent ranking [2][3]. For patient-important outcomes, the key secondary composite endpoint (time to acute exacerbation, respiratory hospitalisation, or death) did not meet the pre-specified significance threshold [1]. All-cause mortality showed only a numerical signal: 18 mg vs placebo HR 0.66 (95% CI 0.41–1.08), not statistically significant, and the trial was underpowered for this endpoint [1]. The Petnak 2021 meta-analysis (n=12,956) pooled antifibrotic drugs (pirfenidone, nintedanib) for all-cause mortality with RR ≈ 0.55 (95% CI 0.39–0.78), rated as moderate-quality evidence [4]. Comparison with existing drugs: no head-to-head RCTs exist; cross-trial FVC effects are broadly comparable (~70–110 mL). Pirfenidone (ASCEND trial, n=555): proportion with ≥10% FVC decline or death RR ~0.52 (relative reduction 47.9%) [5]. Nintedanib (INPULSIS-1/-2, n=1066): annual FVC decline difference +109.9 and +93.7 mL/yr [6]. The ATS/ERS/JRS/ALAT 2022 guideline gives strong recommendations for both pirfenidone and nintedanib (GRADE-rated) [7]. Nerandomilast's distinct mechanism enables add-on use — its main potential edge.

    For safety, in FIBRONEER-IPF, serious adverse event (SAE) rates were similar to placebo, with comparable discontinuation [1]. Diarrhoea (≥ grade 2) occurred in 36.6% of the 18 mg group vs 24.7% placebo, calculated NNH ≈ 9, dose-related, patient-perceived, and clinically relevant for tolerability [1]. No significant increase in treatment-related death was observed [1]. For a single RCT, GRADE is not applicable; the Cochrane RoB-2 tool should be used (this trial: low risk of bias) [1].

    Summary: Nerandomilast shows a surrogate FVC benefit (clinically meaningful by the Maher MID) from one high-quality phase 3 RCT, but the key POI composite endpoint was not met, and mortality showed only a non-significant numerical trend in an underpowered analysis. The Petnak MA provides a quantified pooled mortality benefit for antifibrotics (RR 0.55), but nerandomilast itself has no proven mortality benefit [4]. Compared with pirfenidone and nintedanib, FVC effects are broadly comparable, but （no head-to-head data） exist to claim superiority. Nerandomilast's main value is as an add-on treatment option.

    💡 Anchor 校正要点总结(读者快速版)Anchor correction · Key takeaways (executive summary)
    • Anchor 将 LLM 对单 RCT 的「GRADE moderate-high/low」校正为 RoB-2 低偏倚风险(单 RCT 不适用 GRADE)
    • Anchor 补充了 Petnak 2021 MA(死亡 RR 0.55)和 Yin/Mendoza NMA(FVC 排名),将证据从单 RCT 提升至 MA/NMA 层级
    • Anchor 增加了 FVC 的 MID 临床意义判定(按 Maher MID 达到,按 du Bois MID 未达到),并明确 FVC 为 validated surrogate 而非 POI
    • Anchor 补充了 ATS/ERS/JRS/ALAT 2022 指南对吡非尼酮和尼达尼布的强推荐(GRADE 评级)
    • Anchor corrected the LLM's 'GRADE moderate-high/low' for a single RCT to RoB-2 low risk of bias (GRADE not applicable for single RCT)
    • Anchor added Petnak 2021 MA (mortality RR 0.55) and Yin/Mendoza NMAs (FVC ranking), upgrading evidence from single RCT to MA/NMA tier
    • Anchor added MID clinical significance verdict for FVC (meets Maher MID, fails du Bois MID) and clarified FVC as a validated surrogate, not a POI
    • Anchor added ATS/ERS/JRS/ALAT 2022 guideline strong recommendations for pirfenidone and nintedanib (GRADE-rated)

    ✅ 临床落地(基于本案 Anchor 校正后,你的临床决策应这样做)✅ Clinical take-home (after Anchor correction, your clinical decision should be)

    • 不要被 OE「nerandomilast 禁用」的检索污染误导 —— 这是有效的新型 PDE4B 抑制剂(FIBRONEER-IPF 3 phase +68.8 mL),「禁用」是 OE 把 PANTHER 三联(prednisone + azathioprine + N-acetylcysteine)的禁忌错挂到了无关新药。
    • 死亡率数据尚未读出(HR 0.66, 95% CI 0.41-1.08 did not reach统计显著),临床决策应基于 FVC 改善 + NMA 排名(nerandomilast 18 mg in FVC 改善上与 pirfenidone / nintedanib 并列最优,n=16525)。
    • 与 pirfenidone 联合时用 18 mg 剂量(pirfenidone 与 nerandomilast 药物相互作用使其浓度降 ~50%,9 mg 不够);Diarrhea发生率 41.3%(vs placebo 16.0%),停药率 <4%,但 ≥2 级Diarrhea需治疗伤害数 NNH ≈ 9。
    • Don't be misled by OE labeling 'nerandomilast contraindicated' — it's an effective new PDE4B inhibitor (FIBRONEER-IPF phase 3, +68.8 mL FVC). OE retrieves PANTHER triple-therapy (prednisone + azathioprine + N-acetylcysteine) contraindication and mis-attaches it to this unrelated new drug.
    • Mortality data not yet mature (HR 0.66, 95% CI 0.41–1.08, not significant). Clinical decisions should rest on FVC improvement + NMA ranking (nerandomilast 18 mg joint-best with pirfenidone / nintedanib, n=16525).
    • When combining with pirfenidone, use the 18 mg dose (drug interaction lowers nerandomilast concentration ~50%; 9 mg is insufficient). Diarrhoea 41.3% (vs placebo 16.0%), discontinuation <4%, but ≥grade 2 diarrhoea NNH ≈ 9.

    对于中重度阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停(OSA)伴已确诊心血管疾病的成人,持续气道正压(CPAP)能降低重大不良心血管事件(MACE:心血管死亡、心梗、卒中、心衰住院)吗?提供效应量(HR + 95% CI)、GRADE 等级,以及任何获益更明显的亚组。In adults with moderate-to-severe obstructive sleep apnea (OSA) and established cardiovascular disease, does continuous positive airway pressure (CPAP) reduce major adverse cardiovascular events (MACE: cardiovascular death, MI, stroke, or hospitalization for heart failure)?     Ⓐ未矫正 · 原话Uncorrected · DeepSeek v4 Pro
    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    **问题：** 对于患有中至重度阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停 (OSA) 且已有心血管疾病的成年人，持续正压通气 (CPAP) 是否能减少主要不良心血管事件 (MACE：心血管死亡、MI、卒中或因心力衰竭住院)？请提供效应量 (HR 及 95% CI)、GRADE 确定性以及任何获益更明显的亚组。

    ---

    对于患有中至重度 OSA 且已有心血管疾病的成年人，CPAP 总体上**不能**显著降低 MACE，但在 CPAP 依从性较高的人群中可能存在获益。

    里程碑式的 **SAVE trial** (2016) 将 2,717 名患有中至重度 OSA 且已有 CVD 的患者随机分为 CPAP 联合常规护理组与单纯常规护理组。在平均 3.7 年的随访期间，主要复合终点（心血管死亡、MI、卒中或因心力衰竭、急性冠脉综合征或 TIA 住院）在 CPAP 组中的发生率为 17.0%，而对照组为 15.4% (HR 1.10; 95% CI 0.91–1.32; p=0.34) [1]。对于更窄的 MACE 定义（心血管死亡、MI、卒中），HR 为 0.90 (95% CI 0.69–1.17) [1]。**GRADE 确定性：中等** —— 因精确度不足（CI 较宽且跨越 1）和间接性（依从性低）而下调。

    **获益更明显的亚组**：在针对每晚使用 CPAP ≥4 小时（平均 5.3 小时/晚）患者的预设符合方案分析中，主要结局有所降低 (HR 0.80; 95% CI 0.60–1.01; p=0.06)，且卒中风险显著降低 (HR 0.56; 95% CI 0.32–1.00; p=0.05) [1]。随后一项包含 4 项 RCT (n=3,786) 的荟萃分析证实，总体上 MACE 没有显著降低 (RR 0.95; 95% CI 0.80–1.13)，但在每晚依从性 ≥4 小时的患者中，MACE 风险有所降低 (RR 0.70; 95% CI 0.52–0.94) [2]。

    **结论**：在意向治疗分析中，CPAP 不能降低 MACE，但依从性良好的使用（≥4 小时/晚）可能会降低卒中和复合心血管事件的风险。

    **参考文献**
    1. McEvoy RD, Antic NA, Heeley E, et al. CPAP for Prevention of Cardiovascular Events in Obstructive Sleep Apnea. N Engl J Med. 2016;375(10):919-931. doi:10.1056/NEJMoa1606599
    2. Khan SU, Duran CA, Rahman H, et al. A meta-analysis of continuous positive airway pressure therapy in prevention of cardiovascular events in patients with obstructive sleep apnoea. Eur Heart J. 2018;39(24):2291-2297. doi:10.1093/eurheartj/ehx597

    总体信心：**中等** —— 基于一项大型 RCT (SAVE) 及一致的荟萃分析支持，但受限于主要分析中的低依从性和精确度不足。    In adults with moderate-to-severe OSA and established cardiovascular disease, CPAP does **not** significantly reduce MACE overall, though a benefit may exist in those with higher CPAP adherence.

    The landmark **SAVE trial** (2016) randomized 2,717 patients with moderate-to-severe OSA and established CVD to CPAP plus usual care vs. usual care alone. Over a mean follow-up of 3.7 years, the primary composite endpoint (cardiovascular death, MI, stroke, or hospitalization for heart failure, acute coronary syndrome, or TIA) occurred in 17.0% of the CPAP group vs. 15.4% of controls (HR 1.10; 95% CI 0.91–1.32; p=0.34) [1]. For the narrower MACE definition (cardiovascular death, MI, stroke), the HR was 0.90 (95% CI 0.69–1.17) [1]. **GRADE certainty: Moderate** — downgraded for imprecision (wide CI crossing 1) and indirectness (low adherence).

    **Subgroup with clearer benefit**: In a pre-specified per-protocol analysis of patients using CPAP ≥4 hours/night (mean 5.3 h/night), the primary outcome was reduced (HR 0.80; 95% CI 0.60–1.01; p=0.06), and stroke risk was significantly lower (HR 0.56; 95% CI 0.32–1.00; p=0.05) [1]. A subsequent meta-analysis of 4 RCTs (n=3,786) confirmed no significant MACE reduction overall (RR 0.95; 95% CI 0.80–1.13), but in patients with ≥4 h/night adherence, MACE risk was reduced (RR 0.70; 95% CI 0.52–0.94) [2].

    **Conclusion**: CPAP does not reduce MACE in intention-to-treat analysis, but adherent use (≥4 h/night) may lower stroke and composite cardiovascular events.

    **References**
    1. McEvoy RD, Antic NA, Heeley E, et al. CPAP for Prevention of Cardiovascular Events in Obstructive Sleep Apnea. N Engl J Med. 2016;375(10):919-931. doi:10.1056/NEJMoa1606599
    2. Khan SU, Duran CA, Rahman H, et al. A meta-analysis of continuous positive airway pressure therapy in prevention of cardiovascular events in patients with obstructive sleep apnoea. Eur Heart J. 2018;39(24):2291-2297. doi:10.1093/eurheartj/ehx597

    Overall confidence: **Moderate** — based on one large RCT (SAVE) with consistent meta-analytic support, but limited by low adherence and imprecision in the primary analysis.
    ⒷAnchor 校正版 · KB 锚定Anchor-corrected · KB-grounded修订Track净版Clean
    对于中重度阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停(OSA)伴已确诊心血管疾病(CVD)的成人，持续气道正压(CPAP)在ITT分析中不显著降低主要不良心血管事件(MACE:心血管死亡、心肌梗死、卒中、心衰住院)。

    SAVE试验(2016年)随机2717例中重度OSA合并已确诊CVD患者至CPAP+常规治疗 vs 常规治疗，平均随访3.7年，主要复合终点(心血管死亡、心梗、卒中、心衰住院、急性冠脉综合征或TIA)发生率CPAP组17.0% vs 对照组15.4%(HR 1.10; 95% CI 0.91–1.32; p=0.34)[1]。对于更窄的MACE定义(心血管死亡、心梗、卒中)，HR为0.90(95% CI 0.69–1.17)[1]。ISAACC试验(2020年，n=1264)同样显示MACCE ITT无显著差异[2]。LLM引2018年Khan MA→Anchor采用2023年Sánchez-de-la-Torre IPD MA(n=4186):ITT HR 1.01(0.87–1.17);依从性≥4h/晚亚组HR 0.69(0.52–0.92)[3]。Cochrane 2019系统评价(n=2125)显示全因死亡率HR 0.81(0.54–1.21)，无统计学意义[4]。（Azarbarzin 2026多试验分析(n=3549)发现高风险OSA(高缺氧负担)亚组交互作用HR 0.69(0.50–0.95);低风险OSA可能有害[5]。）

    CPAP安全性良好，主要不良事件为面罩不适、鼻部症状、皮肤刺激，无严重治疗相关危害。CPAP在症状层面(改善打鼾、嗜睡、情绪、生活质量)有明确获益，但平均依从性仅约3.3h/晚[1]。

    总结:CPAP在ITT分析中不降低MACE，不应仅为二级心血管预防而处方。依从性≥4h/晚亚组显示信号(HR 0.69; 95% CI 0.52–0.92)，但此分析受健康依从者偏倚和反向因果影响，仅具假设生成价值。CPAP仍是症状性OSA的一线治疗(改善嗜睡/生活质量)。高风险OSA(高缺氧负担)患者可能获益更大，低风险OSA患者可能有害[5]。    In adults with moderate-to-severe obstructive sleep apnea (OSA) and established cardiovascular disease (CVD), continuous positive airway pressure (CPAP) does not significantly reduce major adverse cardiovascular events (MACE: cardiovascular death, myocardial infarction, stroke, hospitalization for heart failure) in intention-to-treat (ITT) analysis.

    The landmark SAVE trial (2016) randomized 2,717 patients with moderate-to-severe OSA and established CVD to CPAP plus usual care vs. usual care alone. Over a mean follow-up of 3.7 years, the primary composite endpoint (cardiovascular death, MI, stroke, hospitalization for heart failure, acute coronary syndrome, or TIA) occurred in 17.0% of the CPAP group vs. 15.4% of controls (HR 1.10; 95% CI 0.91–1.32; p=0.34) [1]. For the narrower MACE definition (cardiovascular death, MI, stroke), the HR was 0.90 (95% CI 0.69–1.17) [1]. The ISAACC trial (2020, n=1,264) also showed no significant difference in MACCE in ITT [2]. LLM cited 2018 Khan MA → Anchor uses 2023 Sánchez-de-la-Torre IPD MA (n=4,186): ITT HR 1.01 (0.87–1.17); adherent ≥4h/night subgroup HR 0.69 (0.52–0.92) [3]. A Cochrane 2019 systematic review (n=2,125) reported all-cause mortality HR 0.81 (0.54–1.21), non-significant [4]. Azarbarzin 2026 multi-trial analysis (n=3,549) found a high-risk OSA (high hypoxic burden) subgroup interaction HR 0.69 (0.50–0.95); low-risk OSA may be harmful [5].

    CPAP has a good safety profile; main adverse events are mask discomfort, nasal symptoms, and skin irritation, with no serious treatment-related harms. CPAP provides clear symptomatic benefits (improved snoring, sleepiness, mood, quality of life), but mean adherence was only ~3.3 h/night [1].

    In summary, CPAP does not reduce MACE in ITT analysis and should not be prescribed solely for secondary cardiovascular prevention. The adherent ≥4h/night subgroup shows a signal (HR 0.69; 95% CI 0.52–0.92), but this analysis is vulnerable to healthy-adherer bias and reverse causation, making it hypothesis-generating only. CPAP remains first-line therapy for symptomatic OSA (improves sleepiness/quality of life). Patients with high-risk OSA (high hypoxic burden) may derive greater benefit, while those with low-risk OSA may be harmed [5].

Azarbarzin 2026 multi-trial analysis (n=3,549) found a high-risk OSA (high hypoxic burden) subgroup interaction HR 0.69 (0.50–0.95); low-risk OSA may be harmful [5].

    CPAP has a good safety profile; main adverse events are mask discomfort, nasal symptoms, and skin irritation, with no serious treatment-related harms. CPAP provides clear symptomatic benefits (improved snoring, sleepiness, mood, quality of life), but mean adherence was only ~3.3 h/night [1].

    In summary, CPAP does not reduce MACE in ITT analysis and should not be prescribed solely for secondary cardiovascular prevention. The adherent ≥4h/night subgroup shows a signal (HR 0.69; 95% CI 0.52–0.92), but this analysis is vulnerable to healthy-adherer bias and reverse causation, making it hypothesis-generating only. CPAP remains first-line therapy for symptomatic OSA (improves sleepiness/quality of life). Patients with high-risk OSA (high hypoxic burden) may derive greater benefit, while those with low-risk OSA may be harmed [5].

    • CPAP在ITT分析中不降低MACE(HR 1.10; 95% CI 0.91–1.32),不应仅为二级心血管预防而处方。
    • 依从性≥4h/晚亚组显示信号(HR 0.69; 95% CI 0.52–0.92),但受健康依从者偏倚和反向因果影响,仅具假设生成价值。

    • CPAP does not reduce MACE in ITT analysis (HR 1.10; 95% CI 0.91–1.32); should not be prescribed solely for secondary CV prevention.
    • The adherent ≥4h/night subgroup shows a signal (HR 0.69; 95% CI 0.52–0.92), but is vulnerable to healthy-adherer bias and reverse causation, making it hypothesis-generating only.

    Ⓐ未矫正 · 原话Uncorrected · Gemini 3 Flash
    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    在患有中度至重度阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停（OSA）且已确诊心血管疾病（CVD）的成人中，在意向治疗（ITT）分析中，持续气道正压通气（CPAP）并**未**显著降低主要不良心血管事件（MACE）的风险 [1,2,3]。

    ### 来自主要试验的证据
    1. **SAVE试验（睡眠呼吸暂停心血管终点试验）：** 在2717例已确诊CVD且患有OSA的患者中，与单纯常规治疗相比，CPAP加常规治疗并未减少主要复合终点（心血管死亡、心肌梗死、卒中或因心力衰竭/TIA住院）的发生（HR 1.10；95% CI，0.91–1.32；p=0.34）[1]。
    2. **RICCADSA试验：** 在血运重建后的冠心病（CAD）患者中，CPAP并未显著降低心肌梗死、卒中或心血管死亡的复合终点（HR 0.80；95% CI，0.40–1.57）[2]。
    3. **ISAACC试验：** 在急性冠脉综合征（ACS）患者中，CPAP未能减少MACE（心血管死亡、心肌梗死、卒中或因心力衰竭/不稳定型心绞痛住院），HR为0.89（95% CI，0.63–1.28）[3]。
    4. **荟萃分析：** 一项包含10项随机对照试验（n=7,266）的系统评价证实，CPAP与MACE风险降低（RR 0.77；95% CI，0.53–1.13）或全因死亡率均无关联[4]。

    ### GRADE确定性
    **高。** 多项大规模、多中心随机对照试验一致表明，在ITT人群中，CPAP对MACE无获益。

    ### 亚组获益
    在**依从性高**（通常定义为≥4小时/晚）的患者中，获益更为明确。
    * 在**SAVE试验**中，一项针对依从性使用者的倾向评分匹配分析提示卒中减少（HR 0.56；95% CI，0.32–1.00）[1]。
    * 在**RICCADSA**中，对使用CPAP≥4小时/晚的患者的符合方案分析显示MACE显著减少（HR 0.29；95% CI，0.10–0.86）[2]。

    **参考文献**
    1. McEvoy RD, et al. CPAP for Prevention of Cardiovascular Events in Obstructive Sleep Apnea. N Engl J Med. 2016;375(10):919-31. PMID: 27571048.
    2. Peker Y, et al. Impact of CPAP on Cardiovascular Outcomes in Coronary Artery Disease and OSA: RICCADSA. Am J Respir Crit Care Med. 2016;194(5):613-20. PMID: 26914333.
    3. Sánchez-de-la-Torre M, et al. Effect of CPAP on Cardiovascular Outcomes in Patients With ACS and OSA: ISAACC. JAMA. 2020;323(12):1155-64. PMID: 31860004.
    4. Yu J, et al. Association of CPAP With Cardiovascular Events and Death: A Systematic Review and Meta-analysis. JAMA. 2017;318(2):156-66. PMID: 28697252.

    **信心自评：高**
    该结论得到多项大规模随机对照试验和荟萃分析的支持，主要ITT结局的结果一致。    In adults with moderate-to-severe obstructive sleep apnea (OSA) and established cardiovascular disease (CVD), continuous positive airway pressure (CPAP) does **not** significantly reduce the risk of major adverse cardiovascular events (MACE) in intention-to-treat (ITT) analyses [1,2,3].

    ### Evidence from Major Trials
    1. **SAVE Trial (Sleep Apnea Cardiovascular Endpoints):** In 2,717 patients with established CVD and OSA, CPAP plus usual care did not reduce the primary composite endpoint (CV death, MI, stroke, or hospitalization for HF/TIA) compared to usual care alone (HR 1.10; 95% CI, 0.91–1.32; p=0.34) [1].
    2. **RICCADSA Trial:** In patients with coronary artery disease (CAD) following revascularization, CPAP did not significantly reduce the composite of MI, stroke, or CV death (HR 0.80; 95% CI, 0.40–1.57) [2].
    3. **ISAACC Trial:** In patients with acute coronary syndrome (ACS), CPAP failed to reduce MACE (CV death, MI, stroke, or hospitalization for HF/unstable angina) with an HR of 0.89 (95% CI, 0.63–1.28) [3].
    4. **Meta-Analysis:** A systematic review of 10 RCTs (n=7,266) confirmed that CPAP was not associated with a reduced risk of MACE (RR 0.77; 95% CI, 0.53–1.13) or all-cause mortality [4].

    ### GRADE Certainty
    **High.** Multiple large-scale, multicenter RCTs consistently show no benefit for MACE in ITT populations.

    ### Subgroup Benefits
    Benefit is clearer in patients with **high adherence** (typically defined as ≥4 hours/night).
    * In the **SAVE trial**, a propensity-score matched analysis of adherent users suggested a reduction in stroke (HR 0.56; 95% CI, 0.32–1.00) [1].
    * In **RICCADSA**, the per-protocol analysis of patients using CPAP ≥4 hours/night showed a significant reduction in MACE (HR 0.29; 95% CI, 0.10–0.86) [2].

    **References**
    1. McEvoy RD, et al. CPAP for Prevention of Cardiovascular Events in Obstructive Sleep Apnea. N Engl J Med. 2016;375(10):919-31. PMID: 27571048.
    2. Peker Y, et al. Impact of CPAP on Cardiovascular Outcomes in Coronary Artery Disease and OSA: RICCADSA. Am J Respir Crit Care Med. 2016;194(5):613-20. PMID: 26914333.
    3. Sánchez-de-la-Torre M, et al. Effect of CPAP on Cardiovascular Outcomes in Patients With ACS and OSA: ISAACC. JAMA. 2020;323(12):1155-64. PMID: 31860004.
    4. Yu J, et al. Association of CPAP With Cardiovascular Events and Death: A Systematic Review and Meta-analysis. JAMA. 2017;318(2):156-66. PMID: 28697252.

    **Confidence self-rating: High**
    The conclusion is supported by multiple large-scale RCTs and meta-analyses with consistent findings regarding the primary ITT outcomes.
    ⒷAnchor 校正版 · KB 锚定Anchor-corrected · KB-grounded修订Track净版Clean
    对于中重度阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停(OSA)伴已确诊心血管疾病(CVD)的成人,持续气道正压(CPAP)在意向治疗(ITT)分析中未显著降低重大不良心血管事件(MACE:心血管死亡、心肌梗死、卒中、心衰住院)风险 [1,2,3]。

    ### 主要试验证据
    1. SAVE 试验: 在 2717 例已确诊 CVD 和 OSA 患者中,CPAP 联合常规治疗未降低主要复合终点(CV 死亡、MI、卒中、心衰/TIA 住院) vs 常规治疗(HR 1.10;95% CI 0.91–1.32;p=0.34)[1]。
    2. RICCADSA 试验: 在血运重建后的冠状动脉疾病(CAD)患者中,CPAP 未显著降低 MI、卒中或 CV 死亡复合终点(HR 0.80;95% CI 0.40–1.57)[2]。
    3. ISAACC 试验: 在急性冠脉综合征(ACS)患者中,CPAP 未能降低 MACE(CV 死亡、MI、卒中、心衰/不稳定心绞痛住院),HR 0.89(95% CI 0.63–1.28)[3]。
    4. Meta 分析: 一项纳入 10 项 RCT(n=7266)的系统评价证实,CPAP 与 MACE 风险降低无关(RR 0.77;95% CI 0.53–1.13)或全因死亡 [4]。

    ### GRADE 确定性
    高。 多个大规模、多中心 RCT 一致显示 ITT 人群 MACE 无获益。中等质量证据(基于多项一致 RCT,但依从性亚组分析存在偏倚风险,且主要 RCT 为阴性结果)[1,2,3]。

    ### 亚组获益
    在高依从性(通常定义为≥4小时/晚)患者中获益更明显。
    * 在 SAVE 试验中,一项倾向评分匹配分析提示依从性好的患者卒中减少(HR 0.56;95% CI 0.32–1.00)[1]。
    * 在 RICCADSA 中,使用 CPAP≥4小时/晚患者的符合方案分析显示 MACE 显著降低(HR 0.29;95% CI 0.10–0.86)[2]。
    一项纳入 3 项 RCT 的个体患者数据(IPD)meta 分析(n=4186)显示:ITT 分析 HR 1.01(95% CI 0.87–1.17);≥4小时/晚亚组 HR 0.69(95% CI 0.52–0.92),但该亚组分析受健康依从者偏倚和反向因果影响,仅具假设生成意义,不可作为因果结论 [5]。

    一项基于高风险 OSA(呼吸暂停低通气指数/缺氧负担)的多试验 NMA(n=3549)显示:高风险 OSA 患者 CPAP 与 MACE 降低相关(交互作用 HR 0.69;95% CI 0.50–0.95),而低风险患者可能有害 [6]。

    Cochrane 系统评价(2019)显示 CPAP 对全因死亡无显著影响(HR 0.81;95% CI 0.54–1.21)[7]。

    总结: 三项大型 RCT(SAVE、RICCADSA、ISAACC)一致显示 CPAP 在 ITT 分析中不降低 MACE;仅依从性≥4小时/晚亚组有信号(低确定性:健康依从者偏倚+反向因果)。CPAP 仍是症状性 OSA 一线治疗(改善嗜睡/生活质量),但不应仅为二级心血管预防而处方。    In adults with moderate-to-severe obstructive sleep apnea (OSA) and established cardiovascular disease (CVD), continuous positive airway pressure (CPAP) does not significantly reduce the risk of major adverse cardiovascular events (MACE: cardiovascular death, myocardial infarction, stroke, or hospitalization for heart failure) in intention-to-treat (ITT) analyses [1,2,3].

    ### Evidence from Major Trials
    1. SAVE Trial: In 2,717 patients with established CVD and OSA, CPAP plus usual care did not reduce the primary composite endpoint (CV death, MI, stroke, or hospitalization for HF/TIA) compared to usual care alone (HR 1.10; 95% CI, 0.91–1.32; p=0.34) [1].
    2. RICCADSA Trial: In patients with coronary artery disease (CAD) following revascularization, CPAP did not significantly reduce the composite of MI, stroke, or CV death (HR 0.80; 95% CI, 0.40–1.57) [2].
    3. ISAACC Trial: In patients with acute coronary syndrome (ACS), CPAP failed to reduce MACE (CV death, MI, stroke, or hospitalization for HF/unstable angina) with an HR of 0.89 (95% CI, 0.63–1.28) [3].
    4. Meta-Analysis: A systematic review of 10 RCTs (n=7,266) confirmed that CPAP was not associated with a reduced risk of MACE (RR 0.77; 95% CI, 0.53–1.13) or all-cause mortality [4].

    ### GRADE Certainty
    High. Multiple large-scale, multicenter RCTs consistently show no benefit for MACE in ITT populations.Moderate certainty (based on multiple consistent RCTs, but adherence subgroup analyses are at risk of bias and the primary RCTs are null) [1,2,3].

    ### Subgroup Benefits
    Benefit is clearer in patients with high adherence (typically defined as ≥4 hours/night).
    * In the SAVE trial, a propensity-score matched analysis of adherent users suggested a reduction in stroke (HR 0.56; 95% CI, 0.32–1.00) [1].
    * In RICCADSA, the per-protocol analysis of patients using CPAP ≥4 hours/night showed a significant reduction in MACE (HR 0.29; 95% CI, 0.10–0.86) [2].
    An individual patient data (IPD) meta-analysis of 3 RCTs (n=4,186) showed: ITT HR 1.01 (95% CI 0.87–1.17); ≥4h/night subgroup HR 0.69 (95% CI 0.52–0.92), but this subgroup analysis is vulnerable to healthy-adherer bias and reverse causation, and should be considered hypothesis-generating only, not causal [5].

    A multi-trial NMA (n=3,549) based on high-risk OSA (AHI/hypoxic burden) showed that CPAP was associated with reduced MACE in high-risk OSA patients (interaction HR 0.69; 95% CI 0.50–0.95), while potentially harmful in low-risk patients [6].

    A Cochrane systematic review (2019) showed no significant effect of CPAP on all-cause mortality (HR 0.81; 95% CI 0.54–1.21) [7].

    Summary: Three large RCTs (SAVE, RICCADSA, ISAACC) consistently show CPAP does not reduce MACE in ITT; only an adherent ≥4h subgroup shows a signal (low certainty: healthy-adherer bias + reverse causation). CPAP remains first-line for symptomatic OSA (sleepiness/QoL) but should not be prescribed solely for secondary cardiovascular prevention.
    对于中重度阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停(OSA)伴已确诊心血管疾病(CVD)的成人,持续气道正压(CPAP)在意向治疗(ITT)分析中未显著降低重大不良心血管事件(MACE:心血管死亡、心肌梗死、卒中、心衰住院)风险 [1,2,3]。

    ### 主要试验证据
    1. SAVE 试验: 在 2717 例已确诊 CVD 和 OSA 患者中,CPAP 联合常规治疗未降低主要复合终点(CV 死亡、MI、卒中、心衰/TIA 住院) vs 常规治疗(HR 1.10;95% CI 0.91–1.32;p=0.34)[1]。
    2. RICCADSA 试验: 在血运重建后的冠状动脉疾病(CAD)患者中,CPAP 未显著降低 MI、卒中或 CV 死亡复合终点(HR 0.80;95% CI 0.40–1.57)[2]。
    3. ISAACC 试验: 在急性冠脉综合征(ACS)患者中,CPAP 未能降低 MACE(CV 死亡、MI、卒中、心衰/不稳定心绞痛住院),HR 0.89(95% CI 0.63–1.28)[3]。
    4. Meta 分析: 一项纳入 10 项 RCT(n=7266)的系统评价证实,CPAP 与 MACE 风险降低无关(RR 0.77;95% CI 0.53–1.13)或全因死亡 [4]。

    ### GRADE 确定性
    中等质量证据(基于多项一致 RCT,但依从性亚组分析存在偏倚风险,且主要 RCT 为阴性结果)[1,2,3]。

    ### 亚组获益
    在高依从性(通常定义为≥4小时/晚)患者中获益更明显。
    * 在 SAVE 试验中,一项倾向评分匹配分析提示依从性好的患者卒中减少(HR 0.56;95% CI 0.32–1.00)[1]。
    * 在 RICCADSA 中,使用 CPAP≥4小时/晚患者的符合方案分析显示 MACE 显著降低(HR 0.29;95% CI 0.10–0.86)[2]。
    一项纳入 3 项 RCT 的个体患者数据(IPD)meta 分析(n=4186)显示:ITT 分析 HR 1.01(95% CI 0.87–1.17);≥4小时/晚亚组 HR 0.69(95% CI 0.52–0.92),但该亚组分析受健康依从者偏倚和反向因果影响,仅具假设生成意义,不可作为因果结论 [5]。

    一项基于高风险 OSA(呼吸暂停低通气指数/缺氧负担)的多试验 NMA(n=3549)显示:高风险 OSA 患者 CPAP 与 MACE 降低相关(交互作用 HR 0.69;95% CI 0.50–0.95),而低风险患者可能有害 [6]。

    Cochrane 系统评价(2019)显示 CPAP 对全因死亡无显著影响(HR 0.81;95% CI 0.54–1.21)[7]。

    总结: 三项大型 RCT(SAVE、RICCADSA、ISAACC)一致显示 CPAP 在 ITT 分析中不降低 MACE;仅依从性≥4小时/晚亚组有信号(低确定性:健康依从者偏倚+反向因果)。CPAP 仍是症状性 OSA 一线治疗(改善嗜睡/生活质量),但不应仅为二级心血管预防而处方。    In adults with moderate-to-severe obstructive sleep apnea (OSA) and established cardiovascular disease (CVD), continuous positive airway pressure (CPAP) does not significantly reduce the risk of major adverse cardiovascular events (MACE: cardiovascular death, myocardial infarction, stroke, or hospitalization for heart failure) in intention-to-treat (ITT) analyses [1,2,3].

    ### Evidence from Major Trials
    1. SAVE Trial: In 2,717 patients with established CVD and OSA, CPAP plus usual care did not reduce the primary composite endpoint (CV death, MI, stroke, or hospitalization for HF/TIA) compared to usual care alone (HR 1.10; 95% CI, 0.91–1.32; p=0.34) [1].
    2. RICCADSA Trial: In patients with coronary artery disease (CAD) following revascularization, CPAP did not significantly reduce the composite of MI, stroke, or CV death (HR 0.80; 95% CI, 0.40–1.57) [2].
    3. ISAACC Trial: In patients with acute coronary syndrome (ACS), CPAP failed to reduce MACE (CV death, MI, stroke, or hospitalization for HF/unstable angina) with an HR of 0.89 (95% CI, 0.63–1.28) [3].
    4. Meta-Analysis: A systematic review of 10 RCTs (n=7,266) confirmed that CPAP was not associated with a reduced risk of MACE (RR 0.77; 95% CI, 0.53–1.13) or all-cause mortality [4].

    ### GRADE Certainty
    Moderate certainty (based on multiple consistent RCTs, but adherence subgroup analyses are at risk of bias and the primary RCTs are null) [1,2,3].

    ### Subgroup Benefits
    Benefit is clearer in patients with high adherence (typically defined as ≥4 hours/night).
    * In the SAVE trial, a propensity-score matched analysis of adherent users suggested a reduction in stroke (HR 0.56; 95% CI, 0.32–1.00) [1].
    * In RICCADSA, the per-protocol analysis of patients using CPAP ≥4 hours/night showed a significant reduction in MACE (HR 0.29; 95% CI, 0.10–0.86) [2].
    An individual patient data (IPD) meta-analysis of 3 RCTs (n=4,186) showed: ITT HR 1.01 (95% CI 0.87–1.17); ≥4h/night subgroup HR 0.69 (95% CI 0.52–0.92), but this subgroup analysis is vulnerable to healthy-adherer bias and reverse causation, and should be considered hypothesis-generating only, not causal [5].

    A multi-trial NMA (n=3,549) based on high-risk OSA (AHI/hypoxic burden) showed that CPAP was associated with reduced MACE in high-risk OSA patients (interaction HR 0.69; 95% CI 0.50–0.95), while potentially harmful in low-risk patients [6].

    A Cochrane systematic review (2019) showed no significant effect of CPAP on all-cause mortality (HR 0.81; 95% CI 0.54–1.21) [7].

    Summary: Three large RCTs (SAVE, RICCADSA, ISAACC) consistently show CPAP does not reduce MACE in ITT; only an adherent ≥4h subgroup shows a signal (low certainty: healthy-adherer bias + reverse causation). CPAP remains first-line for symptomatic OSA (sleepiness/QoL) but should not be prescribed solely for secondary cardiovascular prevention.

    Ⓐ未矫正 · 原话Uncorrected · OpenEvidence
    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    **问题：** 对于患有中至重度阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停 (OSA) 且已有心血管疾病的成年人，持续正压通气 (CPAP) 是否能减少主要不良心血管事件 (MACE：心血管死亡、MI、卒中或心力衰竭住院)？请提供效应量 (HR 及 95% CI)、GRADE 一致性以及任何获益更明显的亚组。

    ---

    在意向治疗分析中，CPAP 未能降低患有中至重度 OSA 且已有心血管疾病成年人的 MACE 风险。然而，依从的 CPAP 使用（≥4 小时/天）与 MACE 的显著降低相关。[5]

    **效应量：**

    意向治疗分析 (3 项汇总 RCT, n=4,186)：CPAP 对 MACE 没有影响 (HR 1.01, 95% CI 0.87-1.17)。[5] 最大规模的试验 (SAVE, n=2,687) 同样未发现获益：HR 1.10 (95% CI 0.91-1.32)。[6]

    基于依从性的分析 (治疗中分析，边际结构模型)：CPAP 使用 ≥4 小时/天与 MACE 降低 31% 相关 (HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.52-0.92)。[5] 一项独立的荟萃分析 (11 项研究, n=5,410) 证实，依从的 CPAP (≥4 小时/夜) 使 MACE 降低了 42% (RR 0.58, 95% CI 0.42-0.79)，全因死亡率降低了 23% (RR 0.77, 95% CI 0.60-0.99)。[7]

    **获益更明显的亚组：**

    高风险 OSA（定义为心率反应 >9.4 bpm 或缺氧负荷 >87.1% min/h）：CPAP 在高风险 OSA 患者中显示出优先获益 (交互 HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.50-0.95, p=0.024)。[8] 该效应在没有过度嗜睡 (Epworth <11; 交互 HR 0.59, 95% CI 0.41-0.84) 或没有高血压 (BP <140/90 mmHg; 交互 HR 0.54, 95% CI 0.36-0.81) 的患者中更为强烈。[8] 值得注意的是，CPAP 在低风险 OSA 患者中似乎有害。[8]

    **GRADE 一致性：**

    意向治疗 (无获益)：中等 certainty。基于 3 项结果一致的大规模 RCT，但受限于依从性差（平均 3.1 小时/天）。[5]

    基于依从性的获益：低 certainty。源自事后治疗中分析和观察性数据；依从性并非随机分配，尽管进行了统计学调整，但仍存在混杂风险。[5]

    **参考文献：**

    5. Sánchez-de-la-Torre M, Gracia-Lavedan E, Benitez ID, et al. Adherence to CPAP Treatment and the Risk of Recurrent Cardiovascular Events: A Meta-Analysis. The Journal of the American Medical Association. 2023. (SR)
    6. McEvoy RD, Antic NA, Heeley E, et al. CPAP for Prevention of Cardiovascular Events in Obstructive Sleep Apnea. The New England Journal of Medicine. 2016. (RCT)
    7. Yang D, Li L, Dong J, Yang W, Liu Z. Effects of Continuous Positive Airway Pressure on Cardiac Events and Metabolic Components in Patients With Moderate to Severe Obstructive Sleep Apnea and Coronary Artery Disease: A Meta-Analysis. Journal of Clinical Sleep Medicine. 2023. (SR)
    8. Azarbarzin A, Vena D, Esmaeili N, et al. Cardiovascular Benefit of Continuous Positive Airway Pressure According to High-Risk Obstructive Sleep Apnoea: A Multi-Trial Analysis. European Heart Journal. 2026. (NewRCT)    In intention-to-treat analyses, CPAP does not reduce MACE in adults with moderate-to-severe OSA and established cardiovascular disease. However, adherent CPAP use (≥4 hours/day) is associated with significant MACE reduction. [5]

    **Effect Sizes:**

    Intention-to-treat analysis (3 pooled RCTs, n=4,186): CPAP showed no effect on MACE (HR 1.01, 95% CI 0.87-1.17). [5] The largest trial (SAVE, n=2,687) similarly found no benefit: HR 1.10 (95% CI 0.91-1.32). [6]

    Adherence-based analysis (on-treatment, marginal structural model): CPAP use ≥4 hours/day was associated with 31% reduction in MACE (HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.52-0.92). [5] A separate meta-analysis (11 studies, n=5,410) confirmed adherent CPAP (≥4 hours/night) reduced MACE by 42% (RR 0.58, 95% CI 0.42-0.79) and all-cause death by 23% (RR 0.77, 95% CI 0.60-0.99). [7]

    **Subgroup With Clearer Benefit:**

    High-risk OSA (defined by heart rate response >9.4 bpm or hypoxic burden >87.1% min/h): CPAP showed preferential benefit in high-risk OSA (interaction HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.50-0.95, p=0.024). [8] The effect was stronger in patients without excessive sleepiness (Epworth <11; interaction HR 0.59, 95% CI 0.41-0.84) or without hypertension (BP <140/90 mmHg; interaction HR 0.54, 95% CI 0.36-0.81). [8] Notably, CPAP appeared harmful in low-risk OSA. [8]

    **GRADE Certainty:**

    Moderate certainty for intention-to-treat (no benefit): Based on 3 large RCTs with consistent findings but limited by poor adherence (mean 3.1 hours/day). [5]

    Low certainty for adherence-based benefit: Derived from post hoc on-treatment analyses and observational data; adherence is not randomized, introducing confounding risk despite statistical adjustment. [5]

    **References:**

    5. Sánchez-de-la-Torre M, Gracia-Lavedan E, Benitez ID, et al. Adherence to CPAP Treatment and the Risk of Recurrent Cardiovascular Events: A Meta-Analysis. The Journal of the American Medical Association. 2023. (SR)
    6. McEvoy RD, Antic NA, Heeley E, et al. CPAP for Prevention of Cardiovascular Events in Obstructive Sleep Apnea. The New England Journal of Medicine. 2016. (RCT)
    7. Yang D, Li L, Dong J, Yang W, Liu Z. Effects of Continuous Positive Airway Pressure on Cardiac Events and Metabolic Components in Patients With Moderate to Severe Obstructive Sleep Apnea and Coronary Artery Disease: A Meta-Analysis. Journal of Clinical Sleep Medicine. 2023. (SR)
    8. Azarbarzin A, Vena D, Esmaeili N, et al. Cardiovascular Benefit of Continuous Positive Airway Pressure According to High-Risk Obstructive Sleep Apnoea: A Multi-Trial Analysis. European Heart Journal. 2026. (NewRCT)
    ⒷAnchor 校正版 · KB 锚定Anchor-corrected · KB-grounded修订Track净版Clean
    在按意向治疗(ITT)分析中，持续气道正压(CPAP)不能降低中重度阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停(OSA)合并已确诊心血管疾病(CVD)成人的主要不良心血管事件(MACE：心血管死亡、心肌梗死、卒中或心衰住院)。[1][2] 三项大型RCT(随机对照试验)结果一致阴性：SAVE试验(n=2,717)显示复合心血管终点风险比(HR)为1.10(95% CI 0.91–1.32)[1]；ISAACC试验(n=1,264)中MACE无显著差异[2]；Sánchez-de-la-Torre 2023 IPD meta分析(3项RCT，n=4,186)汇总ITT HR为1.01(95% CI 0.87–1.17)[3]。然而，依从性≥4小时/晚的亚组分析显示MACE降低：Sánchez-de-la-Torre 2023 IPD meta分析中HR为0.69(95% CI 0.52–0.92)[3]；另一项meta分析(11项研究，n=5,410)报告依从性≥4小时/晚者MACE降低42%(RR 0.58，95% CI 0.42–0.79)，全因死亡降低23%(RR 0.77，95% CI 0.60–0.99)[4]。但此依从性分析为事后/观察性，易受健康依从者偏倚和反向因果影响，仅具假设生成价值，不可作因果结论[3]。高风险OSA亚组(心率反应>9.4 bpm或缺氧负担>87.1% min/h)中CPAP显示更明显获益(交互作用HR 0.69，95% CI 0.50–0.95，p=0.024)[5]；无过度嗜睡(Epworth<11)或无高血压(BP<140/90 mmHg)者获益更强[5]。值得注意的是，低风险OSA中CPAP可能有害[5]。

    CPAP安全性良好，主要不良事件为面罩不适、皮肤压伤、鼻干或鼻塞等局部问题，严重不良事件罕见。Cochrane 2019系统评价(2,125例)显示CPAP对全因死亡率无显著影响(HR 0.81，95% CI 0.54–1.21)[6]。CPAP在改善打鼾、日间嗜睡、情绪和生活质量方面获益明确(高质量证据)[1]，但平均依从性仅约3.3小时/晚[1]。

    （总结：三项一致的大型RCT(SAVE/ISAACC/Sánchez-de-la-Torre IPD MA)明确显示，CPAP在ITT分析中不降低MACE[1][2][3]。依从性≥4小时/晚的亚组信号虽具提示性，但受偏倚限制，不能作为二级心血管预防的处方依据[3]。CPAP仍是症状性OSA的一线治疗(改善嗜睡和生活质量)，但不应仅为心血管二级预防而处方[1][2][3]。高风险OSA亚组(心率反应或缺氧负担升高)可能获益更大，但需前瞻性验证[5]。）    In intention-to-treat (ITT) analyses, continuous positive airway pressure (CPAP) does not reduce major adverse cardiovascular events (MACE: cardiovascular death, myocardial infarction, stroke, or hospitalization for heart failure) in adults with moderate-to-severe obstructive sleep apnea (OSA) and established cardiovascular disease (CVD). [1][2] Three large RCTs are consistently null: the SAVE trial (n=2,717) showed a hazard ratio (HR) of 1.10 (95% CI 0.91–1.32) for the composite cardiovascular endpoint [1]; the ISAACC trial (n=1,264) found no significant difference in MACE [2]; and the Sánchez-de-la-Torre 2023 IPD meta-analysis (3 RCTs, n=4,186) reported a pooled ITT HR of 1.01 (95% CI 0.87–1.17) [3]. However, in adherent subgroups (≥4 hours/night), CPAP was associated with MACE reduction: HR 0.69 (95% CI 0.52–0.92) in the Sánchez-de-la-Torre 2023 IPD meta-analysis [3]; and a separate meta-analysis (11 studies, n=5,410) reported a 42% reduction in MACE (RR 0.58, 95% CI 0.42–0.79) and a 23% reduction in all-cause death (RR 0.77, 95% CI 0.60–0.99) with adherent CPAP [4]. However, these adherence-based analyses are post hoc/observational, vulnerable to healthy-adherer bias and reverse causation, and should be considered hypothesis-generating only, not causal [3]. In high-risk OSA (defined by heart rate response >9.4 bpm or hypoxic burden >87.1% min/h), CPAP showed preferential benefit (interaction HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.50–0.95, p=0.024) [5]; benefit was stronger in patients without excessive sleepiness (Epworth <11) or without hypertension (BP <140/90 mmHg) [5]. Notably, CPAP appeared harmful in low-risk OSA [5].

    CPAP has a favorable safety profile; main adverse events are local issues such as mask discomfort, skin pressure sores, nasal dryness or congestion, with serious adverse events being rare. A Cochrane 2019 systematic review (2,125 patients) found no significant effect of CPAP on all-cause mortality (HR 0.81, 95% CI 0.54–1.21) [6]. CPAP has well-established benefits for snoring, daytime sleepiness, mood, and quality of life (high-quality evidence) [1], but mean adherence is only ~3.3 hours/night [1].



    Summary: Three consistent large RCTs (SAVE/ISAACC/Sánchez-de-la-Torre IPD MA) clearly show that CPAP does not reduce MACE in ITT analyses [1][2][3]. The signal from the adherent (≥4h/night) subgroup is suggestive but limited by bias and cannot support prescribing CPAP solely for secondary cardiovascular prevention [3]. CPAP remains first-line therapy for symptomatic OSA (improving sleepiness and quality of life) but should not be prescribed solely for secondary CV prevention [1][2][3]. The high-risk OSA subgroup (elevated heart rate response or hypoxic burden) may derive greater benefit, but this requires prospective validation [5].
    在按意向治疗(ITT)分析中，持续气道正压(CPAP)不能降低中重度阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停(OSA)合并已确诊心血管疾病(CVD)成人的主要不良心血管事件(MACE：心血管死亡、心肌梗死、卒中或心衰住院)。[1][2] 三项大型RCT(随机对照试验)结果一致阴性：SAVE试验(n=2,717)显示复合心血管终点风险比(HR)为1.10(95% CI 0.91–1.32)[1]；ISAACC试验(n=1,264)中MACE无显著差异[2]；Sánchez-de-la-Torre 2023 IPD meta分析(3项RCT，n=4,186)汇总ITT HR为1.01(95% CI 0.87–1.17)[3]。然而，依从性≥4小时/晚的亚组分析显示MACE降低：Sánchez-de-la-Torre 2023 IPD meta分析中HR为0.69(95% CI 0.52–0.92)[3]；另一项meta分析(11项研究，n=5,410)报告依从性≥4小时/晚者MACE降低42%(RR 0.58，95% CI 0.42–0.79)，全因死亡降低23%(RR 0.77，95% CI 0.60–0.99)[4]。但此依从性分析为事后/观察性，易受健康依从者偏倚和反向因果影响，仅具假设生成价值，不可作因果结论[3]。高风险OSA亚组(心率反应>9.4 bpm或缺氧负担>87.1% min/h)中CPAP显示更明显获益(交互作用HR 0.69，95% CI 0.50–0.95，p=0.024)[5]；无过度嗜睡(Epworth<11)或无高血压(BP<140/90 mmHg)者获益更强[5]。值得注意的是，低风险OSA中CPAP可能有害[5]。

    CPAP安全性良好，主要不良事件为面罩不适、皮肤压伤、鼻干或鼻塞等局部问题，严重不良事件罕见。Cochrane 2019系统评价(2,125例)显示CPAP对全因死亡率无显著影响(HR 0.81，95% CI 0.54–1.21)[6]。CPAP在改善打鼾、日间嗜睡、情绪和生活质量方面获益明确(高质量证据)[1]，但平均依从性仅约3.3小时/晚[1]。

    （总结：三项一致的大型RCT(SAVE/ISAACC/Sánchez-de-la-Torre IPD MA)明确显示，CPAP在ITT分析中不降低MACE[1][2][3]。依从性≥4小时/晚的亚组信号虽具提示性，但受偏倚限制，不能作为二级心血管预防的处方依据[3]。CPAP仍是症状性OSA的一线治疗(改善嗜睡和生活质量)，但不应仅为心血管二级预防而处方[1][2][3]。高风险OSA亚组(心率反应或缺氧负担升高)可能获益更大，但需前瞻性验证[5]。）    In intention-to-treat (ITT) analyses, continuous positive airway pressure (CPAP) does not reduce major adverse cardiovascular events (MACE: cardiovascular death, myocardial infarction, stroke, or hospitalization for heart failure) in adults with moderate-to-severe obstructive sleep apnea (OSA) and established cardiovascular disease (CVD). [1][2] Three large RCTs are consistently null: the SAVE trial (n=2,717) showed a hazard ratio (HR) of 1.10 (95% CI 0.91–1.32) for the composite cardiovascular endpoint [1]; the ISAACC trial (n=1,264) found no significant difference in MACE [2]; and the Sánchez-de-la-Torre 2023 IPD meta-analysis (3 RCTs, n=4,186) reported a pooled ITT HR of 1.01 (95% CI 0.87–1.17) [3]. However, in adherent subgroups (≥4 hours/night), CPAP was associated with MACE reduction: HR 0.69 (95% CI 0.52–0.92) in the Sánchez-de-la-Torre 2023 IPD meta-analysis [3]; and a separate meta-analysis (11 studies, n=5,410) reported a 42% reduction in MACE (RR 0.58, 95% CI 0.42–0.79) and a 23% reduction in all-cause death (RR 0.77, 95% CI 0.60–0.99) with adherent CPAP [4]. However, these adherence-based analyses are post hoc/observational, vulnerable to healthy-adherer bias and reverse causation, and should be considered hypothesis-generating only, not causal [3]. In high-risk OSA (defined by heart rate response >9.4 bpm or hypoxic burden >87.1% min/h), CPAP showed preferential benefit (interaction HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.50–0.95, p=0.024) [5]; benefit was stronger in patients without excessive sleepiness (Epworth <11) or without hypertension (BP <140/90 mmHg) [5]. Notably, CPAP appeared harmful in low-risk OSA [5].

    CPAP has a favorable safety profile; main adverse events are local issues such as mask discomfort, skin pressure sores, nasal dryness or congestion, with serious adverse events being rare. A Cochrane 2019 systematic review (2,125 patients) found no significant effect of CPAP on all-cause mortality (HR 0.81, 95% CI 0.54–1.21) [6]. CPAP has well-established benefits for snoring, daytime sleepiness, mood, and quality of life (high-quality evidence) [1], but mean adherence is only ~3.3 hours/night [1].

    Summary: Three consistent large RCTs (SAVE/ISAACC/Sánchez-de-la-Torre IPD MA) clearly show that CPAP does not reduce MACE in ITT analyses [1][2][3]. The signal from the adherent (≥4h/night) subgroup is suggestive but limited by bias and cannot support prescribing CPAP solely for secondary cardiovascular prevention [3]. CPAP remains first-line therapy for symptomatic OSA (improving sleepiness and quality of life) but should not be prescribed solely for secondary CV prevention [1][2][3]. The high-risk OSA subgroup (elevated heart rate response or hypoxic burden) may derive greater benefit, but this requires prospective validation [5].

    💡 Anchor 校正要点总结(读者快速版)Anchor correction · Key takeaways (executive summary)
    • 三项大型 RCT 一致显示 CPAP 在 ITT 分析中不降低 MACE。

    • 高风险 OSA 亚组（心率反应或缺氧负担升高）可能获益更大，但需前瞻性验证。

    • The adherent (≥4h/night) subgroup signal is hypothesis-generating only and cannot support prescribing.
    • High-risk OSA subgroup (elevated heart rate response or hypoxic burden) may derive greater benefit, but prospective validation is needed.

    Ⓐ未矫正 · 原话Uncorrected · ChatGPT 5.5
    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    **问题：** 对于患有中至重度阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停 (OSA) 且已有心血管疾病的成年人，持续正压通气 (CPAP) 是否能减少主要不良心血管事件 (MACE：心血管死亡、MI、卒中或心力衰竭住院)？请提供效应量 (HR 及 95% CI)、GRADE 确定性以及任何获益更明显的亚组。

    ---

    对于患有中至重度 OSA 且已有心血管疾病的成年人，不应仅为了减少 MACE 而开具 CPAP 处方，因为随机意向治疗证据显示心血管事件并未明显减少，尽管 CPAP 对于改善嗜睡、提高生活质量、控制血压和 OSA 症状仍然是合适的 [1–4]。

    规模最大的 RCT 研究 SAVE 测试了在 2717 名主要无嗜睡症状且患有中重度 OSA 及冠状动脉/脑血管疾病的患者中，CPAP 联合常规护理与单纯常规护理的效果。CPAP 未能降低复合心血管终点：HR 1.10, 95% CI 0.91–1.32；平均依从性仅为 3.3 小时/夜；GRADE：对于无显著 MACE 获益的结论为中等，因依从性问题及排除了极度嗜睡患者而下调 [1]。

    另外两项二级预防 RCT 研究结果一致。在 RICCADSA 中，针对经血运重建的冠心病且无嗜睡症状的 OSA 患者，CPAP 在意向治疗分析中未显著降低原发性心血管复合终点：HR 0.80, 95% CI 0.46–1.41；GRADE：由于样本量较小且精确度不足而定为低 [2]。在 ISAACC 中，急性冠脉综合征后的 CPAP 未能减少复发性心血管事件：HR 0.89, 95% CI 0.68–1.17；GRADE：中等 [3]。

    最可能的获益出现在实际使用 CPAP ≥4 小时/夜的患者中，但由于依从性是在随机化后确定的，因此该结论的可靠性较低。一项 2023 年针对 SAVE、RICCADSA 和 ISAACC 的个体参与者数据荟萃分析发现，在意向治疗分析中没有获益：HR 1.01, 95% CI 0.87–1.17，但在依从用户中复发性 MACCE 较少：HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.52–0.92；GRADE：对于依从亚组定为低，因为尽管进行了调整分析，但仍存在健康依从者偏差/混杂因素的担忧 [4]。

    RCT 研究尚未证明其在降低死亡率方面的获益；这些试验在心血管死亡方面的统计效能不足且依从性一般。因此，我会针对有症状或具有生理重要性的 OSA 提供 CPAP 治疗，并告知患者减少心血管事件的效果尚不确定，且可能取决于持续的每晚使用。

    **参考文献**

    1. McEvoy RD, Antic NA, Heeley E, et al. CPAP for prevention of cardiovascular events in obstructive sleep apnea. N Engl J Med. 2016;375(10):919-931. doi:10.1056/NEJMoa1606599.
    2. Peker Y, Glantz H, Eulenburg C, Wegscheider K, Herlitz J, Thunström E. Effect of positive airway pressure on cardiovascular outcomes in coronary artery disease patients with nonsleepy obstructive sleep apnea: the RICCADSA randomized controlled trial. Am J Respir Crit Care Med. 2016;194(5):613-620.
    3. Sánchez-de-la-Torre M, Sánchez-de-la-Torre A, Bertran S, et al. Effect of obstructive sleep apnoea and its treatment with continuous positive airway pressure on cardiovascular events in patients with acute coronary syndrome: the ISAACC study. Lancet Respir Med. 2020;8(4):359-367.
    4. Sánchez-de-la-Torre M, Gracia-Lavedan E, Benitez ID, et al. Adherence to CPAP treatment and the risk of recurrent cardiovascular events: a meta-analysis. JAMA. 2023;330(13):1255-1265.

    **总体信心：** 中等 —— 多项 RCT 一致显示意向治疗下 MACE 未减少，但依从性一般，且 ≥4 小时/夜亚组的获益虽具有合理性但存在混杂因素。    In adults with moderate-to-severe OSA and established cardiovascular disease, CPAP should not be prescribed solely to reduce MACE, because randomized intention-to-treat evidence shows no clear cardiovascular event reduction, although it remains appropriate for sleepiness, quality of life, blood pressure, and OSA symptom control [1–4].

    The largest RCT, SAVE, tested CPAP plus usual care vs usual care in 2717 mostly nonsleepy patients with moderate–severe OSA and coronary/cerebrovascular disease. CPAP did not reduce the composite cardiovascular endpoint: HR 1.10, 95% CI 0.91–1.32; mean adherence was only 3.3 h/night; GRADE: moderate for no large MACE benefit, downgraded for adherence and exclusion of very sleepy patients [1].

    Two other secondary-prevention RCTs are consistent. In RICCADSA, CPAP in revascularized coronary artery disease with nonsleepy OSA did not significantly reduce the primary cardiovascular composite by intention-to-treat: HR 0.80, 95% CI 0.46–1.41; GRADE: low because of smaller sample size and imprecision [2]. In ISAACC, CPAP after acute coronary syndrome did not reduce recurrent cardiovascular events: HR 0.89, 95% CI 0.68–1.17; GRADE: moderate [3].

    The clearest possible benefit is in patients who actually use CPAP ≥4 h/night, but this is less secure because adherence is post-randomization. A 2023 individual-participant-data meta-analysis of SAVE, RICCADSA, and ISAACC found no benefit in intention-to-treat analysis: HR 1.01, 95% CI 0.87–1.17, but fewer recurrent MACCE among adherent users: HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.52–0.92; GRADE: low for the adherence subgroup because of healthy-adherer/confounding concerns despite adjusted analyses [4].

    Mortality benefit is unproven in RCTs; trials were underpowered for cardiovascular death and had modest adherence. Therefore, I would offer CPAP for symptomatic or physiologically important OSA, and discuss that cardiovascular event reduction is uncertain and probably depends on sustained nightly use.

    **References**

    1. McEvoy RD, Antic NA, Heeley E, et al. CPAP for prevention of cardiovascular events in obstructive sleep apnea. N Engl J Med. 2016;375(10):919-931. doi:10.1056/NEJMoa1606599.
    2. Peker Y, Glantz H, Eulenburg C, Wegscheider K, Herlitz J, Thunström E. Effect of positive airway pressure on cardiovascular outcomes in coronary artery disease patients with nonsleepy obstructive sleep apnea: the RICCADSA randomized controlled trial. Am J Respir Crit Care Med. 2016;194(5):613-620.
    3. Sánchez-de-la-Torre M, Sánchez-de-la-Torre A, Bertran S, et al. Effect of obstructive sleep apnoea and its treatment with continuous positive airway pressure on cardiovascular events in patients with acute coronary syndrome: the ISAACC study. Lancet Respir Med. 2020;8(4):359-367.
    4. Sánchez-de-la-Torre M, Gracia-Lavedan E, Benitez ID, et al. Adherence to CPAP treatment and the risk of recurrent cardiovascular events: a meta-analysis. JAMA. 2023;330(13):1255-1265.

    **Overall confidence:** Moderate — multiple RCTs consistently show no intention-to-treat MACE reduction, but adherence is modest and the ≥4 h/night subgroup benefit is plausible but confounded.
    ⒷAnchor 校正版 · KB 锚定Anchor-corrected · KB-grounded修订Track净版Clean
    对于中重度阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停(OSA)伴已确诊心血管疾病的成人，持续气道正压(CPAP)不应仅为了降低主要不良心血管事件(MACE:心血管死亡、心肌梗死、卒中、心衰住院)而处方，因为随机意向治疗(ITT)证据显示无明确心血管事件减少，但CPAP仍适用于改善嗜睡、生活质量、血压及OSA症状控制[1–4]。

    最大规模RCT(随机对照试验)SAVE在2717例主要为非嗜睡的中重度OSA合并冠脉/脑血管疾病患者中，比较CPAP联合常规治疗与单纯常规治疗。CPAP未降低复合心血管终点:HR 1.10，95% CI 0.91–1.32；平均依从性仅3.3小时/晚；GRADE:moderate for no large MACE benefit，因依从性和排除嗜睡患者降级单一RCT不评GRADE/confidence，应评Cochrane RoB-2偏倚风险(本试验RoB-2:Low/低偏倚风险)[1]。另两项二级预防RCT结果一致:RICCADSA中，CPAP在血运重建后冠心病伴非嗜睡OSA患者中未显著降低主要心血管复合终点(ITT:HR 0.80，95% CI 0.46–1.41)；GRADE:low因样本量小和不精确(单一RCT不评GRADE，RoB-2:Some concerns)[2]；ISAACC中，CPAP在急性冠脉综合征后未减少复发心血管事件(HR 0.89，95% CI 0.68–1.17)；GRADE:moderate(单一RCT不评GRADE，RoB-2:Low)[3]。Azarbarzin EHJ 2026多试验网络meta分析(NMA)(n=3549)进一步识别出高风险OSA亚组(基于呼吸暂停低通气指数/缺氧负担):交互作用HR 0.69(95% CI 0.50–0.95)，提示该亚组可能从CPAP中获益；而低风险OSA患者中CPAP可能有害[4]。

    最明确的潜在获益见于实际使用CPAP≥4小时/晚的患者，但此证据较弱，因依从性为随机后变量。2023年个体患者数据meta分析(IPD MA)汇总SAVE、RICCADSA和ISAACC，ITT分析无获益(HR 1.01，95% CI 0.87–1.17)，但依从使用者中复发MACCE减少(HR 0.69，95% CI 0.52–0.92)；GRADE:low for the adherence subgroup因健康依从者偏倚/混杂(IPD MA可评GRADE:ITT中等质量证据，依从亚组低质量证据，因健康依从者偏倚和混杂)[5]。全因死亡率在RCT中未证实获益(Cochrane 2019系统评价HR 0.81，95% CI 0.54–1.21)[6]。因此，CPAP应提供给有症状或生理学重要OSA的患者，并告知心血管事件减少不确定，可能取决于持续夜间使用。    In adults with moderate-to-severe OSA and established cardiovascular disease, CPAP should not be prescribed solely to reduce MACE, because randomized intention-to-treat evidence shows no clear cardiovascular event reduction, although it remains appropriate for sleepiness, quality of life, blood pressure, and OSA symptom control [1–4].

    The largest RCT, SAVE, tested CPAP plus usual care vs usual care in 2717 mostly nonsleepy patients with moderate–severe OSA and coronary/cerebrovascular disease. CPAP did not reduce the composite cardiovascular endpoint: HR 1.10, 95% CI 0.91–1.32; mean adherence was only 3.3 h/night; GRADE: moderate for no large MACE benefit, downgraded for adherence and exclusion of very sleepy patients single RCT should not be rated with GRADE/confidence; Cochrane RoB-2 applies (this trial: Low risk of bias) [1]. Two other secondary-prevention RCTs are consistent. In RICCADSA, CPAP in revascularized coronary artery disease with nonsleepy OSA did not significantly reduce the primary cardiovascular composite by intention-to-treat: HR 0.80, 95% CI 0.46–1.41; GRADE: low because of smaller sample size and imprecision (single RCT — RoB-2: Some concerns) [2]. In ISAACC, CPAP after acute coronary syndrome did not reduce recurrent cardiovascular events: HR 0.89, 95% CI 0.68–1.17; GRADE: moderate (single RCT — RoB-2: Low) [3]. The Azarbarzin EHJ 2026 multi-trial network meta-analysis (n=3549) further identified a high-risk OSA subgroup (based on apnea-hypopnea index/hypoxic burden): interaction HR 0.69 (95% CI 0.50–0.95), suggesting possible CPAP benefit in this subgroup, while CPAP may be harmful in low-risk OSA [4].

    The clearest possible benefit is in patients who actually use CPAP ≥4 h/night, but this is less secure because adherence is post-randomization. A 2023 individual-participant-data meta-analysis of SAVE, RICCADSA, and ISAACC found no benefit in intention-to-treat analysis: HR 1.01, 95% CI 0.87–1.17, but fewer recurrent MACCE among adherent users: HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.52–0.92; GRADE: low for the adherence subgroup because of healthy-adherer/confounding concerns despite adjusted analyses (IPD MA allows GRADE: moderate for ITT, low for adherent subgroup due to healthy-adherer bias and confounding) [5]. All-cause mortality is unproven in RCTs (Cochrane 2019 SR: HR 0.81, 95% CI 0.54–1.21) [6]. Therefore, offer CPAP for symptomatic or physiologically important OSA, and discuss that cardiovascular event reduction is uncertain and probably depends on sustained nightly use.

    💡 Anchor 校正要点总结(读者快速版)Anchor correction · Key takeaways (executive summary)
    • LLM 正确报告了 SAVE/RICCADSA/ISAACC 的 ITT 阴性结果，但误将 GRADE 评级用于单一 RCT；Anchor 校正为 RoB-2 偏倚风险评估。
    • Anchor 新增 Azarbarzin EHJ 2026 NMA 高风险 OSA 亚组分析，提供临床可操作的获益信号(交互作用 HR 0.69)。
    • LLM 对依从性亚组的 GRADE 评级不准确；Anchor 明确 IPD MA 可评 GRADE，ITT 中等质量证据，依从亚组低质量证据。
    • 核心临床结论不变:CPAP 不应仅用于二级心血管预防，但高风险 OSA 亚组可能获益。
    • LLM correctly reported ITT null results from SAVE/RICCADSA/ISAACC, but incorrectly applied GRADE to single RCTs; Anchor corrected to RoB-2.
    • Anchor added the Azarbarzin EHJ 2026 NMA high-risk OSA subgroup analysis, providing a clinically actionable benefit signal (interaction HR 0.69).
    • LLM's GRADE rating for the adherence subgroup was inaccurate; Anchor clarified that IPD MA allows GRADE: moderate for ITT, low for adherent subgroup.
    • Core clinical conclusion unchanged: CPAP should not be prescribed solely for secondary CV prevention, but high-risk OSA subgroup may benefit.
    Ⓐ未矫正 · 原话Uncorrected · Claude 4.7
    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    在患有中至重度 OSA 且伴有既往心血管疾病的成人中，随机对照试验显示，在按意向治疗（intention-to-treat）原则开具处方时，CPAP 并不能降低 MACE [1,2,3]。

    **主要 RCT 证据**
    SAVE 试验（n=2,717；中至重度 OSA 且伴有既往冠心病或脑血管疾病）发现，与仅接受常规护理相比，CPAP 联合常规护理在降低心血管死亡、MI、卒中或因不稳定型心绞痛/HF/TIA 入院的复合终点方面没有显著效果：HR 1.10 (95% CI 0.91–1.32) [1]。平均 CPAP 依从性较低（约 3.3 小时/夜）。CPAP 确实改善了打鼾、日间嗜睡、情绪和生活质量 [1]。
    RICCADSA 试验（CAD + 非嗜睡型 OSA）在意向治疗分析中显示 MACE 未显著降低，HR 0.80 (95% CI 0.46–1.41) [2]。
    ISAACC 试验（急性冠脉综合征 + OSA）同样未发现获益，HR ~0.89 (95% CI 0.68–1.17) [3]。

    **获益较为明显的亚组**
    在依从性良好的使用者（≥4 小时/夜）中一致出现了相关信号。一项个体患者数据荟萃分析（Sánchez-de-la-Torre 等，JAMA 2023）发现虽然整体无效果，但在治疗分析（on-treatment analysis）中，良好的依从性与 MACE 降低相关——约为 HR 0.69 (95% CI 0.52–0.92) [4]。一项 SAVE 次级（倾向评分匹配）分析同样提示使用时间 ≥4 小时可减少脑血管事件 [1]。这些分析容易受到“健康依从者”偏倚和反向因果关系的影响，因此它们是用于产生假设而非证实结论的。

    **实践综合：** CPAP 仍然是症状性 OSA（嗜睡、生活质量）的一线治疗方案，但根据目前的试验证据，不应将其主要用于心血管二级预防。针对高依从性和/或更嗜睡表型是否能产生心血管获益仍是一个开放性问题，将在正在进行或未来的试验中予以探讨。

    GRADE 确定性：对于无整体 MACE 获益的结论为 中等-高（多项一致的 RCT，尽管受限于低依从性和排除了极度嗜睡患者）。对于依从性亚组获益的结论为 低（次级/观察性分析，存在混杂因素）。

    References
    1. McEvoy RD, Antic NA, Heeley E, et al. CPAP for prevention of cardiovascular events in obstructive sleep apnea. N Engl J Med. 2016;375(10):919–931. doi:10.1056/NEJMoa1606599
    2. Peker Y, Glantz H, Eulenburg C, et al. Effect of positive airway pressure on cardiovascular outcomes in coronary artery disease patients with nonsleepy obstructive sleep apnea (RICCADSA). Am J Respir Crit Care Med. 2016;194(5):613–620.
    3. Sánchez-de-la-Torre M, Sánchez-de-la-Torre A, Bertran S, et al. Effect of obstructive sleep apnoea and its treatment with CPAP on the prevalence of cardiovascular events in patients with acute coronary syndrome (ISAACC). Lancet Respir Med. 2020;8(4):359–367.
    4. Sánchez-de-la-Torre M, Gracia-Lavedan E, Benitez ID, et al. Adherence to CPAP and cardiovascular outcomes in OSA: an individual patient data meta-analysis. JAMA. 2023;330(13):1255–1265.

    总体信心：（未明确标注；GRADE：无整体获益为 中等-高，依从性亚组为 低）    In adults with moderate-to-severe OSA and established cardiovascular disease, randomized trials do not show that CPAP reduces MACE when prescribed on an intention-to-treat basis [1,2,3].

    Primary RCT evidence
    The SAVE trial (n=2,717; moderate–severe OSA with established coronary or cerebrovascular disease) found no reduction in the composite of CV death, MI, stroke, or hospitalization for unstable angina/HF/TIA: HR 1.10 (95% CI 0.91–1.32) with CPAP plus usual care versus usual care alone [1]. Mean CPAP adherence was low (~3.3 h/night). CPAP did improve snoring, daytime sleepiness, mood, and quality of life [1].
    The RICCADSA trial (CAD + nonsleepy OSA) showed no significant MACE reduction in intention-to-treat analysis, HR 0.80 (95% CI 0.46–1.41) [2].
    The ISAACC trial (acute coronary syndrome + OSA) likewise found no benefit, HR ~0.89 (95% CI 0.68–1.17) [3].

    Subgroup where benefit appears clearer
    The signal consistently emerges in adherent users (≥4 h/night). An individual-patient-data meta-analysis (Sánchez-de-la-Torre et al., JAMA 2023) found no overall effect but, in on-treatment analysis, good adherence was associated with reduced MACE — approximately HR 0.69 (95% CI 0.52–0.92) [4]. A SAVE secondary (propensity-matched) analysis similarly suggested reduced cerebrovascular events with ≥4 h use [1]. These analyses are vulnerable to "healthy adherer" bias and reverse causation, so they are hypothesis-generating rather than confirmatory.

    Practical synthesis: CPAP remains first-line for symptomatic OSA (sleepiness, quality of life), but it should not be prescribed primarily for secondary CV prevention based on current trial evidence. Whether targeting highly adherent and/or sleepier phenotypes yields cardiovascular benefit is an open question being addressed in ongoing/future trials.

    GRADE certainty: Moderate-to-high for no overall MACE benefit (multiple consistent RCTs, though limited by low adherence and exclusion of very sleepy patients). Low for the adherence-subgroup benefit (secondary/observational, confounded).

    References
    1. McEvoy RD, Antic NA, Heeley E, et al. CPAP for prevention of cardiovascular events in obstructive sleep apnea. N Engl J Med. 2016;375(10):919–931. doi:10.1056/NEJMoa1606599
    2. Peker Y, Glantz H, Eulenburg C, et al. Effect of positive airway pressure on cardiovascular outcomes in coronary artery disease patients with nonsleepy obstructive sleep apnea (RICCADSA). Am J Respir Crit Care Med. 2016;194(5):613–620.
    3. Sánchez-de-la-Torre M, Sánchez-de-la-Torre A, Bertran S, et al. Effect of obstructive sleep apnoea and its treatment with CPAP on the prevalence of cardiovascular events in patients with acute coronary syndrome (ISAACC). Lancet Respir Med. 2020;8(4):359–367.
    4. Sánchez-de-la-Torre M, Gracia-Lavedan E, Benitez ID, et al. Adherence to CPAP and cardiovascular outcomes in OSA: an individual patient data meta-analysis. JAMA. 2023;330(13):1255–1265.

    Overall confidence: (not explicitly labeled; GRADE: Moderate-to-high for no overall benefit, Low for adherence subgroup)

    <!-- captured: Claude 4.8 (Opus), case O (OSA CPAP for CV events), locked prompt v1.5 · verbatim -->
    ⒷAnchor 校正版 · KB 锚定Anchor-corrected · KB-grounded修订Track净版Clean
    对于中重度阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停(OSA)伴已确诊心血管疾病(CVD)的成人,随机对照试验(RCT)的意向性治疗(ITT)分析并未显示持续气道正压(CPAP)能降低重大不良心血管事件(MACE:心血管死亡、心肌梗死、卒中或心力衰竭住院)[1,2,3]。

    主要RCT证据:SAVE试验(n=2,717;中重度OSA伴已确诊冠脉或脑血管疾病)发现,CPAP联合常规治疗 vs 常规治疗在心血管死亡、心梗、卒中或不稳定心绞痛/心衰/短暂性脑缺血发作(TIA)复合终点上无显著降低:HR 1.10(95% CI 0.91–1.32)[1]。平均CPAP依从性较低(~3.3小时/晚)。CPAP确实改善了打鼾、日间嗜睡、情绪和生活质量[1]。RICCADSA试验(冠心病+非嗜睡OSA)在ITT分析中未显示MACE显著降低,HR 0.80(95% CI 0.46–1.41)[2]。ISAACC试验(急性冠脉综合征+OSA)同样未发现获益,HR约0.89(95% CI 0.68–1.17)[3]。Cochrane 2019系统评价(n=2,125)显示全因死亡率HR 0.81(95% CI 0.54–1.21),无统计学意义[5]。

    获益可能更明显的亚组:信号一致出现在依从性好的使用者(≥4小时/晚)中。一项个体患者数据meta分析(IPD MA;Sánchez-de-la-Torre等,JAMA 2023)发现ITT无总体效应,但在治疗分析中,良好依从性与MACE降低相关——HR约0.69(95% CI 0.52–0.92)[4]。SAVE的二次分析(倾向评分匹配)同样提示≥4小时使用可减少脑血管事件[1]。Azarbarzin等2026年多试验分析(n=3,549)进一步显示,在缺氧负担高的高风险OSA患者中,CPAP可能降低MACE(交互作用HR 0.69,95% CI 0.50–0.95),而在低风险患者中可能有害[6]。这些分析易受「健康依从者」偏倚和反向因果影响,因此仅具假设生成价值,而非确证性

    实践

    总结:CPAP仍是症状性OSA(嗜睡、生活质量)的一线治疗,但基于当前试验证据,不应主要为了二级心血管预防而处方。ITT分析显示无心血管获益(中等质量证据,来自多个一致RCT);依从性亚组信号为低质量证据(二次/观察性分析,存在混杂)[4]。是否针对高依从性和/或更嗜睡的表型能带来心血管获益,这是一个开放性问题,正在进行的试验正在探索中。    In adults with moderate-to-severe obstructive sleep apnea (OSA) and established cardiovascular disease (CVD), randomized controlled trials (RCTs) do not show that continuous positive airway pressure (CPAP) reduces major adverse cardiovascular events (MACE: cardiovascular death, myocardial infarction, stroke, or hospitalization for heart failure) on an intention-to-treat (ITT) basis [1,2,3].

    Primary RCT evidence: The SAVE trial (n=2,717; moderate–severe OSA with established coronary or cerebrovascular disease) found no reduction in the composite of CV death, MI, stroke, or hospitalization for unstable angina/HF/TIA: HR 1.10 (95% CI 0.91–1.32) with CPAP plus usual care versus usual care alone [1]. Mean CPAP adherence was low (~3.3 h/night). CPAP did improve snoring, daytime sleepiness, mood, and quality of life [1]. The RICCADSA trial (CAD + nonsleepy OSA) showed no significant MACE reduction in ITT analysis, HR 0.80 (95% CI 0.46–1.41) [2]. The ISAACC trial (acute coronary syndrome + OSA) likewise found no benefit, HR ~0.89 (95% CI 0.68–1.17) [3]. A Cochrane 2019 systematic review (n=2,125) reported all-cause mortality HR 0.81 (95% CI 0.54–1.21), not statistically significant [5].

    Subgroup where benefit appears clearer: The signal consistently emerges in adherent users (≥4 h/night). An individual-patient-data meta-analysis (IPD MA; Sánchez-de-la-Torre et al., JAMA 2023) found no overall ITT effect but, in on-treatment analysis, good adherence was associated with reduced MACE — approximately HR 0.69 (95% CI 0.52–0.92) [4]. A SAVE secondary (propensity-matched) analysis similarly suggested reduced cerebrovascular events with ≥4 h use [1]. Azarbarzin et al. 2026 multi-trial analysis (n=3,549) further showed that in high-risk OSA patients with high hypoxic burden, CPAP may reduce MACE (interaction HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.50–0.95), while it may be harmful in low-risk patients [6]. These analyses are vulnerable to &#x27;healthy adherer&#x27; bias and reverse causation, so they are hypothesis-generating rather than confirmatory.

    Practical synthesis: CPAP remains first-line for symptomatic OSA (sleepiness, quality of life), but it should not be prescribed primarily for secondary CV prevention based on current trial evidence. ITT analysis shows no CV benefit (moderate-quality evidence from multiple consistent RCTs); the adherence-subgroup signal is low-quality evidence (secondary/observational, confounded) [4]. Whether targeting highly adherent and/or sleepier phenotypes yields cardiovascular benefit is an open question being addressed in ongoing trials.
    对于中重度阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停(OSA)伴已确诊心血管疾病(CVD)的成人,随机对照试验(RCT)的意向性治疗(ITT)分析并未显示持续气道正压(CPAP)能降低重大不良心血管事件(MACE:心血管死亡、心肌梗死、卒中或心力衰竭住院)[1,2,3]。

    实践

    总结:CPAP仍是症状性OSA(嗜睡、生活质量)的一线治疗,但基于当前试验证据,不应主要为了二级心血管预防而处方。ITT分析显示无心血管获益(中等质量证据,来自多个一致RCT);依从性亚组信号为低质量证据(二次/观察性分析,存在混杂)[4]。是否针对高依从性和/或更嗜睡的表型能带来心血管获益,这是一个开放性问题,正在进行的试验正在探索中。    In adults with moderate-to-severe obstructive sleep apnea (OSA) and established cardiovascular disease (CVD), randomized controlled trials (RCTs) do not show that continuous positive airway pressure (CPAP) reduces major adverse cardiovascular events (MACE: cardiovascular death, myocardial infarction, stroke, or hospitalization for heart failure) on an intention-to-treat (ITT) basis [1,2,3].

    Primary RCT evidence: The SAVE trial (n=2,717; moderate–severe OSA with established coronary or cerebrovascular disease) found no reduction in the composite of CV death, MI, stroke, or hospitalization for unstable angina/HF/TIA: HR 1.10 (95% CI 0.91–1.32) with CPAP plus usual care versus usual care alone [1]. The RICCADSA trial (CAD + nonsleepy OSA) showed no significant MACE reduction in ITT analysis, HR 0.80 (95% CI 0.46–1.41) [2]. The ISAACC trial (acute coronary syndrome + OSA) likewise found no benefit, HR ~0.89 (95% CI 0.68–1.17) [3]. A Cochrane 2019 systematic review (n=2,125) reported all-cause mortality HR 0.81 (95% CI 0.54–1.21), not statistically significant [5].

    Subgroup where benefit appears clearer: The signal consistently emerges in adherent users (≥4 h/night). An individual-patient-data meta-analysis (IPD MA; Sánchez-de-la-Torre et al., JAMA 2023) found no overall ITT effect but, in on-treatment analysis, good adherence was associated with reduced MACE — approximately HR 0.69 (95% CI 0.52–0.92) [4]. Azarbarzin et al. 2026 multi-trial analysis (n=3,549) further showed that in high-risk OSA patients with high hypoxic burden, CPAP may reduce MACE (interaction HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.50–0.95), while it may be harmful in low-risk patients [6]. (These analyses are vulnerable to 'healthy adherer' bias and reverse causation, so they are hypothesis-generating rather than confirmatory.)

    Practical synthesis: CPAP remains first-line for symptomatic OSA (sleepiness, quality of life), but it should not be prescribed primarily for secondary CV prevention based on current trial evidence. ITT analysis shows no CV benefit (moderate-quality evidence from multiple consistent RCTs); the adherence-subgroup signal is low-quality evidence (secondary/observational, confounded) [4]. Whether targeting highly adherent and/or sleepier phenotypes yields cardiovascular benefit is an open question being addressed in ongoing trials.

    ⚠ 3 项一致 RCT(SAVE/RICCADSA/ISAACC)— CPAP 在 ITT 分析不降低 MACE;仅依从性 ≥4h 亚组有信号(低确定性:健康依从者偏倚 + 反向因果)。CPAP 仍是症状性 OSA 一线(改善嗜睡 / 生活质量),但不应仅为二级心血管预防而处方。⚠ 3 consistent RCTs (SAVE/RICCADSA/ISAACC) — CPAP does NOT reduce MACE in ITT; only an adherent ≥4h subgroup shows a signal (low certainty: healthy-adherer bias + reverse causation). CPAP stays first-line for symptomatic OSA (sleepiness / QoL) but should not be prescribed solely for secondary CV prevention.

    ✅ 临床落地(基于本案 Anchor 校正后,你的临床决策应这样做)✅ Clinical take-home (after Anchor correction, your clinical decision should be)

    • 不要为 OSA + 已知 CVD 患者 routine 推 CPAP 用于 CV 事件预防 —— SAVE 阴性(HR 1.10)+ 3-RCT IPD MA 阴性(HR 1.01,n=4186);ITT 没有 MACE 获益。这是 3 LLM 高一致识别的临床误区。
    • CPAP 仍对 OSA 症状 / sleepiness / QoL / 血压有获益 —— 应继续推荐 CPAP for 症状性 OSA,但不要承诺 CV 事件减少,患者教育时清楚分清「症状管理」vs「CV 事件预防」。
    • 临床细分:High-risk OSA(hypoxia burden + heart-rate response)CPAP 可能有 CV 获益(EHJ 2026 NMA HR 0.69)· 低风险 OSA 可能有害(EHJ 2026)· 应先 stratify 风险再决定 CPAP 适应症。
    • Do NOT routinely prescribe CPAP for OSA + CVD purely for CV event prevention — SAVE null (HR 1.10) + 3-RCT IPD MA null (HR 1.01, n=4186); no ITT MACE benefit. This is a clinical misconception identified by all 3 LLMs.
    • CPAP still benefits OSA symptoms / sleepiness / QoL / BP — continue recommending CPAP for symptomatic OSA, but do NOT promise CV event reduction; clearly separate 'symptom management' from 'CV prevention' in patient counselling.
    • Clinical stratification: High-risk OSA (hypoxia burden + heart-rate response) may have CV benefit on CPAP (EHJ 2026 NMA HR 0.69); low-risk OSA may be harmful (EHJ 2026); stratify risk before CPAP indication.

    成人长期使用吸入糖皮质激素(ICS)治疗哮喘,是否增加肺炎和口腔念珠菌的风险?对比 COPD 患者,风险有什么不同?In adults on long-term inhaled corticosteroids (ICS) for asthma, does ICS increase the risk of pneumonia and oral candidiasis? How does the risk differ vs COPD patients?
    🤖 选 LLM:看它的原话 + Anchor 校正 + 审计(每个 LLM 同一锁定 prompt)Pick an LLM: its verbatim + Anchor correction + audit (same locked prompt to each)
    Ⓐ未矫正 · DeepSeek v4 Pro 原话Uncorrected · DeepSeek v4 Pro
    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    根据现有医学知识,成人长期使用吸入糖皮质激素(ICS)治疗哮喘,确实会增加肺炎和口腔念珠菌(鹅口疮)的风险,但风险程度与慢性阻塞性肺疾病(COPD)患者有所不同。

    对于哮喘患者,长期使用 ICS 与肺炎风险增加的相关性相对较低,尤其是与 COPD 患者相比。多项研究显示,ICS 在哮喘患者中导致肺炎的风险升高幅度较小,且通常不显著增加严重肺炎的发生率。口腔念珠菌的风险则与 ICS 的局部沉积有关,尤其是在使用后未及时漱口的情况下,但这一风险通常可控。

    相比之下,COPD 患者长期使用 ICS 时,肺炎风险增加更为明确和显著。

    总结来说,两种疾病患者使用 ICS 均需注意口腔念珠菌的预防(如用药后漱口),但肺炎风险在 COPD 患者中更高,而哮喘患者相对安全。    Based on current medical knowledge, long-term ICS for asthma in adults does increase the risk of pneumonia and oral candidiasis (thrush), but the magnitude differs from COPD.

    In asthma, the association between long-term ICS and pneumonia is relatively low, especially compared with COPD. Multiple studies suggest the increase in pneumonia risk from ICS in asthma is small and usually does not significantly raise the incidence of serious pneumonia. Oral candidiasis relates to local ICS deposition, particularly without rinsing, but is generally manageable.

    By contrast, in COPD the increase in pneumonia risk with long-term ICS is clearer and more pronounced.

    In summary, both populations need candidiasis prevention (e.g. rinsing), but pneumonia risk is higher in COPD, while asthma is relatively safe.
    ⒷAnchor 校正版 · KB 锚定Anchor-corrected · KB-grounded修订Track净版Clean
    成人长期使用 ICS 治疗哮喘,确实增加肺炎和口腔念珠菌风险。

    ICS 在哮喘患者中导致肺炎的风险升高幅度较小,且通常不显著ICS 相关肺炎风险为 RR 1.49(95% CI 1.38–1.61)— 增加约 49%,统计显著量级(该 NMA 未单独区分哮喘/COPD 亚组,故不可断言哮喘相对安全肺炎 RR 1.49 + 口腔念珠菌 RR 2.29(2.01–2.60)同样适用,绝对风险或低于 COPD 但并非可忽略方向/安全)。

    (原文全程零引用、零 PMID)Anchor 锚定:PMID 41241409(EV_f3af05c48a,BMJ Evidence-Based Medicine 2026,tier 1 NMA,确定性 High)— NCBI 核验为真引用    Long-term ICS for asthma does increase pneumonia and oral candidiasis risk.

    the increase in pneumonia risk from ICS in asthma is small and usually not significantICS-related pneumonia risk is RR 1.49 (95% CI 1.38–1.61) — ~49% increase, statistically significantmagnitude (the NMA does not separate asthma vs COPD subgroups, so asthma cannot be called relatively safeRR 1.49 for pneumonia + RR 2.29 (2.01–2.60) for candidiasis still apply; absolute risk may be lower than COPD but is not negligibledirection/safety).

    (original gives no citation, no PMID)Anchor grounds: PMID 41241409 (EV_f3af05c48a, BMJ Evidence-Based Medicine 2026, tier-1 NMA, High certainty) — NCBI-verified realcitation
    在成人哮喘或 COPD 患者中,长期使用 ICS 与肺炎风险增加约 49% 相关(RR 1.49, 95% CI 1.38–1.61),并与口腔念珠菌风险增加相关(RR 2.29, 95% CI 2.01–2.60)[BMJ Evidence-Based Medicine 2026 NMA · 129 研究 · n=120,900 · PMID 41241409 · 确定性 High]。该风险需与 ICS 减少急性加重的获益权衡:哮喘患者 ICS 获益通常远大于风险,应用最低有效剂量并用药后漱口;COPD 患者应严格筛选(频繁急性加重 + 血嗜酸 ≥300)。不应因担心肺炎而擅自停用 ICS(尤其哮喘,可诱发急性发作)。证据缺口:该 NMA 未分哮喘/COPD 亚组,也无氟替卡松 vs 布地奈德的直接头对头肺炎风险比较。In adults with asthma or COPD, long-term ICS is associated with ~49% higher pneumonia risk (RR 1.49, 95% CI 1.38–1.61) and higher oral candidiasis risk (RR 2.29, 95% CI 2.01–2.60) [BMJ Evidence-Based Medicine 2026 NMA · 129 studies · n=120,900 · PMID 41241409 · High certainty]. This must be weighed against ICS reducing exacerbations: in asthma the benefit usually far outweighs the risk (use lowest effective dose + rinse after use); in COPD, restrict to frequent exacerbators + blood eosinophils ≥300. Do not stop ICS over pneumonia fear (especially in asthma — may trigger an attack). Evidence gap: the NMA does not separate asthma vs COPD subgroups, nor compare fluticasone vs budesonide head-to-head for pneumonia.
