    Ⓐ未矫正 · OpenEvidence 原话Uncorrected · OpenEvidence
    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    肺炎风险:成人哮喘长期用 ICS,肺炎风险较非使用者无显著增加;一项哮喘试验 Meta 分析示 ICS vs 安慰剂肺炎 OR 1.03(95% CI 0.89–1.20)。COPD 则不同:TORCH 示氟替卡松 500 µg 每日两次使严重肺炎事件 RR 1.64(1.33–2.01);COPD 试验 Meta 分析合并 OR 1.62(1.46–1.80),氟替卡松>布地奈德、高剂量风险更高。

    口腔念珠菌:哮喘与 COPD 均剂量依赖性增加,发生率 5–15%;储雾罐、漱口、降剂量可减。

    结论:哮喘 ICS 不显著增肺炎风险但增念珠菌(5–15%);COPD ICS 增肺炎约 60%(RR 1.62)。

    参考文献:[1] Cates CJ 等 Cochrane 2013 PMID 23744625 · [2] O'Byrne PM 等 AJRCCM 2011 PMID 20889908 · [3] Calverley PM 等 TORCH NEJM 2007 PMID 17314337 · [4] Kew KM 等 Cochrane 2014 PMID 24615270 · [5] Suissa S 等 Thorax 2013 PMID 24130228 · [6] Singanayagam A 等《激素抑制抗病毒免疫…COPD》Nat Commun 2018 PMID 29884832 · [7] Rachelefsky GS 等 AAAI 2007 PMID 17378253 · [8] Buhl R 等 Allergy 2006 PMID 16629778。
    自评信心:High。    Pneumonia risk: in adults with asthma on long-term ICS, pneumonia risk is not significantly increased; an asthma-trial meta-analysis found ICS vs placebo pneumonia OR 1.03 (95% CI 0.89–1.20). COPD differs: TORCH showed fluticasone 500 µg twice daily raised serious pneumonia events RR 1.64 (1.33–2.01); a COPD meta-analysis pooled OR 1.62 (1.46–1.80), higher with fluticasone vs budesonide and higher doses.

    Oral candidiasis: dose-dependently increased in both asthma and COPD, incidence 5–15%; reduced by spacers, mouth-rinsing, lower doses.

    Bottom line: in asthma ICS does not meaningfully increase pneumonia risk but increases candidiasis (5–15%); in COPD ICS increases pneumonia ~60% (RR 1.62).

    References: [1] Cates CJ et al Cochrane 2013 PMID 23744625 · [2] O'Byrne PM et al AJRCCM 2011 PMID 20889908 · [3] Calverley PM et al TORCH NEJM 2007 PMID 17314337 · [4] Kew KM et al Cochrane 2014 PMID 24615270 · [5] Suissa S et al Thorax 2013 PMID 24130228 · [6] Singanayagam A et al 'Corticosteroid suppression of antiviral immunity…COPD' Nat Commun 2018 PMID 29884832 · [7] Rachelefsky GS et al AAAI 2007 PMID 17378253 · [8] Buhl R et al Allergy 2006 PMID 16629778.
    Confidence self-rating: High.
    ⒷAnchor 校正版 · KB 锚定Anchor-corrected · KB-grounded修订Track净版Clean
    OpenEvidence(医学 RAG)在本题表现明显优于通用 LLM:临床区分正确(哮喘 ICS 肺炎 OR 1.03 不显著 vs COPD OR 1.62 显著),且 8 个引用中 7 个经 NCBI 核验为真(TORCH 17314337、Suissa 24130228、O'Byrne 20889908、Kew 24615270、Cates 23744625、Rachelefsky 17378253、Buhl 16629778)。

    [6] Singanayagam《激素抑制抗病毒免疫…COPD》Nat Commun 2018 PMID 29884832PMID 29884832 经 NCBI 核验实为「无接触式经平面照明快照成像」(Sci Rep 2018,光学成像) — 与 COPD/激素免疫无关,引用造假,已删引用造假。

    (各效应量未与统一 KB 来源对齐)Anchor 锚定 KB:BMJ Evidence-Based Medicine 2026 网络 Meta 分析(PMID 41241409,n=120,900,129 研究)肺炎 RR 1.49(1.38–1.61)、口腔念珠菌 RR 2.29(2.01–2.60)。OpenEvidence 的哮喘/COPD 亚组拆分与之一致,且比该 NMA 更细(NMA 未分哮喘/COPD 亚组)证据锚定。    OpenEvidence (a medical RAG model) clearly outperforms general LLMs here: the clinical distinction is correct (asthma ICS pneumonia OR 1.03 NS vs COPD OR 1.62 significant), and 7 of its 8 citations are NCBI-verified real (TORCH 17314337, Suissa 24130228, O'Byrne 20889908, Kew 24615270, Cates 23744625, Rachelefsky 17378253, Buhl 16629778).

    [6] Singanayagam 'Corticosteroid suppression of antiviral immunity…COPD' Nat Commun 2018 PMID 29884832PMID 29884832 is NCBI-verified to be 'Contact-free trans-pars-planar illumination enables snapshot imaging' (Sci Rep 2018, optical imaging) — unrelated to COPD/steroid immunity, fabricated, deletedfabricated cite.

    (effect sizes not aligned to one unified KB source)Anchor grounds in KB: the BMJ Evidence-Based Medicine 2026 network meta-analysis (PMID 41241409, n=120,900, 129 studies) — pneumonia RR 1.49 (1.38–1.61), oral candidiasis RR 2.29 (2.01–2.60). OpenEvidence's asthma/COPD subgroup split is consistent with it and more granular (the NMA does not separate asthma vs COPD)evidence grounding.
    ICS 的肺炎风险有哮喘 / COPD 之分:哮喘患者 ICS 不显著增加肺炎风险(Meta 分析 OR 1.03, 0.89–1.20),COPD 患者则显著增加约 60%(TORCH 严重肺炎 RR 1.64, 1.33–2.01;Meta 分析 OR 1.62, 1.46–1.80)。两类患者 ICS 均剂量依赖性增加口腔念珠菌(5–15%),可用储雾罐+漱口预防。Anchor KB 的 BMJ EBM 2026 NMA(PMID 41241409)给出合并肺炎 RR 1.49、念珠菌 RR 2.29,与上述方向一致。
    临床建议:哮喘患者不应因担心肺炎而停 ICS(获益远大于风险);COPD 应限于频繁急性加重+高嗜酸者并用最低有效剂量。证据缺口:缺少氟替卡松 vs 布地奈德头对头肺炎风险比较。    ICS pneumonia risk is asthma- vs COPD-specific: in asthma ICS does not significantly raise pneumonia risk (meta-analysis OR 1.03, 0.89–1.20), while in COPD it raises it ~60% (TORCH serious pneumonia RR 1.64, 1.33–2.01; meta-analysis OR 1.62, 1.46–1.80). Both populations get dose-dependent oral candidiasis (5–15%), preventable with spacers + rinsing. Anchor's KB BMJ EBM 2026 NMA (PMID 41241409) gives pooled pneumonia RR 1.49 and candidiasis RR 2.29, concordant in direction.
    Clinical advice: in asthma do not stop ICS over pneumonia fear (benefit far outweighs risk); in COPD restrict to frequent exacerbators with high eosinophils at the lowest effective dose. Evidence gap: no head-to-head fluticasone vs budesonide pneumonia-risk comparison.

    Ⓐ未矫正 · ChatGPT 5.5 原话Uncorrected · ChatGPT 5.5
    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    主题:哮喘 vs COPD 吸入性糖皮质激素(ICS)——肺炎与口腔念珠菌病风险

    哮喘 · 口腔念珠菌病:长期 ICS 明确增加口腔念珠菌病;一项 meta 分析报告 ICS vs 安慰剂 ~OR 3.6(95% CI 2.1–6.3)[1 Rachelefsky AAAI 2007 PMID 17378254]。局部激素效应,剂量/装置依赖;漱口与储雾罐可降低风险。

    哮喘 · 肺炎:信号远弱于 COPD;布地奈德哮喘试验汇总未显示令人信服的肺炎增多[2 O'Byrne AJRCCM 2011 PMID 20889905]。

    COPD · 肺炎:风险一致;一项 COPD meta 分析报告 ICS 肺炎 ~RR 1.60(1.33–1.92),严重肺炎 RR 1.71(1.46–1.99)[3 Singh & Loke Arch Intern Med 2009 PMID 19204211];TORCH 氟替卡松组肺炎更多[4 Calverley TORCH NEJM 2007 PMID 17314337]。

    结论:哮喘 ICS = 获益强、念珠菌病明确、肺炎低/不确定;COPD ICS = 肺炎风险确立(尤其氟替卡松)。
    置信度:中等。    Topic: ICS in asthma vs COPD — pneumonia & oral candidiasis risk

    Asthma · oral candidiasis: Long-term ICS clearly increases oral candidiasis; a meta-analysis reported ICS vs placebo ~OR 3.6 (95% CI 2.1–6.3) [1 Rachelefsky AAAI 2007 PMID 17378254]. Local steroid effect, dose/device dependent; rinsing and spacers reduce risk.

    Asthma · pneumonia: Signal much weaker than in COPD; pooled budesonide asthma trials did not show convincing pneumonia excess [2 O'Byrne AJRCCM 2011 PMID 20889905].

    COPD · pneumonia: Risk consistent; a COPD meta-analysis reported ICS pneumonia ~RR 1.60 (1.33–1.92), serious pneumonia RR 1.71 (1.46–1.99) [3 Singh & Loke Arch Intern Med 2009 PMID 19204211]; TORCH fluticasone arms had more pneumonia [4 Calverley TORCH NEJM 2007 PMID 17314337].

    Bottom line: asthma ICS = strong benefit, clear candidiasis, low/uncertain pneumonia; COPD ICS = established pneumonia risk (esp. fluticasone).
    Confidence: Moderate.
    ⒷAnchor 校正版 · KB 锚定Anchor-corrected · KB-grounded修订Track净版Clean
    临床判断方向正确:ICS 在哮喘中明确升高口腔念珠菌病、在 COPD 中确立肺炎风险(尤其氟替卡松)。四条引用中两条 PMID 经 NCBI 核验为「错号引文」——病、刊、年份对得上,但 PMID 差一位,指向了无关论文。Anchor 逐条核验并以 KB 锚定。[1 Rachelefsky AAAI 2007 PMID 17378254][1 Rachelefsky 口咽不良事件 meta 分析,Ann Allergy Asthma Immunol 2007,真实 PMID 17378253]引用造假(错号)[2 O'Byrne AJRCCM 2011 PMID 20889905][2 O'Byrne 哮喘患者 ICS 肺炎风险,Am J Respir Crit Care Med 2011,真实 PMID 20889908]引用造假(错号)[3 Singh & Loke,COPD ICS 肺炎 meta 分析,Arch Intern Med 2009,PMID 19204211 ✓]引用正确[4 Calverley TORCH,N Engl J Med 2007,PMID 17314337 ✓]引用正确Clinical direction is correct: ICS clearly raises oral candidiasis in asthma and carries an established pneumonia risk in COPD (esp. fluticasone). Two of four PMIDs are NCBI-verified as off-by-one wrong-paper cites — right disease/journal/year, but the PMID lands one digit off on an unrelated article. Anchor verifies each and grounds them in the KB.[1 Rachelefsky AAAI 2007 PMID 17378254][1 Rachelefsky oropharyngeal adverse-events meta-analysis, Ann Allergy Asthma Immunol 2007, REAL PMID 17378253]FABRICATED (off-by-one)[2 O'Byrne AJRCCM 2011 PMID 20889905][2 O'Byrne 'Risks of pneumonia in patients with asthma taking inhaled corticosteroids', Am J Respir Crit Care Med 2011, REAL PMID 20889908]FABRICATED (off-by-one)[3 Singh & Loke, COPD ICS pneumonia meta-analysis, Arch Intern Med 2009, PMID 19204211 ✓]CONFIRMED[4 Calverley TORCH, N Engl J Med 2007, PMID 17314337 ✓]CONFIRMED
    ICS 在哮喘与 COPD 中均增加局部与肺部不良事件,但量级与确定性不同。汇总最大规模证据(Anchor KB:BMJ Evidence-Based Medicine 2026 网络 meta 分析,PMID 41241409,n=120,900):肺炎 RR 1.49(95% CI 1.38–1.61),口腔念珠菌病 RR 2.29(95% CI 2.01–2.60)。COPD 肺炎风险证据更一致(Singh & Loke,PMID 19204211;TORCH 氟替卡松组肺炎更多,PMID 17314337),且氟替卡松信号强于布地奈德;哮喘的肺炎信号相对更弱、更不确定。

    获益—风险:哮喘中 ICS 控制气道炎症的获益明确,念珠菌病为可控的局部副作用(漱口、储雾罐、最低有效剂量可减轻);COPD 中需在加重风险下降与肺炎风险升高间个体化权衡。
    证据缺口:哮喘人群 ICS 肺炎的随机证据有限,多依赖观察性/事后数据;跨装置、跨剂量的头对头比较不足。    ICS increases both local and pulmonary adverse events in asthma and COPD, but the magnitude and certainty differ. Pooling the largest body of evidence (Anchor KB: BMJ Evidence-Based Medicine 2026 network meta-analysis, PMID 41241409, n=120,900): pneumonia RR 1.49 (95% CI 1.38–1.61), oral candidiasis RR 2.29 (95% CI 2.01–2.60). The COPD pneumonia evidence is more consistent (Singh & Loke, PMID 19204211; excess pneumonia in TORCH fluticasone arms, PMID 17314337), and the fluticasone signal exceeds budesonide; the asthma pneumonia signal is comparatively weaker and less certain.

    Benefit–risk: In asthma the airway-inflammation benefit of ICS is clear and candidiasis is a manageable local effect (rinsing, spacers, lowest effective dose mitigate it); in COPD the lower exacerbation risk must be weighed against higher pneumonia risk on an individual basis.
    Evidence gap: Randomized evidence for ICS pneumonia specifically in asthma is limited and leans on observational/post-hoc data; head-to-head comparisons across devices and doses are sparse.

    Ⓐ未矫正 · Claude 4.8 原话Uncorrected · Claude 4.8
    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    COPD: ICS 明确增加肺炎风险。
    TORCH:氟替卡松/沙美特罗组肺炎 ~19.6% vs 安慰剂 ~12.3%(3 年)[1]。
    Cochrane(Kew & Seniukovich):氟替卡松严重肺炎 ~OR 1.78(95% CI ~1.50–2.12),布地奈德较弱 [2]。剂量依赖,氟替卡松更明显;无死亡率增加。
    哮喘:肺炎信号弱/不一致,无稳健 RCT 证据显示有意义的超额风险;远小于 COPD。
    口腔念珠菌病:两者均增加,剂量依赖的局部效应,RR ~2–3 [3];储雾罐、最低剂量、漱口可降低风险。
    引用:[1] Calverley TORCH NEJM 2007 PMID 17314337;[2] Kew & Seniukovich Cochrane 2014(PMID 省略 — 不确定);[3] 口腔念珠菌病 / Cochrane ICS 不良反应综述(PMID 省略以避免造假)。
    置信度:中 — 主动省略两个 PMID,而非冒险造假。    COPD: ICS clearly raise pneumonia risk.
    TORCH: fluticasone/salmeterol pneumonia ~19.6% vs ~12.3% placebo over 3y [1].
    Cochrane (Kew & Seniukovich): fluticasone serious pneumonia ~OR 1.78 (95% CI ~1.50–2.12), weaker for budesonide [2]. Dose-dependent, larger for fluticasone; no mortality increase.
    Asthma: pneumonia signal weak/inconsistent, no robust RCT evidence of meaningful excess; far smaller than COPD.
    Oral candidiasis: increased in both, dose-dependent local effect, RR ~2–3 range [3]; reduced by spacer, lowest dose, rinsing.
    Refs: [1] Calverley TORCH NEJM 2007 PMID 17314337; [2] Kew & Seniukovich Cochrane 2014 (PMID omitted—not certain); [3] Oral candidiasis / Cochrane ICS adverse review (PMID omitted to avoid fabrication).
    Confidence: Moderate — deliberately omitted two PMIDs rather than risk fabricating.

    Anchor 在 NCBI 逐条核验了 Claude 的引用,并对照 KB 锚定每个效应量。结论方向:这是最谨慎的一份回答 —— Claude 主动省略两个不确定的 PMID,而非冒险造假,且唯一给出的 PMID(TORCH)经核验正确。Anchor 确认这一引用,并从 KB 补齐两个被主动省略的来源。[1] Calverley TORCH NEJM 2007 PMID 17314337 ✓ 已核验正确引用正确[2] Kew & Seniukovich Cochrane 2014(PMID 省略 — 不确定)[2] Kew & Seniukovich Cochrane 2014,PMID 24615270(Anchor 从 KB 补齐;Claude 谨慎省略,正确做法)诚实省略·已补齐[3] 口腔念珠菌病 / Cochrane ICS 不良反应综述(PMID 省略以避免造假)[3] 口腔念珠菌病效应量 → 锚定 BMJ EBM 2026 NMA,PMID 41241409:念珠菌病 RR 2.29(2.01–2.60)(Claude 谨慎省略,Anchor 从 KB 补齐)诚实省略·已补齐补充 KB 锚定:同一 BMJ EBM 2026 NMA(PMID 41241409,n=120,900)给出 ICS 肺炎 RR 1.49(1.38–1.61),与 Claude「COPD 肺炎风险明确、剂量依赖、氟替卡松更明显、哮喘信号弱」的临床判断方向完全一致。Claude 此例临床表述无误,仅在引用克制度上优于其他模型。Anchor NCBI-verified each of Claude's citations and KB-anchored every effect size. Anchor confirms that cite and fills the two deliberately-omitted sources from the KB.[1] Calverley TORCH NEJM 2007 PMID 17314337 ✓ verified correctCONFIRMED[2] Kew & Seniukovich Cochrane 2014 (PMID omitted—not certain)[2] Kew & Seniukovich Cochrane 2014, PMID 24615270 (Anchor fills from KB; Claude cautiously omitted — correct call)HONEST OMISSION · FILLED[3] Oral candidiasis / Cochrane ICS adverse review (PMID omitted to avoid fabrication)[3] Oral candidiasis effect → anchored to BMJ EBM 2026 NMA, PMID 41241409: candidiasis RR 2.29 (2.01–2.60) (Claude cautiously omitted; Anchor fills from KB)HONEST OMISSION · FILLEDAdded KB anchor: the same BMJ EBM 2026 NMA (PMID 41241409, n=120,900) gives ICS pneumonia RR 1.49 (1.38–1.61), fully consistent with Claude's clinical read (COPD pneumonia clear & dose-dependent, larger for fluticasone, asthma signal weak). Claude's clinical content here is sound; it simply out-disciplined the other models on citation restraint.
    Anchor KB 对齐结论(以 BMJ EBM 2026 NMA 锚定,PMID 41241409,n=120,900):
    • ICS 相关肺炎 RR 1.49(95% CI 1.38–1.61) —— 风险在 COPD 中确立、剂量依赖,氟替卡松信号强于布地奈德;哮喘中信号弱且不一致。
    • ICS 相关口腔念珠菌病 RR 2.29(95% CI 2.01–2.60) —— 局部、剂量依赖效应,储雾罐 + 漱口 + 最低有效剂量可降低风险。
    • 支持性试验/综述:TORCH(PMID 17314337,氟替卡松臂肺炎更多)、Kew & Seniukovich Cochrane 2014(PMID 24615270,氟替卡松严重肺炎风险)。
    获益–风险一句话:哮喘中 ICS 获益明确(控制、急性发作),念珠菌病为可控局部风险;COPD 中 ICS 在选定表型获益与已确立的肺炎风险之间权衡。
    证据缺口:哮喘肺炎风险缺乏充分把握度的前瞻 RCT;念珠菌病的器械/剂量调节效应多来自间接证据。    Anchor KB-aligned summary (anchored to BMJ EBM 2026 NMA, PMID 41241409, n=120,900):
    • ICS-related pneumonia RR 1.49 (95% CI 1.38–1.61) — risk established in COPD, dose-dependent, fluticasone signal stronger than budesonide; weak/inconsistent in asthma.
    • ICS-related oral candidiasis RR 2.29 (95% CI 2.01–2.60) — local, dose-dependent effect; spacer + rinsing + lowest effective dose reduce risk.
    • Supporting trials/reviews: TORCH (PMID 17314337, more pneumonia in fluticasone arms); Kew & Seniukovich Cochrane 2014 (PMID 24615270, fluticasone serious-pneumonia risk).
    Benefit–risk in one line: in asthma ICS benefit is clear (control, exacerbations) with candidiasis a manageable local risk; in COPD ICS trades selected-phenotype benefit against an established pneumonia risk.
    Evidence gap: no adequately powered prospective RCT for asthma pneumonia risk; device/dose modulation of candidiasis rests largely on indirect evidence.

    携带至少一个 F508del 等位基因的囊性纤维化患者,2025 年最有效的 CFTR 调节剂方案是什么?请将 elexacaftor-tezacaftor-ivacaftor 与任何更新的每日一次三联组合比较(ppFEV1 的 MD 含 95% CI),说明获批状态与关键试验,并附 PMID。For people with cystic fibrosis carrying ≥1 F508del allele, what is the most effective CFTR modulator regimen as of 2025? Compare elexacaftor-tezacaftor-ivacaftor with any newer once-daily triple combination (ppFEV1 MD with 95% CI), state approval status and pivotal trials, with PMIDs.
    🤖 选 LLM:看它的原话 + Anchor 校正 + 审计(每个 LLM 同一锁定 prompt)Pick an LLM: its verbatim + Anchor correction + audit (same locked prompt to each)
    Ⓐ未矫正 · DeepSeek v4 Pro 原话Uncorrected · DeepSeek v4 Pro
    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    问:携带至少一个 F508del 等位基因的囊性纤维化患者,2025 年最有效的 CFTR 调节剂方案是什么?将 elexacaftor-tezacaftor-ivacaftor(ETI)与更新的每日一次三联方案比较 ppFEV1(MD + 95% CI),说明审批状态并列出关键试验与 PMID。

    截至 2025 年,F508del 患者的最有效方案仍以三联调节剂为基础。既有标准是 ETI(Trikafta/Kaftrio),2019 年获批,关键试验 VX17-445-102(PMID 31697873)与 VX17-445-103(PMID 31697874)显示稳健的 FEV1 改善。

    更新的每日一次三联为 vanzacaftor-tezacaftor-deutivacaftor(VTD,Alyftrek),FDA 于 2024-12-20 获批(≥6 岁,至少一个 F508del 或其他应答突变)。在头对头 SKYLINE 102 试验(PMID 39141873,称 NEJM 2024-08,DOI 10.1056/NEJMoa2403028)中,VTD 对 ETI 非劣效:第 24 周 ppFEV1 绝对变化差值 +0.2 个百分点(95% CI −0.7 至 1.0),满足非劣效界值 −3.0。

    结论:无更新三联在 ppFEV1 上证明优于 ETI;VTD 非劣效,优势在每日一次给药便利性。DeepSeek 自承对 39141873 不确定("I'll assign PMID 39141873 for now, but I should be careful")。    Q: For CF patients carrying at least one F508del allele, what is the most effective CFTR modulator regimen as of 2025? Compare elexacaftor-tezacaftor-ivacaftor (ETI) against a newer once-daily triple on ppFEV1 (MD + 95% CI), state approval status, name pivotal trials with PMIDs.

    As of 2025 the most effective regimen for F508del patients remains triple-modulator-based. The established standard is ETI (Trikafta/Kaftrio), approved 2019, with pivotal trials VX17-445-102 (PMID 31697873) and VX17-445-103 (PMID 31697874) showing robust FEV1 improvement.

    The newer once-daily triple is vanzacaftor-tezacaftor-deutivacaftor (VTD, Alyftrek), FDA-approved 2024-12-20 (age ≥6, at least one F508del or other responsive mutation). In the head-to-head SKYLINE 102 trial (PMID 39141873, cited as NEJM 2024-08, DOI 10.1056/NEJMoa2403028), VTD was non-inferior to ETI: difference in absolute ppFEV1 change at week 24 of +0.2 percentage points (95% CI −0.7 to 1.0), meeting the −3.0 non-inferiority margin.

    Conclusion: No newer triple has demonstrated ppFEV1 superiority over ETI; VTD is non-inferior, its advantage being once-daily dosing convenience. DeepSeek self-flagged uncertainty on 39141873 ("I'll assign PMID 39141873 for now, but I should be careful").
    ⒷAnchor 校正版 · KB 锚定Anchor-corrected · KB-grounded修订Track净版Clean
    问题:F508del 囊性纤维化患者 2025 年最有效的 CFTR 调节剂方案 — ETI 对比更新的每日一次三联(ppFEV1 MD + 95% CI、审批状态、关键试验)。DeepSeek 的临床判断正确:既有标准为 ETI,更新方案为 vanzacaftor-tezacaftor-deutivacaftor(Alyftrek),2024-12 FDA 获批,头对头试验中对 ETI 非劣效。问题全在 PMID 引用造假。头对头 SKYLINE 102 试验(PMID 39141873,NEJM 2024-08,DOI 10.1056/NEJMoa2403028)头对头 SKYLINE VX20-121-102/103 试验(PMID 39756424,Lancet Respir Med 2025;13(3):256-271)引用造假VX17-445-103(PMID 31697874)VX17-445-102 主试验(PMID 31697873,Middleton, NEJM 2019)— ETI 证据以此为锚引用造假VTD 对 ETI 非劣效:第 24 周 ppFEV1 差值 +0.2 个百分点(95% CI −0.7 至 1.0)非劣效方向与 KB 一致;严谨证据应锚定真实 SKYLINE(39756424)及 CFTR 调节剂 NMA(40102290:vanza-三联 vs 安慰剂 ppFEV1 MD +12.78,95% CI 6.41–19.15)待溯源已确认正确:PMID 31697873(Middleton, ETI 单 F508del, NEJM 2019)— 真实且切题,保留。Question: Most effective CFTR modulator regimen in 2025 for F508del CF — ETI vs a newer once-daily triple (ppFEV1 MD + 95% CI, approval status, pivotal trials).DeepSeek's clinical reasoning is correct: the standard is ETI, the newer regimen is vanzacaftor-tezacaftor-deutivacaftor (Alyftrek), FDA-approved 2024-12, non-inferior to ETI head-to-head. Every problem is a fabricated PMID.head-to-head SKYLINE 102 trial (PMID 39141873, NEJM 2024-08, DOI 10.1056/NEJMoa2403028)head-to-head SKYLINE VX20-121-102/103 trial (PMID 39756424, Lancet Respir Med 2025;13(3):256-271)FABRICATEDVX17-445-103 (PMID 31697874)VX17-445-102 pivotal trial (PMID 31697873, Middleton, NEJM 2019) — ETI evidence anchored hereFABRICATEDVTD non-inferior to ETI: ppFEV1 difference at week 24 of +0.2 percentage points (95% CI −0.7 to 1.0)non-inferiority direction matches KB; rigorous evidence should anchor to the real SKYLINE (39756424) and the CFTR-modulator NMA (40102290: vanza-triple vs placebo ppFEV1 MD +12.78, 95% CI 6.41–19.15)GROUNDConfirmed correct: PMID 31697873 (Middleton, ETI for single F508del, NEJM 2019) — real and on-topic, retained.
    截至 2025 年,携带至少一个 F508del 的 CF 患者:既有标准为 elexacaftor-tezacaftor-ivacaftor(ETI;关键试验 Middleton, PMID 31697873)。更新的每日一次三联 vanzacaftor-tezacaftor-deutivacaftor(品牌 Alyftrek,2024-12 FDA 获批)在头对头 SKYLINE VX20-121-102/103 试验(PMID 39756424,Lancet Respir Med 2025)中对 ETI 非劣效,ppFEV1 差异极小。CFTR 调节剂网状 meta 分析(PMID 40102290,Lung 2025)显示 vanza-三联 vs 安慰剂 ppFEV1 MD +12.78(95% CI 6.41–19.15),与 ETI 量级相当。获益-风险:VTD 提供每日一次给药便利,疗效与 ETI 相当;两者均需肝功能监测。证据缺口:尚无证明任何更新三联在 ppFEV1 上优于 ETI 的头对头优效数据,长期结局(肺移植/生存)与稀有非-F508del 基因型证据有限。注:Anchor 呈现证据,不开具具体剂量/给药途径/疗程。As of 2025, for CF patients carrying at least one F508del: the established standard is elexacaftor-tezacaftor-ivacaftor (ETI; pivotal trial Middleton, PMID 31697873). The newer once-daily triple vanzacaftor-tezacaftor-deutivacaftor (brand Alyftrek, FDA-approved 2024-12) was non-inferior to ETI in the head-to-head SKYLINE VX20-121-102/103 trials (PMID 39756424, Lancet Respir Med 2025), with minimal ppFEV1 difference. A CFTR-modulator network meta-analysis (PMID 40102290, Lung 2025) shows vanza-triple vs placebo ppFEV1 MD +12.78 (95% CI 6.41–19.15), comparable in magnitude to ETI. Benefit-risk: VTD offers once-daily dosing convenience with efficacy equivalent to ETI; both require liver-function monitoring. Evidence gap: no head-to-head superiority data show any newer triple beats ETI on ppFEV1, and long-term outcomes (lung transplant/survival) and rare non-F508del genotypes remain under-studied. Note: Anchor presents evidence and does not prescribe a specific dose/route/duration.

    Ⓐ未矫正 · Gemini 3 Flash 原话Uncorrected · Gemini 3 Flash
    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    截至 2025 年,携带至少一个 F508del 的 CF 最有效方案是每日一次三联 vanzaftor-tezacaftor-deutivacaftor [1]。

    ppFEV1:在 3 期 RIDGELINE/SKYLINE 试验中,该三联相对 ETI 非劣效,ppFEV1 变化 MD 约 0.2 个百分点(95% CI −1.1 至 1.5) [1];汗氯下降更优(MD −5.8 至 −9.5 mmol/L)[1]。
    对照 ETI(Trikafta):AURORA F/MF ppFEV1 MD 14.3 [2]、F/F MD 10.0 [3]。

    获批:FDA 于 2025-01 批准该三联(商品名 Trizavia),用于 ≥6 岁、携带至少一个 F508del 者。

    参考文献:[1] Walsworth M, et al. Vanzaftor–Tezacaftor–Deutivacaftor in CF. N Engl J Med. 2024;391(17):1573-1584. PMID: 39340354. [2] Middleton PG, et al. ETI for CF with a Single Phe508del. N Engl J Med. 2019;381(19):1809-1819. PMID: 31697873. [3] Heijerman HGM, et al. ETI homozygous Phe508del. Lancet. 2019;394:1940-1948. PMID: 31679846.
    自评信心:High。    As of 2025, the most effective regimen for CF with ≥1 F508del is the once-daily triple vanzaftor-tezacaftor-deutivacaftor [1].

    ppFEV1: in the phase-3 RIDGELINE/SKYLINE trials it was non-inferior to ETI, ppFEV1 change MD ≈ 0.2 points (95% CI −1.1 to 1.5) [1]; sweat-chloride reduction was superior (MD −5.8 to −9.5 mmol/L) [1].
    For comparison, ETI (Trikafta): AURORA F/MF ppFEV1 MD 14.3 [2], F/F MD 10.0 [3].

    Approval: FDA approved this triple (brand Trizavia) in 2025-01 for ages ≥6 with ≥1 F508del.

    References: [1] Walsworth M, et al. Vanzaftor–Tezacaftor–Deutivacaftor in CF. N Engl J Med. 2024;391(17):1573-1584. PMID: 39340354. [2] Middleton PG, et al. ETI for CF with a Single Phe508del. N Engl J Med. 2019;381(19):1809-1819. PMID: 31697873. [3] Heijerman HGM, et al. ETI homozygous Phe508del. Lancet. 2019;394:1940-1948. PMID: 31679846.
    Confidence self-rating: High.

    截至 2025 年,vanzacaftor-tezacaftor-deutivacaftor(商品名 Alyftrek,FDA 2024-12 获批)是携带 ≥1 个 F508del 的 CF 的新一代每日一次 CFTR 三联。相对 ETI 头对头非劣效(ppFEV1 MD≈0.2,95% CI −1.1 至 1.5),汗氯下降更优;相对安慰剂,2025 网络 Meta 分析示 ppFEV1 MD +12.78(6.41–19.15)[PMID 40102290]。头对头试验见 Lancet Respir Med 2025(PMID 39756424);ETI 仍为对照标准(Middleton, PMID 31697873)。
    获益–风险:选用主要基于每日一次便利 + CFTR 功能(汗氯)改善,而非 ppFEV1 的优效。证据缺口:长期(加重率、生存)结局与真实世界数据仍在积累。    As of 2025, vanzacaftor-tezacaftor-deutivacaftor (brand Alyftrek, FDA-approved 2024-12) is the next-gen once-daily CFTR triple for CF with ≥1 F508del. It is non-inferior to ETI head-to-head (ppFEV1 MD≈0.2, 95% CI −1.1 to 1.5) with greater sweat-chloride reduction; vs placebo the 2025 network meta-analysis shows ppFEV1 MD +12.78 (6.41–19.15) [PMID 40102290]. The head-to-head trial is in Lancet Respir Med 2025 (PMID 39756424); ETI remains the comparator standard (Middleton, PMID 31697873).
    Benefit–risk: choice is driven mainly by once-daily convenience + CFTR-function (sweat-chloride) gains, not ppFEV1 superiority. Evidence gap: long-term (exacerbation, survival) outcomes and real-world data are still accruing.

    Ⓐ未矫正 · OpenEvidence 原话Uncorrected · OpenEvidence
    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    问题:CF(≥1个F508del)下一代CFTR调节剂 — ETI vs 万扎卡托/Alyftrek
    截至2025年,ETI 仍是 ≥1个F508del 患者最有效、应用最广的 CFTR 调节剂;新近获批的每日一次万扎卡托-特扎卡托-deutivacaftor(VTD/Alyftrek)疗效非劣,且具给药便利优势。

    ETI 数据:F508del/MF ppFEV1 第4周 +13.8(12.1–15.4)、第24周 +14.3(12.7–15.8);纯合子 +10.0(7.4–12.6);急性加重 RR 0.37(0.25–0.55);汗氯 −41.8 mmol/L;192周疗效维持。
    VTD 数据:SKYLINE(VX20-121-102/103)52周 ppFEV1 非劣,汗氯下降更大(−49至−50 vs −45),每日一次,获批 ≥6岁。

    引用(给出PMID):McNally RECOVER AJRCCM 2023 PMID 37703083;Nichols AJRCCM 2022 PMID 34784492;Daines AJRCCM 2025 PMID 40209082;Daines ERJ 2023 PMID 37945033;另 Middleton / Keating SKYLINE / Safeer NMA / Hoppe RIDGELINE(未给PMID)。
    置信度:高。    Q: Next-gen CFTR modulator for CF (≥1 F508del) — ETI vs vanzacaftor/Alyftrek
    As of 2025 ETI remains the most effective and most widely used CFTR modulator for ≥1 F508del; the newer once-daily vanzacaftor-tezacaftor-deutivacaftor (VTD/Alyftrek) is non-inferior on efficacy with dosing-convenience advantages.

    ETI data: F508del/MF ppFEV1 +13.8 (12.1–15.4) at 4wk, +14.3 (12.7–15.8) at 24wk; homozygous +10.0 (7.4–12.6); exacerbations RR 0.37 (0.25–0.55); sweat chloride −41.8 mmol/L; 192-wk sustained.
    VTD data: SKYLINE (VX20-121-102/103) non-inferior ppFEV1 over 52wk, greater sweat-chloride fall (−49 to −50 vs −45), once-daily, approved ages ≥6.

    Refs (PMIDs given): McNally RECOVER AJRCCM 2023 PMID 37703083; Nichols AJRCCM 2022 PMID 34784492; Daines AJRCCM 2025 PMID 40209082; Daines ERJ 2023 PMID 37945033; plus Middleton / Keating SKYLINE / Safeer NMA / Hoppe RIDGELINE (no PMID given).
    Confidence: High.

    Anchor 锚定结论(基于 KB 核验 PMID):
    • ETI(Middleton 关键试验,PMID 31697873):F508del/MF ppFEV1 显著提升、急性加重显著下降、汗氯大幅降低 —— 截至2025年仍为 ≥1个F508del 的标准与基准方案。
    • 万扎卡托-特扎卡托-deutivacaftor(VTD/Alyftrek):头对头 SKYLINE(Keating,Lancet Respir Med 2025,PMID 39756424)显示对 ETI ppFEV1 非劣(非更优),差值约 +0.2(95%CI 约 −0.7 至 +1.1);汗氯下降更大;每日一次。KB 内 NMA(PMID 40102290)示 VTD vs 安慰剂 ppFEV1 MD +12.78(6.41–19.15)。
    • 获益–风险一句话:VTD 在肺功能上与 ETI 相当、生物标志物与依从性更优,但尚无肺功能"更优"的证据。
    • 证据缺口:VTD 长期(多年)结局、真实世界数据与更年幼/罕见基因型人群证据仍待积累;头对头多为非劣性设计。
    Anchor 仅呈现证据,不给出处方剂量/途径/疗程。    Anchor-grounded summary (KB-verified PMIDs):
    • ETI (Middleton pivotal trial, PMID 31697873): marked ppFEV1 gain, large reduction in exacerbations, large sweat-chloride fall in F508del/MF — remains the standard and benchmark for ≥1 F508del as of 2025.
    • Vanzacaftor-tezacaftor-deutivacaftor (VTD/Alyftrek): head-to-head SKYLINE (Keating, Lancet Respir Med 2025, PMID 39756424) shows it is non-inferior, not superior, to ETI on ppFEV1 (diff ~+0.2, 95%CI ~−0.7 to +1.1); greater sweat-chloride fall; once-daily. KB NMA (PMID 40102290) shows VTD vs placebo ppFEV1 MD +12.78 (6.41–19.15).
    • Benefit–risk, one line: VTD matches ETI on lung function with a biomarker and adherence edge, but there is no evidence it is superior for lung function.
    • Evidence gap: long-term (multi-year) VTD outcomes, real-world data, and evidence in younger/rare-genotype populations are still accruing; head-to-heads are largely non-inferiority designs.
    Anchor presents evidence only and does not issue a prescriptive dose/route/duration.

    问题:CF(≥1 个 F508del)的次世代 CFTR 调节剂——ETI vs vanzacaftor/Alyftrek
    对于携带 ≥1 个 F508del 的 CF,基准方案仍是 ETI(Trikafta/Kaftrio):关键 F508del/MF 试验 ppFEV1 较安慰剂 +14.3(12.7–15.8),急性加重 RR 0.37(0.25–0.55) [1]。
    更新的每日一次 vanzacaftor/tezacaftor/deutivacaftor(ALYFTREK):SKYLINE 102/103 头对头非劣效而非优效,ppFEV1 差异 +0.2(102:−0.7 至 1.1;103:−0.5 至 0.9),汗氯下降更大 [2]。
    结论:截至 2025,ETI 仍是经过验证的标准;VTD 是次世代替代——ppFEV1 相近、药片负担更低、汗氯更佳,但肺功能未证明更优。ALYFTREK 于 2024 年 12 月获 FDA 批准(≥6 岁)。
    参考:[1] Middleton NEJM 2019 PMID 31697873;[2] Keating SKYLINE 102/103 Lancet Respir Med 2025 PMID 39756424。
    置信度:中等。    Q: Next-gen CFTR modulator for CF (≥1 F508del) — ETI vs vanzacaftor/Alyftrek
    For CF with ≥1 F508del, the benchmark remains ETI (Trikafta/Kaftrio): pivotal F508del/MF trial ppFEV1 +14.3 (12.7–15.8) vs placebo, exacerbations RR 0.37 (0.25–0.55) [1].
    Newer once-daily vanzacaftor/tezacaftor/deutivacaftor (ALYFTREK): SKYLINE 102/103 head-to-head non-inferior, not superior, ppFEV1 diff +0.2 (102: −0.7 to 1.1; 103: −0.5 to 0.9), greater sweat-chloride reduction [2].
    Bottom line: as of 2025 ETI remains the proven standard; VTD is a next-gen alternative — similar ppFEV1, lower pill burden, better sweat-chloride, not proven better for lung function. ALYFTREK FDA-approved Dec 2024 (ages ≥6).
    Refs: [1] Middleton NEJM 2019 PMID 31697873; [2] Keating SKYLINE 102/103 Lancet Respir Med 2025 PMID 39756424.
    Confidence: Moderate.

    证据对齐摘要(以 KB 核验 PMID 锚定)
    • ETI(Middleton, PMID 31697873):F508del/最低功能基因型 ppFEV1 +14.3(95%CI 12.7–15.8),急性加重 RR 0.37(0.25–0.55)——确立的标准方案。
    • vanzacaftor/tezacaftor/deutivacaftor(头对头 vs ETI, PMID 39756424):ppFEV1 非劣效(差异约 +0.2,CI 跨 0),汗氯下降更大,每日一次。
    • NMA(PMID 40102290):vanzacaftor vs 安慰剂 ppFEV1 MD +12.78(6.41–19.15)。
    获益–风险一句话:VTD 在肺功能上与 ETI 相当(非优效),提供给药便利与更佳汗氯生物标志物,代价是更短的长期安全随访。

    • ETI (Middleton, PMID 31697873): F508del/minimal-function ppFEV1 +14.3 (95% CI 12.7–15.8), exacerbations RR 0.37 (0.25–0.55) — established standard.
    • vanzacaftor/tezacaftor/deutivacaftor (head-to-head vs ETI, PMID 39756424): ppFEV1 non-inferior (difference ≈ +0.2, CI spans 0), greater sweat-chloride reduction, once-daily.
    • NMA (PMID 40102290): vanzacaftor vs placebo ppFEV1 MD +12.78 (6.41–19.15).
    Benefit–risk (one line): VTD matches ETI on lung function (non-superior) and adds dosing convenience plus a better sweat-chloride biomarker, at the cost of shorter long-term safety follow-up.
    Evidence gap: no long-term (multi-year) head-to-head VTD vs ETI on hard endpoints (exacerbations, transplant, survival); non-inferiority is not the same as equivalence on long-term outcomes.

    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    问题:≥1 个 F508del 的 CF,首选 CFTR 调节剂(ETI 对比新一代 vanzacaftor/Alyftrek)。

    核心论断:截至 2025,≥1 F508del 的两种最有效方案为 ETI 与新型每日一次 vanzacaftor-tezacaftor-deutivacaftor(VTD;Alyftrek)——肺功能上头对头基本等效。
    ETI: VX17-445-102(Middleton)F508del/MF,ppFEV1 +13.8(12.1–15.4),4 周 [1];纯合子(Heijerman)ETI 对比 tez-iva +10(7.4–12.6)[2]。
    VTD: SKYLINE 102/103 对 ETI 非劣效,ppFEV1 差值 ~+0.2(~-0.7 至 +1.1),汗氯下降更大(~-2 至 -3 mmol/L 偏向 VTD),每日一次 [3]。
    结论:VTD 在 ppFEV1 上非劣效而非优效;优势在依从性 + 生物标志物。
    批准: ETI 广泛获批 ≥2 岁;VTD FDA 2024-12 ≥6 岁。

    参考文献:
    [1] Middleton NEJM 2019 PMID 31697873
    [2] Heijerman Lancet 2019 PMID 31679946
    [3] SKYLINE program 2024–2025(PMID omitted—not certain)
    置信度:中等。    Q: Preferred CFTR modulator for CF with ≥1 F508del (ETI vs next-gen vanzacaftor/Alyftrek).

    Core claims: As of 2025 the two most effective regimens for ≥1 F508del are ETI and the newer once-daily vanzacaftor-tezacaftor-deutivacaftor (VTD; Alyftrek)—head-to-head essentially equivalent on lung function.
    ETI: VX17-445-102 (Middleton) F508del/MF ppFEV1 +13.8 (12.1–15.4) at 4wk [1]; homozygous (Heijerman) ETI vs tez-iva +10 (7.4–12.6) [2].
    VTD: SKYLINE 102/103 non-inferiority vs ETI, ppFEV1 difference ~+0.2 (~-0.7 to +1.1), greater sweat-chloride (~-2 to -3 mmol/L favouring VTD), once-daily [3].
    Conclusion: VTD non-inferior not superior on ppFEV1; convenience + biomarker edge.
    Approval: ETI broadly approved ≥2y; VTD FDA Dec 2024 ≥6y.

    References:
    [1] Middleton NEJM 2019 PMID 31697873
    [2] Heijerman Lancet 2019 PMID 31679946
    [3] SKYLINE program 2024–2025 (PMID omitted—not certain)
    Confidence: Moderate.

    Anchor KB 锚定结论(CF,≥1 F508del):
    • ETI 仍是基准:F508del/最小功能基因型 ppFEV1 +13.8(95% CI 12.1–15.4),4 周(Middleton,PMID 31697873);纯合子对比 tez-iva +10(7.4–12.6)(Heijerman,PMID 31679946)。
    • vanzacaftor-tezacaftor-deutivacaftor(VTD/Alyftrek) 头对头对 ETI 非劣效而非优效:ppFEV1 差值 ~+0.2(~-0.7 至 +1.1),汗氯下降更大,每日一次(PMID 39756424,Lancet Respir Med 2025)。NMA 显示 VTD 对比安慰剂 ppFEV1 MD +12.78(6.41–19.15)(PMID 40102290)。
    • 获益-风险: 两方案肺功能获益相当;VTD 优势在每日一次依从性 + 汗氯生物标志物,而非更优肺功能。
    • 证据缺口: VTD 长期(多年)结局与广泛真实世界数据尚少于 ETI;头对头终点为非劣效设计,不证明优效。
    Anchor 仅呈现证据,不开具剂量/途径/疗程。    Anchor KB-grounded summary (CF, ≥1 F508del):
    • ETI remains the benchmark: F508del/minimal-function ppFEV1 +13.8 (95% CI 12.1–15.4) at 4wk (Middleton, PMID 31697873); homozygous vs tez-iva +10 (7.4–12.6) (Heijerman, PMID 31679946).
    • Vanzacaftor-tezacaftor-deutivacaftor (VTD/Alyftrek) is non-inferior, not superior, vs ETI head-to-head: ppFEV1 difference ~+0.2 (~-0.7 to +1.1), greater sweat-chloride reduction, once-daily (PMID 39756424, Lancet Respir Med 2025). NMA gives VTD vs placebo ppFEV1 MD +12.78 (6.41–19.15) (PMID 40102290).
    • Benefit–risk: comparable lung-function benefit; VTD's edge is once-daily adherence + sweat-chloride biomarker, not better lung function.
    • Evidence gap: long-term (multi-year) outcomes and broad real-world data for VTD remain thinner than for ETI; the head-to-head endpoint is non-inferiority by design and does not prove superiority.
    Anchor presents evidence only; no dose/route/duration prescribed.

    不可切除恶性胸膜间皮瘤成人,一线全身治疗首选是什么?请将 nivolumab+ipilimumab 与铂+培美曲塞化疗对比(总生存 HR 含 95% CI)、说明关键试验与最长(如 5 年)随访结局,并附 PMID。In adults with unresectable malignant pleural mesothelioma, what is the first-line systemic therapy of choice and its long-term survival benefit? Compare nivolumab+ipilimumab vs platinum-pemetrexed (OS HR with 95% CI), name the pivotal trial and longest (e.g. 5-yr) outcomes, with PMIDs.
    🤖 选 LLM:看它的原话 + Anchor 校正 + 审计(每个 LLM 同一锁定 prompt)Pick an LLM: its verbatim + Anchor correction + audit (same locked prompt to each)
    Ⓐ未矫正 · DeepSeek v4 Pro 原话Uncorrected · DeepSeek v4 Pro
    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    问:不可切除恶性胸膜间皮瘤一线全身治疗首选?nivo+ipi vs 含铂+培美曲塞化疗的总生存(HR+95%CI),关键试验名,最长(如5年)生存结局,附PMID。

    答:首选 纳武利尤单抗(nivolumab)+伊匹木单抗(ipilimumab),依据 III 期 CheckMate 743 试验 [1],对所有组织学亚型(尤其非上皮样)确立为标准治疗。
    • 主分析(Baas 等,2021)[1]:中位 OS 18.1 vs 14.1 个月,HR 0.74(95%CI 0.60–0.91;p=0.0020)。
    • 5年更新(Peters 等,2024)[2]:最短随访 61.3 个月,5 年 OS 率 23% vs 12%,HR 0.72(95%CI 0.59–0.89);绝对获益 11 个百分点,跨上皮/非上皮样组织学一致倾向免疫,非上皮样获益更显著。
    • 免疫相关不良事件总体可控;现为主要指南一线优选。

    参考:1. Baas P 等. CheckMate 743. Lancet. 2021;397:375-386. PMID 33485464。 2. Peters S 等. CheckMate 743 五年结局. J Clin Oncol. 2024;42(22):2662-2671. PMID 38976876。
    置信度:高 —— 基于成熟 III 期 5 年数据。    Q: First-line systemic therapy of choice in unresectable malignant pleural mesothelioma? Compare nivo+ipi vs platinum-pemetrexed on OS (HR+95%CI), name the pivotal trial, give longest (e.g. 5-yr) outcomes, with PMIDs.

    A: Choice is nivolumab + ipilimumab, per the phase 3 CheckMate 743 trial [1]; standard of care across all histologies (esp. non-epithelioid).
    • Primary analysis (Baas et al., 2021) [1]: median OS 18.1 vs 14.1 mo, HR 0.74 (95%CI 0.60–0.91; p=0.0020).
    • 5-yr update (Peters et al., 2024) [2]: min follow-up 61.3 mo, 5-yr OS rate 23% vs 12%, HR 0.72 (95%CI 0.59–0.89); absolute 11-pt gain, consistent across epithelial/non-epithelial, larger in non-epithelial.
    • irAEs generally manageable; now preferred first-line in major guidelines.

    References: 1. Baas P et al. CheckMate 743. Lancet. 2021;397:375-386. PMID 33485464. 2. Peters S et al. CheckMate 743 5-year outcomes. J Clin Oncol. 2024;42(22):2662-2671. PMID 38976876.
    Confidence: High — mature phase 3 5-year data.

    不可切除恶性胸膜间皮瘤一线:纳武利尤单抗+伊匹木单抗 vs 含铂+培美曲塞化疗。CheckMate 743(III 期)主分析(PMID 33485464,Lancet 2021):中位 OS 18.1 vs 14.1 个月,HR 0.74(95%CI 0.60–0.91)。5 年随访(PMID 41734361,JCO 2026,中位随访 66.8 个月):5 年 OS 率 14% vs 6%,OS HR 0.74(95%CI 0.62–0.88);获益跨上皮样/非上皮样组织学一致。
    获益-风险:持久 OS 获益(HR 0.74),代价为免疫相关不良事件,总体可控但需专科监测。
    证据缺口:缺乏与新一代方案(如化疗+免疫,CheckMate 743 未涵盖)的头对头;PD-L1/组织学的预测价值仍未定论。Anchor 仅呈现证据,不开具剂量/给药途径/疗程。    Unresectable malignant pleural mesothelioma, first line: nivolumab + ipilimumab vs platinum-pemetrexed chemotherapy. CheckMate 743 (phase 3) primary analysis (PMID 33485464, Lancet 2021): median OS 18.1 vs 14.1 mo, HR 0.74 (95%CI 0.60–0.91). Five-year follow-up (PMID 41734361, JCO 2026, median follow-up 66.8 mo): 5-yr OS rate 14% vs 6%, OS HR 0.74 (95%CI 0.62–0.88); benefit consistent across epithelioid/non-epithelioid histology.
    Benefit-risk: durable OS benefit (HR 0.74) at the cost of immune-related adverse events, generally manageable but requiring specialist monitoring.
    Evidence gap: no head-to-head vs newer regimens (e.g. chemo-immunotherapy, not in CheckMate 743); predictive value of PD-L1/histology remains unsettled. Anchor presents evidence and does not prescribe a dose/route/duration.

    对于不可切除恶性胸膜间皮瘤,nivolumab+ipilimumab 是一线选择之一,较铂+培美曲塞改善总生存:CheckMate 743 主分析 HR 0.74(96.6% CI 0.60–0.91);5 年随访 OS HR 0.74(0.62–0.88)、5 年 OS 率约 14% vs 6% [J Clin Oncol 2026 · PMID 41734361]。获益在非上皮样亚型更大(HR 0.48),上皮样较小(0.82),故组织学指导选择;两方案对上皮样均为一线标准。
    获益–风险:免疫治疗有 irAE 风险,需权衡;证据缺口:尚无 nivo+ipi 与化疗-免疫联合方案的头对头数据。    For unresectable malignant pleural mesothelioma, nivolumab+ipilimumab is a first-line option improving OS vs platinum-pemetrexed: CheckMate 743 primary HR 0.74 (96.6% CI 0.60–0.91); 5-year OS HR 0.74 (0.62–0.88), 5-yr OS ~14% vs 6% [J Clin Oncol 2026 · PMID 41734361]. Benefit is larger in non-epithelioid disease (HR 0.48) and smaller in epithelioid (0.82), so histology guides the choice; both regimens are first-line standards for epithelioid.
    Benefit–risk: immunotherapy carries immune-related AE risk; evidence gap: no head-to-head of nivo+ipi vs chemo-immunotherapy.

    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    问题:不可切除恶性胸膜间皮瘤(MPM)一线 纳武利尤单抗+伊匹木单抗 vs 化疗
    结论:纳武+伊匹一线优于 铂+培美曲塞(CheckMate 743,n=605)。
    • 中位 OS 18.1(16.8-21.4)vs 14.1(12.4-16.2)月;OS HR 0.74(96.6% CI 0.60-0.91;p=0.0020);2年 OS 41% vs 27% [1]
    • 3年(中位随访 43.1 月):HR 0.73(0.61-0.87),3年 OS 23% vs 15% [2]
    • 5年(中位随访 66.8 月):HR 0.74(0.62-0.88),5年 OS 14% vs 6%;校正交叉后 HR 0.64(0.53-0.78)[3]
    • 全组织学获益,非上皮样获益更大;3-4级 TRAE 30% vs 32%;FDA 2020年10月批准
    引用:[1] Baas Lancet 2021 PMID 33485464;[2] Peters 3-yr Ann Oncol 2022 PMID 35124183;[3] Scherpereel 5-yr JCO 2026 PMID 41734361
    置信度:High    Q: Unresectable malignant pleural mesothelioma (MPM) first-line nivolumab+ipilimumab vs chemo
    Bottom line: Nivo+ipi first-line superior to platinum-pemetrexed (CheckMate 743, n=605).
    • Median OS 18.1 (16.8-21.4) vs 14.1 (12.4-16.2) mo; OS HR 0.74 (96.6% CI 0.60-0.91; p=0.0020); 2-yr OS 41% vs 27% [1]
    • 3-yr (median f/u 43.1 mo): HR 0.73 (0.61-0.87), 3-yr OS 23% vs 15% [2]
    • 5-yr (median f/u 66.8 mo): HR 0.74 (0.62-0.88), 5-yr OS 14% vs 6%; crossover-adjusted HR 0.64 (0.53-0.78) [3]
    • Benefit across histology, greater in non-epithelioid; Grade 3-4 TRAE 30% vs 32%; FDA approved Oct 2020
    Refs: [1] Baas Lancet 2021 PMID 33485464; [2] Peters 3-yr Ann Oncol 2022 PMID 35124183; [3] Scherpereel 5-yr JCO 2026 PMID 41734361
    Confidence: High

    不可切除恶性胸膜间皮瘤(MPM)一线:纳武利尤单抗+伊匹木单抗较 铂+培美曲塞 显著延长总生存(CheckMate 743,n=605)。中位 OS 18.1 vs 14.1 月,OS HR 0.74(96.6% CI 0.60-0.91;PMID 33485464);三年 OS 23% vs 15%、HR 0.73(0.61-0.87;PMID 35124183);五年 OS 14% vs 6%、HR 0.74(0.62-0.88),校正交叉后 HR 0.64(0.53-0.78;PMID 41734361)。获益贯穿全组织学,非上皮样亚组幅度更大。
    获益-风险:长期生存"尾部"约翻倍(5年 14% vs 6%),3-4级治疗相关不良事件双臂相当(约30% vs 32%);免疫毒性需监测,组织学为分层选择的关键因素。
    证据缺口:缺乏纳武+伊匹与化疗-免疫联合(铂-培美曲塞+帕博利珠单抗)的头对头比较;最佳一线策略按组织学的选择仍待前瞻验证。    Unresectable malignant pleural mesothelioma (MPM), first-line: nivolumab+ipilimumab significantly prolongs overall survival vs platinum-pemetrexed (CheckMate 743, n=605). Median OS 18.1 vs 14.1 mo, OS HR 0.74 (96.6% CI 0.60-0.91; PMID 33485464); 3-yr OS 23% vs 15%, HR 0.73 (0.61-0.87; PMID 35124183); 5-yr OS 14% vs 6%, HR 0.74 (0.62-0.88), crossover-adjusted HR 0.64 (0.53-0.78; PMID 41734361). Benefit is seen across histology and is larger in the non-epithelioid subgroup.
    Benefit-risk: the long-term survival tail roughly doubles (5-yr 14% vs 6%), with comparable Grade 3-4 treatment-related adverse events between arms (~30% vs 32%); immune-related toxicity requires monitoring, and histology is a key factor for treatment selection.
    Evidence gap: no head-to-head comparison of nivo+ipi vs chemo-immunotherapy (platinum-pemetrexed + pembrolizumab); the optimal first-line strategy by histology still needs prospective confirmation.

    不可切除 MPM 一线 nivolumab+ipilimumab vs 铂-培美曲塞化疗,KB 锚定证据:
    • CheckMate 743(Baas, Lancet 2021, PMID 33485464,n=605):中位 OS 18.1 vs 14.1 个月,OS HR 0.74(96.6% CI 0.60-0.91)。
    • 组织学:非上皮样型获益显著(HR 0.46,0.31-0.68);上皮样型获益有限(HR 0.86,0.69-1.08,CI 跨 1)。
    • 5 年长期随访(Scherpereel, JCO 2026, PMID 41734361):OS HR 0.74(0.62-0.88),5 年 OS 约 14% vs 6%。
    • 获益-风险一句话:免疫双药使长期生存「尾部」相对延长(5 年存活率约翻倍),非上皮样型受益最大;免疫相关毒性需监测。
    • 证据缺口:上皮样型最优一线仍有争议(化疗-免疫 vs 双免疫尚无头对头);跨研究 5 年生存率读数因交叉与人群差异略有出入。
    仅呈现证据,不给出处方性剂量/给药途径/疗程。    Unresectable MPM, first-line nivolumab+ipilimumab vs platinum-pemetrexed, KB-anchored evidence:
    • CheckMate 743 (Baas, Lancet 2021, PMID 33485464, n=605): median OS 18.1 vs 14.1 mo, OS HR 0.74 (96.6% CI 0.60-0.91).
    • Histology: substantial benefit in non-epithelioid (HR 0.46, 0.31-0.68); limited benefit in epithelioid (HR 0.86, 0.69-1.08, CI crosses 1).
    • 5-year follow-up (Scherpereel, JCO 2026, PMID 41734361): OS HR 0.74 (0.62-0.88), 5-yr OS ~14% vs 6%.
    • Benefit-risk one-liner: dual IO lengthens the long-term survival tail (roughly doubling 5-yr survival), with the largest gain in non-epithelioid; immune-related toxicity requires monitoring.
    • Evidence gap: optimal first-line for epithelioid remains debated (no head-to-head chemo-IO vs dual-IO); cross-study 5-yr survival readouts vary modestly due to crossover and population differences.
    Evidence only — no prescriptive dose/route/duration.

    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    问题:不可切除恶性胸膜间皮瘤(MPM)一线 纳武利尤单抗+伊匹木单抗 vs 化疗。
    核心主张:
    • 一线选 nivo+ipi 或 铂+培美曲塞,按组织学决定。
    • CheckMate 743: 总生存 HR ~0.74(~0.60-0.91),中位 OS ~18.1 vs ~14.1 月 [1]。
    • 上皮样获益较小(~18.7 vs ~16.5 月;HR ~0.85);非上皮样获益显著(~18.1 vs ~8.8 月;HR ~0.46)。
    • 5 年生存:~12-13% nivo+ipi vs ~3% 化疗——长期尾部约增至 3 倍 [2]。
    • 非上皮样 → 首选 nivo+ipi;上皮样 → 两者皆可。
    • 化疗-免疫(铂+培美曲塞+帕博利珠单抗)后续亦显示获益。
    参考文献: [1] Baas CheckMate 743 Lancet 2021 PMID 33485464;[2] Peters 5-year CheckMate 743(PMID 省略——不确定)。
    置信度:中-高。    Q: First-line nivolumab+ipilimumab vs chemo for unresectable malignant pleural mesothelioma (MPM).
    Key claims:
    • First-line: nivo+ipi or platinum-pemetrexed, histology guiding choice.
    • CheckMate 743: OS HR ~0.74 (~0.60-0.91), median OS ~18.1 vs ~14.1 mo [1].
    • Epithelioid modest (~18.7 vs ~16.5 mo; HR ~0.85); non-epithelioid substantial (~18.1 vs ~8.8 mo; HR ~0.46).
    • 5-year: ~12-13% nivo+ipi vs ~3% chemo — roughly tripling the long-term tail [2].
    • Non-epithelioid → nivo+ipi preferred; epithelioid → either.
    • Chemo-IO (platinum-pemetrexed+pembrolizumab) also shown benefit later.
    Refs: [1] Baas CheckMate 743 Lancet 2021 PMID 33485464; [2] Peters 5-year CheckMate 743 (PMID omitted—not certain).
    Confidence: Moderate-High.

    不可切除恶性胸膜间皮瘤(MPM)一线,纳武利尤单抗+伊匹木单抗 vs 铂-培美曲塞化疗,以组织学指导选择。

    5 年更新(Scherpereel, JCO 2026, PMID 41734361):中位随访 66.8 月,OS HR 0.74(0.62-0.88),5 年 OS 14% vs 6%——长期生存尾部约翻倍。
    获益-风险一行:nivo+ipi 在非上皮样组织学中带来明确、持久的生存获益,上皮样获益较温和;免疫治疗相关毒性需监测。
    证据缺口:头对头化疗-免疫(铂-培美曲塞+帕博利珠单抗)与 nivo+ipi 的直接比较缺乏;上皮样亚组的最佳一线选择仍有争议。
    (本卡仅呈现证据,不给出处方剂量/途径/疗程。)    Unresectable malignant pleural mesothelioma (MPM), first-line: nivolumab+ipilimumab vs platinum-pemetrexed chemo, histology guiding choice.
    CheckMate 743 (PMID 33485464): median OS 18.1 vs 14.1 mo, OS HR 0.74 (96.6% CI 0.60-0.91). Non-epithelioid benefit substantial (HR ~0.46); epithelioid modest (HR ~0.85).
    5-year update (Scherpereel, JCO 2026, PMID 41734361): median follow-up 66.8 mo, OS HR 0.74 (0.62-0.88), 5-yr OS 14% vs 6% — roughly doubling the long-term survival tail.
    Benefit-risk (one line): nivo+ipi gives a clear, durable survival benefit in non-epithelioid histology and a more modest benefit in epithelioid; immune-related toxicity requires monitoring.
    Evidence gap: no direct head-to-head of chemo-IO (platinum-pemetrexed + pembrolizumab) vs nivo+ipi; optimal first-line choice in the epithelioid subgroup remains debated.

    重症社区获得性肺炎住院成人,是否应使用辅助糖皮质激素?请给对死亡率的效应量(RR 含 95% CI)、证据支持的激素与方案、关键试验/系统评价名称与 PMID。In adults hospitalized with severe community-acquired pneumonia, should adjunctive corticosteroids be used?     🤖 选 LLM:看它的原话 + Anchor 校正 + 审计(每个 LLM 同一锁定 prompt)Pick an LLM: its verbatim + Anchor correction + audit (same locked prompt to each)
    Ⓐ未矫正 · DeepSeek v4 Pro 原话Uncorrected · DeepSeek v4 Pro
    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    问:成人住院重症社区获得性肺炎(CAP),是否应加用辅助糖皮质激素?给出对死亡率的效应(RR + 95% CI)、推荐的激素与方案、关键试验/系统评价名称及 PMID。

    DeepSeek v4 Pro 作答(要点):
    • CAPE COD 试验(Dequin 等,NEJM 2023)——氢化可的松 200 mg/日持续静脉滴注 4 或 7 天后减量;重症 CAP(多数 ICU/机械通气)。28 天死亡 6.2%(25/400)vs 安慰剂 11.9%(47/395),绝对差 −5.6 个百分点(95% CI −9.6 至 −1.7,P=0.006),死亡 HR 0.51(95% CI 0.31–0.82)。PMID 36942789。
    • Confalonieri 2005(Am J Respir Crit Care Med)——氢化可的松 200 mg 静推后 10 mg/h 持续 7 天;ICU 死亡 0/23 vs 7/23(30.4%),P=0.009。PMID 15557131。
    • Torres 2015(JAMA)——甲泼尼龙 0.5 mg/kg q12h × 5 天 vs 安慰剂;30 天死亡 9.8%(6/61)vs 20.3%(12/59),OR 0.39(95% CI 0.13–1.14,无统计学意义)。PMID 25665002。
    • Siemieniuk 2015 系统评价/Meta(Ann Intern Med,13 RCT)——重症 CAP 死亡 RR 0.67(95% CI 0.45–1.01)。PMID 26258555(文中亦曾犹豫写 PMID 28384789)。
    • Dequin IPD Meta(称 Intensive Care Med 2023,6 RCT)——28 天死亡 OR 0.56(95% CI 0.36–0.86),P=0.008,绝对风险降 5.3%。PMID 37433960。
    • 文中亦推测一篇"Chest 2023 Meta"PMID 37019347。

    结论:重症 CAP 支持加用辅助激素(尤以氢化可的松证据最充分)以降低死亡率。自评信心:中–高。    Q: In adults hospitalized with severe community-acquired pneumonia (CAP), should adjunctive corticosteroids be used? Give the mortality effect (RR + 95% CI), the steroid/regimen supported, pivotal trial/SR names and PMIDs.

    DeepSeek v4 Pro answer (key claims):
    • CAPE COD trial (Dequin et al., NEJM 2023) — hydrocortisone 200 mg/day continuous IV for 4 or 7 days then taper; severe CAP (mostly ICU/ventilated). 28-day mortality 6.2% (25/400) vs placebo 11.9% (47/395), absolute difference −5.6 percentage points (95% CI −9.6 to −1.7, P=0.006), death HR 0.51 (95% CI 0.31–0.82). PMID 36942789.
    • Confalonieri 2005 (Am J Respir Crit Care Med) — hydrocortisone 200 mg bolus then 10 mg/h × 7 days; ICU mortality 0/23 vs 7/23 (30.4%), P=0.009. PMID 15557131.
    • Torres 2015 (JAMA) — methylprednisolone 0.5 mg/kg q12h × 5 days vs placebo; 30-day mortality 9.8% (6/61) vs 20.3% (12/59), OR 0.39 (95% CI 0.13–1.14, NS). PMID 25665002.
    • Siemieniuk 2015 SR/MA (Ann Intern Med, 13 RCTs) — severe-CAP mortality RR 0.67 (95% CI 0.45–1.01). PMID 26258555 (text also wavered toward PMID 28384789).
    • Dequin IPD meta-analysis (claimed Intensive Care Med 2023, 6 RCTs) — 28-day mortality OR 0.56 (95% CI 0.36–0.86), P=0.008, ARR 5.3%. PMID 37433960.
    • Text also speculated a "Chest 2023 meta-analysis" PMID 37019347.

    Conclusion: Severe CAP supports adjunctive steroids (hydrocortisone best-evidenced) to reduce mortality. Self-rated confidence: Moderate–High.

    重症 CAP 辅助糖皮质激素 · Anchor KB 校正
    DeepSeek 的临床骨架基本正确——重症 CAP 加用激素可降低死亡率,氢化可的松证据最充分。但其引用列表混入多个张冠李戴的 PMID:真实论文与肺炎激素毫无关系。逐一核验如下。① 正确引用先确认。CAPE COD 主试验 PMID 36942789(Dequin,NEJM 2023)、Confalonieri 2005 PMID 15557131、Siemieniuk 2015 系统评价 PMID 26258555 —— NCBI esummary 均核验为真且切题,保留。CAPE COD 的 28 天死亡 6.2% vs 11.9%、HR 0.51(0.31–0.82)复述准确。Torres 2015 甲泼尼龙试验,PMID 25665002Torres 2015 试验真实 PMID 为 25688779(JAMA 2015)引用造假Dequin IPD Meta(Intensive Care Med 2023),PMID 37433960,28天死亡 OR 0.56(0.36–0.86)删除——此 PMID 及所述 IPD Meta 无法核实;改用真实 2025 网络 Meta(BMC Pulm Med,PMID 40307783):氢化可的松降死亡 RR 0.35(95% CI 0.14–0.64)引用造假Siemieniuk 系统评价备选 PMID 28384789删除——Siemieniuk 2015 唯一正确 PMID 是 26258555引用造假推测的 Chest 2023 Meta,PMID 37019347删除——该 Meta 及 PMID 均无依据引用造假② 证据落点(KB 锚定)。重症 CAP:CAPE COD(PMID 36942789)28 天死亡 6.2% vs 11.9%、HR 0.51(0.31–0.82);2025 网络 Meta(PMID 40307783)示氢化可的松降死亡 RR 0.35(95% CI 0.14–0.64);Torres 2015(PMID 25688779)为治疗失败而非死亡终点的支持性 RCT。获益—风险:激素降死亡,代价为高血糖等可控不良反应。证据缺口:最佳激素种类、剂量与疗程仍未头对头确定;非重症 CAP 获益不明确。Anchor 仅呈现证据,不开具具体剂量/给药途径/疗程。    Adjunctive corticosteroids in severe CAP · Anchor KB correction
    DeepSeek's clinical skeleton is broadly right — adding steroids in severe CAP lowers mortality, with hydrocortisone best-evidenced. But its reference list is salted with mismatched PMIDs whose real papers have nothing to do with pneumonia steroids. Verified below.① Confirm the correct cites first. CAPE COD pivotal trial PMID 36942789 (Dequin, NEJM 2023), Confalonieri 2005 PMID 15557131, and Siemieniuk 2015 SR PMID 26258555 — all NCBI-verified as real and on-topic; retained. CAPE COD's 28-day mortality 6.2% vs 11.9% and HR 0.51 (0.31–0.82) are reproduced accurately.Torres 2015 methylprednisolone trial, PMID 25665002the real PMID for the Torres 2015 trial is 25688779 (JAMA 2015)FABRICATEDDequin IPD meta-analysis (Intensive Care Med 2023), PMID 37433960, 28-day mortality OR 0.56 (0.36–0.86)deleted — this PMID and the cited IPD-MA cannot be verified; substitute the real 2025 network meta-analysis (BMC Pulm Med, PMID 40307783): hydrocortisone reduces mortality RR 0.35 (95% CI 0.14–0.64)FABRICATEDalternate PMID 28384789 floated for the Siemieniuk SRdeleted — the only correct PMID for Siemieniuk 2015 is 26258555FABRICATEDspeculated Chest 2023 meta-analysis, PMID 37019347deleted — neither the meta-analysis nor the PMID has any basisFABRICATED② Where the evidence lands (KB-anchored). Severe CAP: CAPE COD (PMID 36942789) 28-day mortality 6.2% vs 11.9%, HR 0.51 (0.31–0.82); the 2025 network meta-analysis (PMID 40307783) shows hydrocortisone reduces mortality RR 0.35 (95% CI 0.14–0.64); Torres 2015 (PMID 25688779) is a supporting RCT on treatment failure, not mortality. Benefit–risk: steroids lower mortality at the cost of manageable harms such as hyperglycemia.     重症社区获得性肺炎(CAP)· 辅助糖皮质激素
    在成人重症 CAP 中,辅助糖皮质激素可降低死亡率。关键证据:
    • CAPE COD 试验(Dequin,NEJM 2023,PMID 36942789):氢化可的松组 28 天死亡 6.2%(25/400)vs 安慰剂 11.9%(47/395),绝对差 −5.6 个百分点(95% CI −9.6 至 −1.7),死亡 HR 0.51(95% CI 0.31–0.82)。
    • 2025 网络 Meta(BMC Pulm Med,PMID 40307783):氢化可的松降死亡 RR 0.35(95% CI 0.14–0.64),为重症 CAP 中证据最强的激素。
    • Torres 2015(JAMA,PMID 25688779):高炎症反应重症 CAP 的支持性 RCT(主终点为治疗失败)。
    获益—风险:激素降低死亡风险,代价是高血糖等通常可控的不良反应。
    证据缺口:最佳激素种类/剂量/疗程尚无充分头对头比较;非重症 CAP 获益不确定。
    注:Anchor 呈现已核实证据,不开具具体剂量、给药途径或疗程。    Severe community-acquired pneumonia (CAP) · adjunctive corticosteroids
    In adults with severe CAP, adjunctive corticosteroids reduce mortality. Key evidence:
    • CAPE COD trial (Dequin, NEJM 2023, PMID 36942789): 28-day mortality 6.2% (25/400) on hydrocortisone vs 11.9% (47/395) on placebo, absolute difference −5.6 percentage points (95% CI −9.6 to −1.7), death HR 0.51 (95% CI 0.31–0.82).
    • 2025 network meta-analysis (BMC Pulm Med, PMID 40307783): hydrocortisone reduces mortality RR 0.35 (95% CI 0.14–0.64) — the best-evidenced steroid in severe CAP.
    • Torres 2015 (JAMA, PMID 25688779): supporting RCT in severe CAP with high inflammatory response (primary endpoint treatment failure).
    Benefit–risk: steroids lower mortality risk at the cost of usually-manageable harms such as hyperglycemia.
    Evidence gap: the optimal steroid / dose / duration lack adequate head-to-head comparison; benefit in non-severe CAP is uncertain.
    Note: Anchor presents verified evidence and does not prescribe a specific dose, route or duration.

    Ⓐ未矫正 · Gemini 3 Flash 原话Uncorrected · Gemini 3 Flash
    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    近期高质量证据支持在重症社区获得性肺炎(sCAP)住院成人中使用辅助糖皮质激素以降低死亡率 [1,2]。

    死亡率:CAPE COD RCT(2023)示早期氢化可的松显著降低 28 天死亡(6.2% vs 11.9%;RR 0.52, 95% CI 0.33–0.82)[1];Pitre 等 2023 Meta 分析 sCAP 死亡 RR 0.62(0.45–0.85)[2];MEDUSA(甲泼尼龙)降治疗失败但死亡未达显著 [3]。

    推荐方案:氢化可的松 200 mg/日,持续静脉输注(或每 6 小时 50 mg),疗程 4–8 天 [1]。

    参考文献:[1] Dequin PF, et al. Hydrocortisone in Severe CAP. N Engl J Med. 2023;388:1931-1941. PMID: 36942646. [2] Pitre T, et al. Corticosteroids in CAP: NMA. Ann Intern Med. 2023;176:1084-1094. PMID: 37276134. [3] Torres A, et al. JAMA. 2015;313:677-686. PMID: 25688779. [4] Siemieniuk RA, et al. Ann Intern Med. 2015;163:519-528. PMID: 26258528.
    自评信心:High。    Recent high-quality evidence supports adjunctive corticosteroids in adults hospitalized with severe CAP (sCAP) to reduce mortality [1,2].

    Mortality: the CAPE COD RCT (2023) showed early hydrocortisone significantly cut 28-day mortality (6.2% vs 11.9%; RR 0.52, 95% CI 0.33–0.82) [1]; Pitre et al. 2023 meta-analysis sCAP mortality RR 0.62 (0.45–0.85) [2]; MEDUSA (methylprednisolone) cut treatment failure but mortality was NS [3].

    Recommended regimen: hydrocortisone 200 mg/day, continuous IV infusion (or 50 mg every 6 h), for 4–8 days [1].

    References: [1] Dequin PF, et al. Hydrocortisone in Severe CAP. N Engl J Med. 2023;388:1931-1941. PMID: 36942646. [2] Pitre T, et al. Corticosteroids in CAP: NMA. Ann Intern Med. 2023;176:1084-1094. PMID: 37276134. [3] Torres A, et al. JAMA. 2015;313:677-686. PMID: 25688779. [4] Siemieniuk RA, et al. Ann Intern Med. 2015;163:519-528. PMID: 26258528.
    Confidence self-rating: High.

Mortality grounded in KB: 2025 NMA hydrocortisone mortality RR 0.35 (0.14–0.64, PMID 40307783) + the real CAPE COD (36942789).

    Recommended regimen: hydrocortisone 200 mg/day, continuous IV infusion, 4–8 daysAnchor reports the evidence and the trial's regimen (CAPE COD used hydrocortisone) but does not issue a prescriptive dose/route/duration — specific dosing must be individualised by the treating team per current guidelinesprescriptive overreach · red-line.
    在重症社区获得性肺炎住院成人中,早期辅助糖皮质激素降低死亡率:CAPE COD 氢化可的松 28 天死亡 RR 0.52(0.33–0.82)[PMID 36942789];2025 网络 Meta 分析氢化可的松降死亡 RR 0.35(0.14–0.64)[PMID 40307783]。获益在需高流量氧/无创或有创通气的重症者最明显;流感或疑似真菌性肺炎应避免(可能增加病毒载量/死亡),并监测高血糖。
    关于给药:Anchor 呈现试验所用药物与证据,但不输出具体剂量/途径/疗程处方,具体方案由主管团队依指南个体化。证据缺口:不同激素种类与给药策略的头对头比较仍有限。    In adults hospitalized with severe community-acquired pneumonia, early adjunctive corticosteroids reduce mortality: CAPE COD hydrocortisone 28-day mortality RR 0.52 (0.33–0.82) [PMID 36942789]; the 2025 network meta-analysis for hydrocortisone shows mortality RR 0.35 (0.14–0.64) [PMID 40307783]. Benefit is greatest in severe disease needing high-flow oxygen / non-invasive or invasive ventilation; avoid in influenza or suspected fungal pneumonia (may raise viral load/mortality), and monitor for hyperglycemia.
    On dosing: Anchor presents the drug used and the evidence but does not issue a specific dose/route/duration — the regimen is individualised by the treating team per guidelines. Evidence gap: head-to-head comparisons of corticosteroid type and strategy remain limited.

    Ⓐ未矫正 · OpenEvidence 原话Uncorrected · OpenEvidence
    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    问题:重症社区获得性肺炎(CAP)是否应加用糖皮质激素辅助治疗?

    OpenEvidence 回答(置信度:高):SCCM 2024 推荐重症 CAP(PSI IV/V、CURB-65≥3 或入 ICU)加用糖皮质激素辅助治疗。
    • 重症 CAP:激素可能降低住院死亡率 RR 0.62(0.45-0.85,中等确定性);非重症无获益(RR 1.08,0.83-1.42)。
    • 降低有创通气 RR 0.56(0.42-0.74)、ICU 入住 RR 0.65(0.43-0.97)。
    • CAPE COD(2023,n=800):28 天死亡率 6.2% vs 11.9%(绝对差 -5.6,-9.6 至 -1.7);降低插管 HR 0.69、升压药 HR 0.59。
    • 2023 荟萃分析(18 RCT,4661 例):重症 RR 0.62(0.45-0.85)。2025 SR(20 研究):RR 0.73(0.57-0.93)。
    • ESCAPe(586 例):60 天死亡率无差异(16% vs 18%)。

    参考文献(给出 PMID):Bergmann CID 2023 PMID 37876267;Soumare Ann Intern Med PMID 41325621;Pitre JGIM 2023 PMID 37076606;Pirracchio JAMA 2024 PMID 38967943;Chaudhuri SCCM CCM 2024 PMID 38349150;Dequin CAPE COD NEJM 2023 PMID 37224232。    Question: Should adjunctive corticosteroids be used in severe community-acquired pneumonia (CAP)?

    OpenEvidence answer (confidence: High): SCCM 2024 recommends adjunctive corticosteroids for severe CAP (PSI IV/V, CURB-65≥3, or ICU).
    • Severe CAP: corticosteroids probably reduce hospital mortality RR 0.62 (0.45-0.85, moderate certainty); no benefit in less-severe (RR 1.08, 0.83-1.42).
    • Reduce invasive ventilation RR 0.56 (0.42-0.74), ICU admission RR 0.65 (0.43-0.97).
    • CAPE COD (2023, n=800): 28-d mortality 6.2% vs 11.9% (absolute -5.6, -9.6 to -1.7); reduced intubation HR 0.69, vasopressors HR 0.59.
    • 2023 MA (18 RCTs, 4661): severe RR 0.62 (0.45-0.85). 2025 SR (20 studies): RR 0.73 (0.57-0.93).
    • ESCAPe (586): no difference in 60-d mortality (16% vs 18%).

    References (PMIDs given): Bergmann CID 2023 PMID 37876267; Soumare Ann Intern Med PMID 41325621; Pitre JGIM 2023 PMID 37076606; Pirracchio JAMA 2024 PMID 38967943; Chaudhuri SCCM CCM 2024 PMID 38349150; Dequin CAPE COD NEJM 2023 PMID 37224232.
    ⒷAnchor 校正版 · KB 锚定Anchor-corrected · KB-grounded修订Track净版Clean

    重症社区获得性肺炎(CAP)加用糖皮质激素可降低死亡率,获益集中于重症亚组。

    已核验证据:
    • CAPE COD RCT(Dequin,NEJM 2023,PMID 36942789):氢化可的松使 28 天死亡率显著下降,RR 0.52;同时降低插管与升压药需求。
    • 2025 网状荟萃分析(PMID 40307783):氢化可的松死亡率 RR 0.35(95% CI 0.14-0.64)。
    • 聚合证据(Bergmann CID 2023 PMID 37876267;Pitre JGIM 2023 PMID 37076606)一致提示重症亚组获益(RR≈0.62,0.45-0.85),非重症无获益。

    获益-风险:重症 CAP 中死亡率获益明确,代价主要为高血糖等可控不良反应;非重症人群无净获益,且需警惕活动性流感/真菌感染等禁忌。

    证据缺口:不同 RCT(CAPE COD 阳性 vs ESCAPe 中性)在人群、激素种类与时机上存在异质性;最佳激素种类与启动时机尚未在头对头试验中确立。Anchor 仅呈现证据,不开具剂量/途径/疗程。    Adjunctive corticosteroids reduce mortality in severe community-acquired pneumonia (CAP), with benefit concentrated in the severe subgroup.

    Verified evidence:
    • CAPE COD RCT (Dequin, NEJM 2023, PMID 36942789): hydrocortisone significantly lowered 28-day mortality, RR 0.52; also reduced intubation and vasopressor requirement.
    • 2025 network meta-analysis (PMID 40307783): hydrocortisone mortality RR 0.35 (95% CI 0.14-0.64).
    • Pooled evidence (Bergmann CID 2023 PMID 37876267; Pitre JGIM 2023 PMID 37076606) consistently shows benefit in the severe subgroup (RR ≈0.62, 0.45-0.85) and none in non-severe disease.

    Benefit-risk: a clear mortality benefit in severe CAP, at the cost of largely manageable adverse effects such as hyperglycaemia; no net benefit in non-severe patients, and contraindications (active influenza/fungal infection) must be respected.

    Evidence gap: RCTs are heterogeneous in population, steroid type and timing (positive CAPE COD vs neutral ESCAPe); the optimal agent and timing of initiation have not been settled head-to-head. Anchor presents evidence only and issues no dose/route/duration.

    模型说：Model said: Dequin CAPE COD NEJM 2023 PMID 37224232Dequin CAPE COD NEJM 2023 PMID 37224232
    ✅

The pivotal CAPE COD RCT is pinned to an antidepressant trial. Real CAPE COD = PMID 36942789 (28-d mortality RR 0.52).
    💡
    为什么：Why: 用药结论方向虽对,但其单一最重要的证据来源指向完全无关的论文,临床医生按图索骥将查到错误文献。Although the clinical direction is right, the single most important evidence source resolves to a completely unrelated paper; a clinician following the cite lands on the wrong article.

Both mis-attributed.
    💡
    为什么：Why: 作为推荐依据的指南与综述均无法溯源,削弱了整段‘SCCM 2024 推荐’陈述的可验证性。Both the guideline and the review underpinning the recommendation are untraceable, undermining the verifiability of the entire 'SCCM 2024 recommends' statement.

    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    问题:重症社区获得性肺炎(CAP)是否应辅助使用糖皮质激素?
    回答(ChatGPT 5.5):是 — 重症 CAP(尤其 ICU 级细菌性 CAP)应考虑辅助激素;轻症不常规使用 [1,2]。
    • 最清晰方案:静脉氢化可的松 200 mg/天,4–7 天后减量,总疗程 8–14 天。
    • CAPE COD:28 天死亡率 25/400 vs 47/395,据事件计算 RR ~0.53(0.33–0.84)[1];降低插管/血管活性药需求。
    • Cochrane《Corticosteroids for pneumonia》:重症肺炎死亡率 ~RR 0.58(0.40–0.84),高血糖增多 [2]。
    • 用法:抗生素后于重症 CAP 中使用氢化可的松;流感、未控高血糖、消化道出血、真菌/结核需避免或个体化。
    参考文献:[1] Dequin CAPE COD NEJM 2023 PMID 36942789;[2] Stern Corticosteroids for pneumonia Cochrane 2017 PMID 29236286。
    置信度:中等。    Q: Should adjunctive corticosteroids be used in severe community-acquired pneumonia (CAP)?
    A (ChatGPT 5.5): Yes — consider adjunctive steroids in severe CAP (esp. ICU-level bacterial CAP); not routine for mild [1,2].
    • Clearest regimen: IV hydrocortisone 200 mg/day, 4–7d then taper, total 8–14d.
    • CAPE COD: 28-d mortality 25/400 vs 47/395, RR ~0.53 (0.33–0.84) calculated from events [1]; reduced intubation/vasopressors.
    • Cochrane 'Corticosteroids for pneumonia': severe pneumonia mortality ~RR 0.58 (0.40–0.84), more hyperglycaemia [2].
    • Use: hydrocortisone in severe CAP after antibiotics; avoid/individualize in influenza, uncontrolled hyperglycaemia, GI bleed, fungal/TB.
    Refs: [1] Dequin CAPE COD NEJM 2023 PMID 36942789; [2] Stern Corticosteroids for pneumonia Cochrane 2017 PMID 29236286.
    Confidence: Moderate.

    重症 CAP 辅助糖皮质激素 — 证据对齐摘要(Anchor KB 锚定):
    • CAPE COD(Dequin,NEJM 2023,PMID 36942789):重症 CAP 中氢化可的松降低 28 天死亡率,RR 0.52;并减少插管与血管活性药需求。
    • Cochrane《Corticosteroids for pneumonia》(Stern,2017,PMID 29236286):重症肺炎死亡率约 RR 0.58(0.40–0.84),高血糖增多。
    • 2025 网络 Meta 分析(PMID 40307783):氢化可的松降低死亡率 RR 0.35(95% CI 0.14–0.64)。
    • Torres(JAMA 2015,PMID 25688779):CRP>150 重症亚组减少治疗失败。
    效益—风险:重症 CAP 中辅助激素降低死亡与器官支持需求,代价为高血糖增多;轻症无获益。
    证据缺口:最佳制剂、剂量与疗程未经头对头确定;流感/真菌/结核背景证据有限,须避免或个体化。
    (Anchor 呈现证据,不给出处方级剂量/途径/疗程。)    Adjunctive corticosteroids in severe CAP — evidence-aligned summary (Anchor KB-grounded):
    • CAPE COD (Dequin, NEJM 2023, PMID 36942789): in severe CAP, hydrocortisone reduces 28-d mortality, RR 0.52; also reduces intubation and vasopressor need.
    • Cochrane 'Corticosteroids for pneumonia' (Stern, 2017, PMID 29236286): severe-pneumonia mortality ~RR 0.58 (0.40–0.84), more hyperglycaemia.
    • 2025 network meta-analysis (PMID 40307783): hydrocortisone reduces mortality RR 0.35 (95% CI 0.14–0.64).
    • Torres (JAMA 2015, PMID 25688779): reduced treatment failure in the severe (CRP>150) subgroup.
    Benefit–risk: in severe CAP, adjunctive steroids reduce death and organ-support needs at the cost of more hyperglycaemia; no benefit in mild disease.
    Evidence gap: optimal agent/dose/duration not settled head-to-head; limited evidence in influenza/fungal/TB, where steroids should be avoided or individualized.
    (Anchor presents evidence and gives no prescriptive dose/route/duration.)

    Ⓐ未矫正 · Claude 4.8 原话Uncorrected · Claude 4.8
    LLM 逐字原文。黄色高亮 = Anchor 在 Ⓒ 栏核验后标出的可疑主张。LLM verbatim. Yellow highlight = claims Anchor flagged after verification (see Ⓒ).
    问题:重症社区获得性肺炎(CAP)是否应辅助使用糖皮质激素?
    回答:是——推荐用于住院的重症 CAP 成人,基于近期 RCT 证据,且无禁忌(活动性流感/真菌感染)。

    声称的效应量:
    • CAPE COD(Dequin):氢化可的松 200 mg/日持续静脉滴注 4–7 天后减量,降低 28 天死亡率约 6.2% vs 11.9%,绝对差约 −5.6(CI 约 −9.6 至 −1.7)[1]。
    • Torres(甲泼尼龙,CRP>150):降低治疗失败 [2]。
    • ESCAPe(甲泼尼龙,美国退伍军人):死亡率中性 [3]。
    • Meta 分析:死亡获益集中于重症亚组(RR 约 0.6–0.7),非重症几乎无获益。
    • 注意事项:流感/真菌感染避免使用;监测高血糖。

    参考文献:
    [1] Dequin CAPE COD NEJM 2023 PMID 36942789
    [2] Torres JAMA 2015 PMID 25688779
    [3] Meduri ESCAPe(PMID 省略——不确定)
    置信度:中等偏高    Q: Should adjunctive corticosteroids be used in severe community-acquired pneumonia (CAP)?
    A: Yes — recommended in adults hospitalized with severe CAP, based on recent RCT evidence, with no contraindication (active influenza/fungal).

    Claimed effect sizes:
    • CAPE COD (Dequin): hydrocortisone 200 mg/day continuous IV 4–7d then taper, reduced 28-d mortality ~6.2% vs ~11.9%, absolute ~−5.6 (CI ~−9.6 to −1.7) [1].
    • Torres (methylprednisolone, CRP>150): reduced treatment failure [2].
    • ESCAPe (methylprednisolone, US veterans): neutral on mortality [3].
    • Meta-analyses: mortality benefit concentrated in severe subgroup (RR ~0.6–0.7), little/no benefit non-severe.
    • Cautions: avoid in influenza/fungal; monitor hyperglycaemia.

    References:
    [1] Dequin CAPE COD NEJM 2023 PMID 36942789
    [2] Torres JAMA 2015 PMID 25688779
    [3] Meduri ESCAPe (PMID omitted — not certain)
    Confidence: Moderate-High

    Anchor 经 NCBI 核验本回答的引用并以 KB 锚定效应量。临床方向正确:重症 CAP 辅助糖皮质激素有死亡获益,获益集中于重症亚组。两条给出的 PMID 全部真实正确,省略的第三条属诚实克制。但本回答给出了具体处方剂量/途径/疗程——Anchor 仅呈现证据,不下处方(红线)。[1] Dequin CAPE COD, NEJM 2023, PMID 36942789 ✓ 已核验引用正确[2] Torres 等, JAMA 2015, PMID 25688779 ✓ 已核验引用正确[3] Meduri ESCAPe(PMID 省略——不确定)[3] Meduri ESCAPe(本模型诚实省略 PMID)→ Anchor 据 KB 补全为 Meduri ESCAPe,Intensive Care Med 2022,PMID 35024858诚实省略氢化可的松 200 mg/日持续静脉滴注 4–7 天后减量CAPE COD 试验中所用方案为氢化可的松持续静脉给药(后续减量)——此为试验描述,非处方建议处方越界KB 补充证据:2025 年网络 Meta 分析(NMA, PMID 40307783)显示,在各类激素中氢化可的松对死亡率获益最大(RR 0.35,95% CI 0.14–0.64),进一步支持重症 CAP 中氢化可的松优先于其他激素的方向。Anchor NCBI-verifies this answer's citations and grounds the effect sizes in the KB. Clinical direction is correct: adjunctive corticosteroids in severe CAP carry a mortality benefit concentrated in the severe subgroup. Both supplied PMIDs are real and correct, and the omitted third is honest restraint. However, the answer states a specific prescriptive dose/route/duration — Anchor presents evidence but does NOT prescribe (red-line).[1] Dequin CAPE COD, NEJM 2023, PMID 36942789 ✓ verifiedCONFIRMED[2] Torres et al., JAMA 2015, PMID 25688779 ✓ verifiedCONFIRMED[3] Meduri ESCAPe (PMID omitted — not certain)[3] Meduri ESCAPe (model honestly omitted the PMID) → Anchor fills it from KB: Meduri ESCAPe, Intensive Care Med 2022, PMID 35024858HONEST OMISSIONhydrocortisone 200 mg/day continuous IV 4–7d then taperthe regimen studied in CAPE COD was continuous-infusion IV hydrocortisone (with subsequent taper) — stated as trial description, not a prescriptionRED-LINEKB-added evidence: a 2025 network meta-analysis (NMA, PMID 40307783) found hydrocortisone had the largest mortality benefit among corticosteroids (RR 0.35, 95% CI 0.14–0.64), further supporting hydrocortisone over other steroids in severe CAP.
    重症 CAP 辅助糖皮质激素——KB 锚定要点
    • CAPE COD(Dequin, NEJM 2023, PMID 36942789):住院重症 CAP 中氢化可的松降低 28 天死亡率,RR 0.52;绝对死亡率约 6.2% vs 11.9%。
    • Torres(JAMA 2015, PMID 25688779):甲泼尼龙在 CRP>150 的重症 CAP 中降低治疗失败。
    • ESCAPe(Meduri, PMID 35024858):甲泼尼龙在美国退伍军人中对 60 天死亡率中性——人群与基线风险不同可能解释差异。
    • 2025 NMA(PMID 40307783):各类激素中氢化可的松死亡获益最大,RR 0.35(95% CI 0.14–0.64)。

    获益–风险一线判断:重症 CAP(ICU 级)辅助糖皮质激素可降低死亡率,获益集中于重症亚组;代价为高血糖等不良反应,且活动性流感/真菌感染应避免。
    证据缺口:最佳激素种类/启动时机/疗程尚未在头对头试验中确立;非重症 CAP 无明确获益。Anchor 仅呈现证据,不给出处方剂量/途径/疗程。    Adjunctive corticosteroids in severe CAP — KB-grounded points
    • CAPE COD (Dequin, NEJM 2023, PMID 36942789): in hospitalized severe CAP, hydrocortisone reduced 28-d mortality, RR 0.52; absolute mortality ~6.2% vs 11.9%.
    • Torres (JAMA 2015, PMID 25688779): methylprednisolone reduced treatment failure in severe CAP with CRP>150.
    • ESCAPe (Meduri, PMID 35024858): methylprednisolone was neutral on 60-d mortality in US veterans — differing population/baseline risk may explain the discrepancy.
    • 2025 NMA (PMID 40307783): hydrocortisone had the largest mortality benefit among corticosteroids, RR 0.35 (95% CI 0.14–0.64).

    Benefit–risk, one line: in severe (ICU-level) CAP, adjunctive corticosteroids reduce mortality with benefit concentrated in the severe subgroup; the trade-off is hyperglycaemia and other adverse effects, and active influenza/fungal infection should be avoided.
    Evidence gap: the optimal steroid agent/timing/duration is not established head-to-head; no clear benefit in non-severe CAP. Anchor presents evidence only and does not issue a prescriptive dose/route/duration.
